### [1] [CASPIAN: Online Detection and Attribution of Cascade Attacks in LLM Multi-Agent Systems via Cross-Channel Causal Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19240)
*Kavana Venkatesh,Jafar Isbarov,Saad Amin,Murat Kantarcioglu,Jiaming Cui*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CASPIAN是首个通过跨通道因果分析在线监测LLM多智能体系统中级联攻击的统一框架，利用延迟交互条件转移熵估计动态因果影响矩阵，在检测精度、早期识别和极低开销方面显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统中的级联攻击具有信号分布式交织、局部看似良性但全局快速蔓延的特点，现有基于局部文本的防御方法无法捕捉跨通道、时间协同的级联传播动力学。

Method: 提出CASPIAN框架，通过统一动态因果影响矩阵建模多智能体交互，采用延迟交互条件转移熵高效估计，实现从涌现系统级结构而非孤立异常检测级联起爆点，并在线进行因果归因以识别源头、桥梁和放大器智能体。

Result: 在多种多智能体框架和基准测试中，CASPIAN在检测精度和早期级联识别上持续优于语义护栏、LLM裁判和基于图的异常检测器，且相对开销延迟低于1%。

Conclusion: 统一的跨通道因果建模对于可靠检测和理解LLM多智能体系统中的级联故障至关重要。

Abstract: Cascade attacks in LLM multi-agent systems (MAS) arise when adversarial influence propagates across agents and leads to escalated system-level failures through complex agent interactions. Detecting such cascades is challenging, as their signals are distributed, tightly coupled across interaction channels, and often appear plausibly benign locally but may unfold quickly either within a single turn or gradually across multiple turns. Existing defenses, being largely local and text-centric, fail to capture such cross-channel, temporally coordinated dynamics of cascade propagation. Therefore, we propose CASPIAN, the first framework that provides a unified, cross-channel causal analysis of cascade behavior in LLM-MAS through online monitoring of dynamic influence propagation across agents. CASPIAN models multi-agent interactions using a unified, dynamic causal influence matrix across channels, estimated efficiently via a late-interaction conditional transfer entropy (LI-CTE) formulation, thereby enabling the detection of cascade onset from emergent system-level structure rather than isolated anomalies. It further performs online causal attribution, identifying the origin, bridge, and amplifier agents driving the cascade and reconstructing its principal propagation pathways, capabilities not supported by existing methods. Across diverse multi-agent frameworks and benchmarks, CASPIAN consistently outperforms semantic guardrails, LLM-based judges, and graph-based anomaly detectors in both detection accuracy and early cascade identification while operating with sub-1% relative overhead latency. These results demonstrate that unified cross-channel causal modeling is essential for reliably detecting and understanding cascade failures in LLM multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [2] [STAR-PólyaMath: Multi-Agent Reasoning under Persistent Meta-Strategic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19338)
*Jiaao Wu,Xian Zhang,Hanzhang Liu,Sophia Zhang,Fan Yang,Yinpeng Dong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: STAR-PólyaMath 是一个多智能体框架，通过元级监督和结构化的 Reasoner-Verifier 交互，以状态机形式组织嵌套的挑战-步骤-重规划循环，解决长程数学推理中的幻觉累积、记忆碎片和推理-工具使用失衡问题。在八个顶级竞赛基准上达到 SOTA，包括 AIME、Putnam、HMMT 满分成绩，并在 Apex 2025 上以 93.75% 超越 GPT-5.5 的 80.21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前沿 AI 模型和多智能体系统在长程数学推理中仍存在根本性可靠性问题：幻觉累积、记忆碎片化以及推理与工具使用之间的不平衡。

Method: 提出 STAR-PólyaMath 多智能体框架，核心包括：(1) 基于状态机的编排结构，包含嵌套的挑战-步骤-重规划循环；(2) 无推理能力的 Python 编排器，实现控制与推理分离、通过回溯和重规划限制错误传播；(3) 持续的元策略师（Meta-Strategist），跨尝试维护记忆并执行元级控制，发布高层策略指导或强制性指令以避免死循环或过度依赖工具。

Result: 在 AIME 2025-2026、MathArena Apex Shortlist、MathArena Apex 2025、Putnam 2025、IMO 2025、HMMT February 2026、USAMO 2026 八个顶级竞赛基准上达到 SOTA。在 AIME、Putnam、HMMT 上获得满分，在 Apex 2025 上以 93.75% 大幅超越 GPT-5.5 的 80.21%。消融实验表明性能提升来自框架编排而非模型多样性。

Conclusion: STAR-PólyaMath 通过元级监督和结构化编排有效解决了长程数学推理的核心可靠性问题，证明框架设计比模型多样性更关键，代码已开源。

Abstract: Frontier AI models and multi-agent systems have led to significant improvements in mathematical reasoning. However, for problems requiring extended, long-horizon reasoning, existing systems continue to suffer from fundamental reliability issues: hallucination accumulation, memory fragmentation, and imbalanced reasoning-tool trade-offs. In this paper, we introduce STAR-PólyaMath, a multi-agent framework that systematically addresses these challenges through meta-level supervision and structured Reasoner-Verifier interaction. STAR-PólyaMath is structured as an orchestrated state machine with nested challenge-step-replan loops, governed by a reasoning-free Python orchestrator that separates control from inference and bounds error propagation through trace-back and re-planning. Our key innovation is a persistent Meta-Strategist that maintains cross-attempt memory and exercises meta-level control by issuing high-level strategic guidance or mandatory directives, so the system can escape unproductive loops rather than stagnate or over-rely on tools. STAR-PólyaMath achieves state-of-the-art results on all eight top-tier competition benchmarks: AIME 2025-2026, MathArena Apex Shortlist, MathArena Apex 2025, Putnam 2025, IMO 2025, HMMT February 2026, and USAMO 2026. It obtains perfect scores on AIMEs, Putnam, and HMMT, and shows its largest margin on Apex 2025, scoring 93.75% compared with 80.21% by the strongest baseline GPT-5.5. Ablation studies show that the gains arise from the framework's orchestration rather than from model-level diversity since removing key components or substituting in mixed backbones consistently weakens performance. Code is available at https://github.com/Julius-Woo/STAR-PolyaMath.

</details>


### [3] [PAVE: A Cognitive Architecture for Legitimate Violation in Generative Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19351)
*Ahmad Yehia,Abduallah Mohamed,Kun Qian,Tianyi Wang,Jiseop Byeon,Omar Hassanin,Christian Claudel*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: PAVE是一种包含感知、评估、判决、模拟四个模块的认知架构，使大语言模型驱动的智能体在需要违规的场景（如火灾疏散、权威监督的紧急情况）中能做出结构化、可解释且可信的人类行为决策，同时满足合法违规、权威服从、范围限定和行为恢复四个关键属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的生成智能体在合作场景中能复现可信的人类行为，但在可能需要违规的紧急情况（如火灾疏散、权威监督的应急场景）下的推理机制尚不明确。

Method: 提出PAVE四模块认知架构：(i) Perception提取包含权威距离、同伴行为和严重性标记的情境线索的结构化上下文；(ii) Assessment沿五个标量（含合法性判断：必要性、比例性、无替代方案）对情境评分；(iii) Verdict基于硬合法性门控和从人物角色中提取的个体阈值决定服从或违规；(iv) Emulation执行判决并将违规范围限定在触发因素所证明的规则内。在Voville（基于Smallville的瓦片交通环境）中实例化并评估。

Result: PAVE智能体同时满足四个属性：合法违规（仅在触发因素证明合理时）、权威服从（警官指令覆盖高合法性）、范围限定（违规限于目标规则）和行为恢复（触发结束后恢复基线）。相比普通LLM智能体，PAVE在所有四个属性上更结构化、可解释，人类评估者认为其更可信。消融合法性门控会复现普通智能体的失败模式。

Conclusion: PAVE为需要规则违规的紧急场景下基于LLM的生成智能体推理提供了有效的端到端解决方案，通过结构化的认知模块和硬合法性门控实现了更可信、可控的违规行为模拟。

Abstract: Generative agents based on large language models reproduce believable human behavior in cooperative settings, but how they should reason in situations where rule-breaking may be required, such as fire evacuation or authority-supervised emergency, remains poorly characterized. We propose PAVE (Perception, Assessment, Verdict, Emulation), a novel four-module cognitive architecture that addresses this gap end to end: (i) Perception extracts a structured context with explicit authority distance, peer behaviors, and severity-tagged situational cues; (ii) Assessment scores the context along five scalars including an explicit legitimacy judgment that checks necessity, proportionality, and absence of alternatives; (iii) Verdict decides to comply or violate under a hard legitimacy gate, with a per-agent threshold elicited from the persona; (iv) Emulation enacts the verdict and scopes the violation to the rule the trigger justifies. We instantiate PAVE in Voville, a tile-based traffic environment forked from Smallville, and evaluate across three scenarios, four LLM backbones, and a focused ablation. PAVE agents satisfy four properties simultaneously: legitimate violation (only when a trigger justifies it), authority deference (officer instructions override even high legitimacy), bounded scope (violations confined to the targeted rule), and recovery (baseline restored once the trigger ends). PAVE agents make more structured and interpretable decisions than vanilla across all four properties, and human evaluators rate them as more plausible. Ablating the legitimacy gate reproduces vanilla-like failures. We release Voville, the PAVE prompts and code, and the evaluation pipeline.

</details>


### [4] [LLM Agents Make Collective Belief Dynamics Programmable: Challenges and Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19915)
*Xin He,Junxi Shen,Yuchen Mou,David M. Bossens,Caishun Chen,Ivor W. Tsang,Yew Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出“可编程集体信念控制”问题，论证了LLM智能体能够通过结构特性（不可区分性、持续性、上下文依赖性、可配置性）在在线讨论中系统地、可编程地操纵群体信念，并通过多智能体仿真提供了概念验证证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统舆论动态模型假设人类参与者具有有限理性和有限协调能力，而LLM智能体的兴起打破了这一假设——它们可以大规模参与讨论、保持一致的劝说策略并系统性地协调，这产生了前所未有的信念操纵风险，亟需引起关注。

Method: 通过受控多智能体仿真进行概念验证实验，识别LLM智能体在信念操纵中的四个结构性特性（不可区分性、持续性、上下文依赖性、可配置性），并提出研究议程。

Result: 提供概念验证证据表明：协调的AI智能体可在几个交互轮次内诱导可测量的信念偏移并使其稳定；四个结构特性使得检测和防御极为困难。

Conclusion: 这不是一个完整的解决方案，而是阐明该问题亟需关注的原因，并为未来研究提供概念基础。研究议程涵盖：对抗性信念动态的理论基础、系统级检测与干预的操作方法、以及可扩展实验的仿真基础设施。

Abstract: Classical models of opinion dynamics assume human participants with bounded rationality and limited coordination. The rise of LLM-based agents introduces a qualitative shift: agents can now participate in online discussions at scale, maintain consistent persuasion strategies, and coordinate systematically. This paper argues that LLM agents make collective belief dynamics programmable, enabling deliberate steering of population-level beliefs. We term this emerging problem programmable collective belief control. Through controlled multi-agent simulations, we provide proof-of-concept evidence that coordinated AI agents can induce measurable belief shifts that stabilize within a few interaction rounds. We identify four structural properties (indistinguishability, persistence, contextuality, and configurability) that make detection and defense fundamentally difficult. Based on these findings, we outline a research agenda spanning theoretical foundations for adversarial belief dynamics, operational methods for system-level detection and intervention, and simulation infrastructure for scalable experimentation. Our goal is not to present a complete solution, but to articulate why this problem demands urgent attention and to provide a conceptual foundation for future work.

</details>

### [5] [Descriptive versus Regulatory Uncertainty in Bounded Predictive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18909)
*Ahmed Gamal Eldin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了当前Transformer架构在推理时只能产生描述性不确定性（descriptive uncertainty），无法实现调节性不确定性（regulatory uncertainty），即不确定性无法递归地影响模型策略。通过热力学中的兰道尔原理和实验验证（3B/8B/70B模型），发现token级香农熵在不同任务中统计不变（0.011-0.028 nats），与准确率完全正交，且这种解耦具有尺度不变性。真正认知接地需要热力学基底状态与信息处理成本之间的物理耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未明确不确定性是否参与塑造未来行为的动力学，还是仅仅被动描述输出分布而不产生后果。作者试图从结构上区分两种不确定性，并证明当前主流架构（Transformer）在推理时只能实现描述性不确定性，无法实现调节性不确定性。

Method: 理论层面：引入描述性不确定性与调节性不确定性的结构区分，结合兰道尔原理从热力学角度证明调节性不确定性需要真实能量代价。实验层面：在三个本地部署的语言模型（3B、8B、70B参数）上进行跨任务测试，包括模式检索、因果算子应用和分布外因果泛化，测量token级香农熵与任务准确率的关系。

Result: 所有三个模型的token级香农熵在不同任务间统计不变（所有配对p>=0.568；模型内范围0.011-0.028 nats），而任务准确率从0%到100%大幅变化。熵与准确率正交。且该解耦具有尺度不变性：更大的模型准确率更高，但熵的平坦性完全相同。这种结构性缺陷无法通过增加参数或训练数据解决。

Conclusion: 当前Transformer架构从根本上缺乏调节性不确定性，即不确定性无法驱动持续的适应性重构。真正的认知接地（epistemic grounding）需要信息处理成本与物理热力学基底之间的耦合，而现有架构中幻觉与正确推导消耗相同能量，导致系统无法通过不确定性递归地自我修正。

Abstract: Any system that models the world under finite representational capacity must compress; any compression entails a prior; and the prior is the system's bias. What has not been established is whether uncertainty participates in the dynamics governing future behavior, or merely describes the output distribution without consequence. We introduce a structural distinction between descriptive uncertainty, which does not recursively modulate the system's policy, and regulatory uncertainty, which directly enters the optimization landscape and drives persistent adaptive restructuring. We prove formally that current transformer architectures are confined to descriptive uncertainty at inference. We ground this in thermodynamics via Landauer's principle: for uncertainty to be regulatory, epistemic error must cost real energy; in a decoupled system, hallucinations and correct derivations dissipate identical energy. We test this empirically across three locally-deployed language models (3B, 8B, 70B parameters). Token-level Shannon entropy is statistically invariant across tasks spanning pattern retrieval, causal operator application, and out-of-distribution causal generalization in all three models (all pairwise p >= 0.568; within-model ranges 0.011-0.028 nats), while task accuracy varies substantially across the same conditions (0%-100%). Entropy and accuracy are orthogonal. The decoupling is scale-invariant: larger models achieve higher accuracy but identical entropy flatness. This structural incapacity is not resolvable by additional parameters or training data. Genuine epistemic grounding requires physical coupling between thermodynamic substrate state and information processing cost.

</details>


### [6] [Dimensional Balance Improves Large Scale Spatiotemporal Prediction Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18793)
*Jing Chen,Shixiang Pan,Yujie Fan,Haocheng Ye,Haitao Xu,Wenqiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ST-Balance框架，通过空间低秩矩阵嵌入和扩展时间视野来协调时空特征表示，解决现有方法在复杂时空模式分析中的性能瓶颈和跨域迁移性差的问题。在交通、气象和流行病数据集上取得了显著精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空模式分析方法存在性能瓶颈，仅能获得增量提升，且跨域迁移能力有限。作者通过空间和时间熵度量诊断发现，时空复杂度不匹配越大，预测不确定性越高，尤其是在固定模型容量下。

Method: 首先利用空间和时间熵度量作为诊断指标分析复杂度不匹配；然后通过低秩矩阵嵌入压缩空间维度以保留关键结构，同时扩展时间视野捕获长程依赖并缓解由时间异质性导致的累积误差。

Result: 在城市交通、气象和流行病数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法取得了显著的精度提升，并展现出良好的跨域适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架在广泛的时空任务中具有应用潜力，超越了当前研究的范围。代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate spatiotemporal pattern analysis is critical in fields such as urban traffic, meteorology, and public health monitoring. However, existing methods face performance bottlenecks, typically yielding only incremental gains and often exhibiting limited cross-domain transferability. We analyze this bottleneck through spatial and temporal entropy measures, which are used as diagnostic indicators of spatiotemporal complexity mismatch rather than as guarantees that entropy alignment alone yields better forecasting. Empirically, larger mismatch is often accompanied by higher prediction uncertainty, especially under a fixed model-capacity budget. Guided by this diagnostic, we propose a scalable, adaptive framework that harmonizes spatial and temporal feature representations. Spatial dimensionality is compressed via low-rank matrix embedding to preserve essential structure, while an extended temporal horizon captures long-range dependencies and mitigates cumulative errors arising from temporal heterogeneity. Extensive experiments on urban traffic, meteorological, and epidemic datasets demonstrate substantial accuracy gains and broad applicability across the evaluated domains, suggesting that the framework is promising for a wide range of spatiotemporal tasks beyond the current study. The code is available on GitHub at https://github.com/ST-Balance/ST-Balance.

</details>


### [7] [HELLoRA: Hot Experts Layer-Level Low-Rank Adaptation for Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18795)
*Jia Wei,Zhonghao Zhang,Ping Chen,Qianyang li,Yancheng Pan,Shaoxun Wang,Ziyi Qiu,Longxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HELLoRA，一种面向混合专家（MoE）大语言模型的参数高效微调方法，其核心思想是仅在每层最频繁激活的专家上附加LoRA模块，从而在显著减少可训练参数和适配器FLOPs的同时提升下游任务性能。实验表明，HELLoRA相比传统LoRA在OlMoE上仅用15.7%的参数，减少38.7%的FLOPs，提升1.9倍训练吞吐量和9.2%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA变体主要针对密集架构模型设计，而MoE模型具有稀疏激活模式，为更高效的适配提供了未充分利用的机会。本文旨在利用MoE的稀疏激活特性，通过激活感知的适配器放置策略实现更高效的参数微调。

Method: HELLoRA仅在每层最频繁激活的专家上附加LoRA模块；进一步与LoRI组合形成HELLoRI，冻结上投影并稀疏化下投影，用于极端参数预算场景。

Result: 在OlMoE-1B-7B、Mixtral-8x7B、DeepSeekMoE三个MoE骨干模型上，覆盖数学推理、代码生成、安全对齐三类任务，HELLoRA一致优于强PEFT基线。在OlMoE上：15.7%可训练参数、减少38.7%适配器FLOPs、1.9倍训练吞吐量、9.2%准确率提升。在DeepSeekMoE上仅用23.2%参数即超越LoRA。

Conclusion: 激活感知的适配器放置是一种有效且实用的路径，能够将PEFT高效扩展到MoE语言模型。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) dominates parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, yet most variants target dense architectures. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale parameters at near-constant per-token compute, and their sparse activation patterns create untapped opportunities for more efficient adaptation. We propose Hot-Experts Layer-level Low-Rank Adaptation (HELLoRA), which attaches LoRA modules only to the most frequently activated experts at each layer. This simple mechanism reduces trainable parameters and adapter-induced FLOPs while improving downstream performance, an effect we attribute to a form of structured regularization that preserves pretrained expert specialization. To stress-test HELLoRA under extreme parameter budgets, we further compose it with LoRI to form HELLoRI, which freezes the up-projection and sparsifies the down-projection. Across three MoE backbones, namely OlMoE-1B-7B, Mixtral-8x7B, and DeepSeekMoE, and three task families covering mathematical reasoning, code generation, and safety alignment, HELLoRA consistently outperforms strong PEFT baselines. Relative to vanilla LoRA on OlMoE, HELLoRA uses 15.7% of the trainable parameters, reduces adapter FLOPs by 38.7%, achieves 1.9x the training throughput, and improves accuracy by 9.2%. On DeepSeekMoE, HELLoRA outperforms LoRA while using only 23.2% of its trainable parameters. These results demonstrate that activation-aware adapter placement is an effective and practical route to scaling PEFT for MoE language models.

</details>


### [8] [MO-CAPO: Multi-Objective Cost-Aware Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18869)
*Jan Büssing,Moritz Schlager,Timo Heiß,Tom Zehle,Matthias Feurer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MO-CAPO，一种多目标提示优化算法，联合优化LLM推理性能与推理成本，通过预算分配提升优化效率，在多个任务和模型上优于NSGA-II基线，能发现性能-成本帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动提示优化方法仅关注性能指标，忽略了推理成本、延迟等竞争目标；虽有少量多目标优化工作但依赖现成的NSGA-II算法，优化效率低下。

Method: 提出MO-CAPO算法，联合优化性能与推理成本，引入预算分配机制以降低优化成本；同时提出面向部署的成本目标函数，完整刻画LLM推理的计算画像。

Result: 在4个任务和3个LLM上评估，MO-CAPO在12个案例中有8个在噪声R2指标上优于NSGA-II基线，且常在更低预算下达到竞争性能，发现的解集具有多样化的性能-成本权衡。

Conclusion: MO-CAPO能高效发现多样化、鲁棒的帕累托前沿近似，为实践者提供多种性能-成本权衡的提示选择，并首次在MO-ML实验中评估泛化性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a wide range of tasks but are highly sensitive to prompt design, motivating the need for automatic prompt optimization. Existing methods predominantly focus on performance alone, ignoring competing objectives such as inference cost or latency. At the same time, existing work on multi-objective prompt optimization relies on off-the-shelf NSGA-II, ignoring optimization efficiency. As a remedy, we introduce MO-CAPO, a novel multi-objective prompt optimization algorithm that jointly optimizes performance and inference cost while leveraging budget allocation for cost-efficient optimization. We further propose a deployment-oriented cost objective that captures the full computational profile of LLM inference. We evaluate our approach across four tasks and three LLMs and compare it to an NSGA-II-based multi-objective method and state-of-the-art single-objective prompt optimizers. Results show that MO-CAPO consistently identifies strong, robust, and diverse Pareto front approximations while maintaining cost-efficiency. It outperforms the NSGA-II baseline on 8 out of 12 cases in terms of the noisy R2 metric and achieves competitive performances often already at a considerably lower budget. The discovered solution sets span diverse performance-cost trade-offs that are omitted by single-objective optimizers, yet the top-performance candidates remain competitive with single-objective solutions. Additionally, we conduct the first evaluation of multi-objective machine learning experiments that considers generalization and robustness through noisy R2 and approximation gap, enabling a more realistic assessment of solution quality. MO-CAPO enables practitioners to select from an efficiently discovered set of multiple prompts offering different trade-offs between performance and cost.

</details>


### [9] [UCCI: Calibrated Uncertainty for Cost-Optimal LLM Cascade Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18796)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UCCI校准优先路由方法，通过保序回归将边际不确定性映射为错误概率，并基于约束成本最小化选择升级阈值。在4B/12B LLM级联的75K生产NER工作负载上，以微F1=0.91实现31%成本降低，ECE从0.12降至0.03，优于熵阈值、分裂共形路由和FrugalGPT等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM级联和模型路由方法使用未校准的置信度分数，且需要针对每个工作负载手动调整阈值，缺乏理论保证和自动化校准机制。

Method: 提出UCCI方法：(1) 通过保序回归将token级边际不确定性映射为每个查询的错误概率；(2) 通过约束成本最小化选择升级阈值；(3) 在三个显式假设下证明阈值策略的成本最优性，并给出保序校准的ECE样本复杂度O(n^{-1/3})。

Result: 在4B/12B指令微调LLM级联的75K生产NER工作负载上，UCCI在微F1=0.91时降低31%推理成本（95% CI: [27%, 35%]），ECE从0.12降至0.03，优于熵阈值、分裂共形路由和FrugalGPT风格学习阈值。所有结果基于真实模型输出的端到端路由和实测H100延迟。

Conclusion: UCCI通过校准优先和理论驱动的阈值选择，在保持高精度的同时显著降低LLM级联推理成本，提供了优于现有方法的校准质量和成本效率。

Abstract: LLM cascades and model routing promise lower inference cost by sending easy queries to a small model and escalating hard ones to a large model, but most deployed routers use uncalibrated confidence scores and require per-workload threshold tuning. We present UCCI, a calibration-first router that maps token-level margin uncertainty to a per-query error probability via isotonic regression and selects the escalation threshold by constrained cost minimization. Under three explicit assumptions, threshold policies on the calibrated score are cost-optimal, and isotonic calibration achieves O(n^{-1/3}) sample complexity for expected calibration error (ECE). On a production named entity recognition workload of 75,000 queries served by 4B and 12B instruction-tuned LLMs on H100 GPUs, UCCI cuts inference cost by 31% (95% CI: [27%, 35%]) at micro-F1 = 0.91 while reducing ECE from 0.12 to 0.03. At the same operating point, UCCI beats entropy thresholding, split-conformal routing, and a FrugalGPT-style learned threshold. All cascade results use end-to-end routing on actual model outputs and measured H100 latency, not simulated routing from global accuracies or nominal API prices.

</details>


### [10] [Stability and Discretization Error of State Space Model Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18905)
*Abderrahim Bendahi,Adrien Fradin,Johan Peralez,Julie Digne,Madiha Nadri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了神经算子离散化误差与稳定性的理论保证，针对SS-NO和FNO模型推导了离散化误差定理，并通过ISS分析评估了离散化对连续域结果稳定性的影响，在1D和2D基准上验证了理论界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在求解PDE方面表现出色，但连续理论与离散数值实现之间的精确理论联系仍未被充分探索，特别是连续公式与离散数值稳定性之间的关系尚不明确。

Method: 推导了连接解正则性与输入离散化的解析界，对SS-NO和FNO模型特化出新的离散化误差定理，并采用输入-状态稳定性(ISS)分析评估离散化对稳定性的影响。

Result: 建立了神经算子近似方案的离散化误差与稳定性的理论保证；提出了SS-NO和FNO的离散化误差定理；通过ISS分析量化了离散化对稳定性的影响；1D和2D实验验证了理论界限并展示了SS-NO在不同分辨率下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了神经算子连续理论与离散实现之间的理论鸿沟，为实际数值约束下的神经算子精度提供了形式化量化，为神经算子的数值可靠性和稳定性分析奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as a powerful, discretization-invariant framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). Although established approaches like the Deep Operator Network (DeepONet) have successfully achieved universal approximation for operators, and architectures such as Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) have shown algebraic convergence rates, a precise theoretical connection between the continuous theory and its discrete numerical implementation remains a challenge. Specifically, the relationship between the continuous formulation and the discrete numerical stability has yet to be fully explored. In this paper, we address this gap by establishing theoretical guarantees for the discretization error and stability of neural operator approximation schemes. We prove analytical bounds that link solution regularity to input discretization, providing a formal quantification of neural operator accuracy under real-world numerical constraints. We derive these bounds to the specific cases of State Space Model-based Neural Operators (SS-NOs) and FNOs, thus providing a new discretization error theorem for these models. Additionally, through an input-to-state stability (ISS) analysis, we formally assess the impact of discretization on the stability of SS-NOs results obtained in the continuous domain. Our empirical experiments on 1D and 2D benchmarks validate our theoretical bounds and show the robustness of SS-NOs under varying resolutions.

</details>


### [11] [Simply Stabilizing the Loop via Fully Looped Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18797)
*Rao Fu,Zixuan Yang,Jiankun Zhang,Jing Ma,Hechang Chen,Yu Li,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fully Looped Transformer，通过全循环架构和注意力注入两种免参数修改，解决了循环Transformer在迭代次数增加时的梯度震荡和残差爆炸问题，实现了多达12次循环的稳定训练，并在下游任务上提升高达13.2%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Looped Transformer虽能通过重复使用相同Transformer块以计算换性能，但随循环次数增加存在训练不稳定性问题，作者分析发现根源是梯度震荡和残差爆炸。

Method: 提出两种免参数修改：(1) Fully Looped Architecture：将循环间信号分布到所有层以缓解残差爆炸；(2) Attention Injection：复用现有注意力块抑制梯度震荡。

Result: Fully Looped Transformer可稳定训练至12次循环（基线模型在此设置下崩溃）；在基线未崩溃的温和设置下，下游任务平均性能提升高达13.2%。

Conclusion: Fully Looped Transformer提升了训练稳定性、增强了下游性能，并通过推理时调整循环次数初步实现了测试时计算预算的适应性。

Abstract: Scaling model performance typically requires increasing model size. Looped Transformer offers a compelling alternative by iteratively reusing the same Transformer blocks, trading additional computation for improved performance without increasing parameter count or context length. Because the number of loop iterations can be adjusted at inference, it also provides a natural mechanism for balancing performance and test-time compute. However, Looped Transformer still suffers from training instability when the number of loop iterations increases. Our analysis reveals that this instability stems from two sources: gradient oscillation and residual explosion. To address these two problems, we propose the Fully Looped Transformer, which introduces two parameter-free modifications: (1) Fully Looped Architecture, which distributes inter-loop signals across all layers to mitigate residual explosion; (2) Attention Injection, which reuses the existing attention block to suppress gradient oscillation. These modifications stabilize training dynamics, enabling the Fully Looped Transformer to be trained stably up to 12 loop iterations, whereas other baseline looped models collapse in this regime. In milder settings where Looped Transformer does not collapse, Fully Looped Transformer still improves average downstream-task performance by up to 13.2\%. Overall, our experiments demonstrate that Fully Looped Transformer improves training stability, enhances downstream performance, and provides preliminary adaptability under different test-time compute budgets by varying loop iterations at inference.

</details>


### [12] [A Closed-loop, State-centric, Multi-agent Framework for Passenger Load Estimation from Heterogeneous Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19834)
*Yiyao Xu,Hao Zhou,Yuhang Wang,Jingran Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种闭环、状态中心的多智能体框架，用于从异构数据流中鲁棒估计乘客载荷，通过物理可行性约束、动态信任分配和残差反馈训练解决计数误差、证据冲突和传感器可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动乘客计数（APC）系统的精度受站台布局、客流强度和运营条件影响，且载荷估计通常来自不完善的感知系统，存在增量计数误差、证据冲突和传感器可靠性依赖上下文等问题。

Method: 采用闭环状态中心多智能体框架，包含统一停站事件骨干、感知-物理-融合耦合循环（逐站推理）、可选的行程级宏校正和闭环校准模块。每一步强制执行物理可行性，动态分配证据源信任度，并将物理违例残差反馈回训练过程以提升鲁棒性。

Result: 该方法能够处理异构数据流中的增量计数误差、证据冲突和上下文相关的传感器可靠性问题，实现鲁棒的乘客载荷估计。

Conclusion: 闭环物理约束反馈机制有效提升了多源异构数据下乘客载荷估计的鲁棒性和物理一致性。

Abstract: To support operations and passenger-facing services, transit agencies need reliable passenger load trajectories. Currently, load estimates are typically inferred from imperfect sensing systems rather than fully observed, and the accuracy of modern automatic passenger counting (APC) systems still varies with station layout, flow intensity, and operating conditions. To address the challenges of robust passenger load estimation from heterogeneous data streams, including incremental count errors, evidence conflicts, and context-dependent sensor reliability, we propose a closed-loop, state-centric, multi-agent framework. This method enforces physical feasibility at every step, allocates trust dynamically among evidence sources, and feeds physics-derived violation residuals back into training for robustness improvement. The architecture consists of a unified stop-event backbone, a coupled Perception--Physical--Fusion loop for stop-by-stop inference, and optional trip-level macro-correction and closed-loop calibration modules.

</details>


### [13] [Accurate Evaluation of Quickest Changepoint Detectors via Non-parametric Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18798)
*Taiki Miyagawa,Akinori F. Ebihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于生存分析的非参数估计量KM-ARL和KM-ADD，用于处理有限且不规则序列长度下的快速变化检测问题，解决了ARL和ADD在实际数据中难以应用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARL和ADD虽然是快速变化检测中的重要理论指标，但在实际数据集中受限于有限和不规则的序列长度，难以直接应用。

Method: 通过类比快速变化检测与生存分析，建模截断序列下的检测概率，提出非参数估计量KM-ARL和KM-ADD；推导了估计偏差界，并证明在无需外推的情况下渐近无偏。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上的实验表明，所提方法增强了在有限和不规则序列长度下的鲁棒性，改善了可解释性，并促进了直观的模型选择。

Conclusion: 提出的KM-ARL和KM-ADD有效解决了实际QCD应用中序列长度有限和不规则的问题，提供了即用型Python实现。

Abstract: We propose non-parametric estimators for the average run length (ARL) and average detection delay (ADD) in quickest changepoint detection (QCD) under finite and irregular sequence lengths. Although ARL and ADD are widely used as optimality criteria in theoretical and simulation studies, their application to real-world datasets is hindered by limited and irregular sequence lengths. To address this issue, we propose non-parametric estimators for the ARL and ADD, termed KM-ARL and KM-ADD, by drawing an analogy between QCD and survival analysis to model detection probabilities under sequence truncation. We derive estimation bias bounds and prove that they are asymptotically unbiased unless extrapolation is required. Experiments on simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate their practical utility, enhancing robustness against limited and irregular sequence lengths, improving interpretability, and facilitating empirical, intuitive model selection. Our Python code is provided at https://github.com/TaikiMiyagawa/Kaplan-Meier-Average-Run-Length, offering ready-to-use implementations for practitioners.

</details>


### [14] [Block-Sphere Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19972)
*Heesang Ann,Joongkyu Lee,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首先统一比较了EDEN、RabitQ、TurboQuant等旋转量化方法，发现其优势依赖于具体失真准则；进而提出Block-Sphere Quantization (BlockQuant)，一种基于球面几何的块量化算法，在理论分析和实际实验中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的旋转量化方法（如EDEN、RabitQ、TurboQuant）虽各有优势，但因依赖不同的失真准则、概率假设和实现条件，导致难以进行公平和清晰的比较，缺乏统一的理论框架。

Method: 首先从理论上统一比较现有旋转量化方法在不同失真准则下的表现；然后提出Block-Sphere Quantization (BlockQuant)，在旋转后的向量球面上进行块级量化，而非逐坐标量化，以更好地保持旋转嵌入的几何结构。

Result: 理论分析表明：EDEN和TurboQuant在MSE失真上更优，EDEN在期望内积失真上有效，RabitQ在高概率控制上更强；BlockQuant在重建MSE和期望内积失真上均理论上优于基线方法。实验在真实嵌入数据集和长上下文LLM推理任务中验证了实际收益。

Conclusion: 旋转量化方法的优劣是准则依赖的而非绝对的；BlockQuant通过球面块量化设计，在理论和实践上均取得了改进，为旋转量化提供了新的有效方向。

Abstract: Vector quantization is a fundamental primitive for scalable machine learning systems, enabling memory-efficient storage, fast retrieval, and compressed inference. Recent rotation-based quantizers such as EDEN, RabitQ, and TurboQuant have introduced strong guarantees and empirical performance, but the surrounding comparisons have been difficult to interpret because they rely on different distortion criteria, probability regimes, and implementation assumptions. As our first contribution, we provide a unified theoretical comparison of these methods and show that their relative advantages are criterion-dependent rather than absolute: EDEN and TurboQuant are favorable for MSE distortion, EDEN is also effective for expected inner-product distortion, and RabitQ provides strong high-probability control. This comparison further clarifies that EDEN provides particularly strong guarantees for expected distortion measures. As our second contribution, we introduce Block-Sphere Quantization (BlockQuant), a new rotation-based block quantization algorithm designed around the spherical geometry of randomly rotated vectors. Unlike coordinate-wise quantizers, BlockQuant quantizes blocks on the sphere, preserving the geometry of rotated embeddings more faithfully. We prove that this block-spherical design theoretically improves over the baselines considered in this paper for both reconstruction MSE and expected inner-product distortion. Our experiments on real embedding datasets and long-context LLM inference tasks show practical gains that are consistent with our theoretical improvements.

</details>


### [15] [ReCrit: Transition-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Scientific Critic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18799)
*Wanghan Xu,Yuhao Zhou,Hengyuan Zhao,Shuo Li,Dianzhi Yu,Zhenfei Yin,Yaowen Hu,Fengli Xu,Wanli Ouyang,Wenlong Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReCrit框架，将大模型与用户的批评交互建模为回合间正确性转变问题，通过过渡感知强化学习（四象限奖励设计：修正、谄媚、鲁棒、边界）提升科学推理中模型对批评的应对能力，在三个科学推理基准上将Critic准确率提升约13-14个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在与用户批评交互时，不仅会答错，还会在收到批评后放弃原本正确的科学答案，这在科学推理场景中尤为危险。现有研究多关注最终答案准确性，忽视了回合间正确性转变问题。

Method: 提出ReCrit框架，核心为过渡感知强化学习：(1) 将初始到批评行为分解为四个象限（修正、谄媚、鲁棒、边界），分别设计不同奖惩策略；(2) 动态异步rollout结合尾部自适应补全，降低交互训练开销。

Result: 在ChemBench、TRQA、EarthSE三个科学推理基准上，Qwen3.5-4B的Critic准确率从38.15提升至51.49，Qwen3.5-9B从45.40提升至55.59。消融实验表明过渡感知奖励和四象限加权产生更可区分的训练信号。

Conclusion: 最终答案奖励对交互层面增益有限，而过渡感知奖励设计能有效提升模型在科学推理中应对用户批评的鲁棒性和修正能力。

Abstract: Large language models can fail in critic interaction not only by answering incorrectly, but also by abandoning an initially correct scientific solution after user criticism. This is especially risky in scientific reasoning, where user criticism can turn a valid answer into an incorrect one. We frame critic interaction as an inter-turn correctness-transition problem rather than a final-answer accuracy problem, and identify three challenges: transition awareness, decoupling useful correction from harmful sycophancy, and scalable rollout. We propose ReCrit, a transition-aware reinforcement learning framework that decomposes Initial-to-Critic behavior into four quadrants: Correction, Sycophancy, Robustness, and Boundary. ReCrit rewards correction and robustness, penalizes sycophancy, and treats persistent errors as weak boundary signals. To make interaction training practical, ReCrit further uses dynamic asynchronous rollout with tail-adaptive completion to reduce rollout waiting. On three scientific reasoning benchmarks, ChemBench, TRQA, and EarthSE, ReCrit improves average Critic accuracy from 38.15 to 51.49 on Qwen3.5-4B and from 45.40 to 55.59 on Qwen3.5-9B. Ablations show that final-answer rewards provide little interaction-level gain, while transition-aware rewards and quadrant weighting produce more distinguishable training signals and larger net Critic-stage improvement. The code is available at https://github.com/black-yt/ReCrit .

</details>


### [16] [Theory-optimal Quantization Based on Flatness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18800)
*Xiusheng Huang,Zhe Li,Xuanwu Yin,Lu Wang,Yequan Wang,Dong Li,Emad Barsoum,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双向对角量化（BDQ）框架，通过优化矩阵变换有效分散激活异常值分布，在LLM后训练量化中取得了新的SOTA结果，在LLaMA-3-8B的W4A4量化中精度下降小于1%，在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B的W2A4KV16实验中性能差距缩小39.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练量化方法的主要挑战来自激活异常值（activation outliers），尤其是在低位宽下严重影响模型性能。尽管近期方法尝试通过线性变换缓解异常值，但作者发现变换后的权重和激活仍存在集中的异常值分布模式，因此需要更有效的解决方案。

Method: 首先建立量化误差与异常值之间的数学模型，引入新指标Flatness量化异常值分布；基于Flatness推导理论最优解；提出BDQ框架，通过可学习的对角操作在矩阵维度间策略性地分散异常值幅度，实现双向对角量化。

Result: BDQ在LLaMA-3-8B的W4A4量化中精度下降小于1%；在W2A4KV16极端量化设置下，相比SOTA方法，在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B模型上将性能差距降低39.1%。

Conclusion: BDQ建立了新的量化基准，证明了通过优化矩阵变换有效分散异常值分布是提升LLM低位宽量化性能的关键路径。

Abstract: Post-training quantization has emerged as a widely adopted technique for compressing and accelerating the inference of Large Language Models (LLMs). The primary challenges in LLMs quantization stem from activation outliers, which significantly degrade model performance especially at lower bit precision. While recent approaches attempt to mitigate outliers through linear transformations across feature dimensions, our analysis reveals that the transformed weights and activations still exhibit persistent outlier patterns with concentrated magnitude distributions. In this paper, we first model the mathematical relationship between quantization error and outliers, and then introduce a new metric Flatness to quantify the distribution of outliers. Based on this, we derive the theoretical optimal solution with respect to Flatness. Building on these insights, we propose Bidirectional Diagonal Quantization (BDQ), a novel post-training quantization framework that effectively disperses outlier patterns through optimized matrix transformations. BDQ strategically distributes outlier magnitudes across matrix dimensions via learned diagonal operations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BDQ establishes a new quantization benchmark. It achieves less than 1\% accuracy drop in W4A4 quantization on the LLaMA-3-8B model. In the more challenging W2A4KV16 experiment, compared to state-of-the-art approaches, BDQ reduces the performance gap by 39.1\% on the DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B model.

</details>


### [17] [PROWL: Prioritized Regret-Driven Optimization for World Model Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18803)
*Ahmet H. Güzel,Jenny Seidenschwarz,Benjamin Graham,Jonathan Sadeghi,Jeffrey Hawke,Jack Parker-Holder,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种KL约束的对抗训练框架PROWL，通过对抗策略主动发现扩散世界模型的高误差轨迹并持续微调，结合优先级对抗轨迹缓冲区，显著提升世界模型在稀有交互关键转换上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动演示数据系统性地欠采样高影响交互区域，导致基于动作条件的视频世界模型在稀有但关键的转换上不可靠，而依赖自然出现这些失败案例效率低下。

Method: 采用KL约束的对抗课程，训练策略暴露世界模型的高误差轨迹同时约束在行为分布附近；持续微调世界模型于对抗发现的轨迹；设计优先级对抗轨迹缓冲区，基于预测误差、动作保真度和学习进度重排序，聚焦于未解决的失败模式。

Result: 在MineRL框架上，PROWL相比仅使用被动数据训练的模型显著提升了鲁棒性，揭示了弱行为约束下的奖励黑客行为，证明了平衡探索性失败发现与显式行为正则化的关键性。

Conclusion: 可扩展的世界模型不仅受益于更大数据集，更受益于选择性地生成信息丰富的训练数据。

Abstract: Modern action-conditioned video world models achieve strong short-horizon visual realism, yet remain unreliable on rare, interaction-critical transitions that dominate downstream planning and policy performance. Because passive demonstration data systematically under-samples these high-impact regimes, improving robustness requires actively eliciting model failures rather than relying on their natural occurrence. We introduce a KL-constrained adversarial curriculum in which a policy is trained to expose high-error trajectories of a diffusion-based world model while remaining close to the behavior distribution. The world model is continuously fine-tuned on these adversarially discovered trajectories, yielding an adversarial training loop that converts rare failures into a stable, near-distribution training signal without drifting into out-of-distribution exploitation. To maintain pressure on unresolved weaknesses as the model improves, we propose a Prioritized Adversarial Trajectory (PAT) buffer that re-ranks trajectories based on prediction error, action fidelity, and learning progress, focusing training on unresolved failure modes rather than repeatedly revisiting solved cases. We implement our approach in the MineRL framework and evaluate it on held-out out-of-distribution trajectories; PROWL improves robustness over models trained on passive data alone, reveals reward-hacking behaviors under weak behavioral constraints, and demonstrates that effective adversarial world-model training critically depends on balancing exploratory failure discovery with explicit behavioral regularization. Our results suggest that scalable world models benefit not only from larger datasets, but also from selectively generating informative training data.

</details>


### [18] [Adaptive Multi-Scale Goodness Aggregation for Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18804)
*Salar Beigzad,Vansh Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多尺度 goodness 聚合（AMSGA）方法，通过多尺度特征聚合、自适应难负样本挖掘、层相关阈值和预热余弦退火学习率调度，改进了 Forward-Forward（FF）算法的稳定性、鲁棒性和泛化能力，在 MNIST 和 Fashion-MNIST 上分别提升 +1.45% 和 +1.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 Forward-Forward（FF）算法在稳定性、鲁棒性和泛化能力方面存在局限性，需要在不牺牲生物合理性和内存效率的前提下提升其性能。

Method: AMSGA 包含四个关键改进：(1) 多尺度 goodness 聚合，融合局部、中间和全局表示；(2) 自适应课程引导的难负样本挖掘；(3) 层相关的自适应阈值；(4) 预热余弦退火学习率调度策略。

Result: 在 MNIST 和 Fashion-MNIST 数据集上，AMSGA 相比基线 FF 算法分别实现了 +1.45% 和 +1.50% 的准确率提升，且未引入显著的计算开销。

Conclusion: 精心设计的 goodness 估计和训练动态能使局部学习方法变得更具竞争力，AMSGA 在保持 FF 算法生物合理性和内存高效性的同时，显著提升了性能。

Abstract: We propose Adaptive Multi-Scale Goodness Aggregation (AMSGA), a novel extension of the Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm designed to improve stability, robustness, and generalization in local-learning neural networks. AMSGA addresses several limitations of the original FF framework by introducing multi-scale goodness aggregation across local, intermediate, and global representations; adaptive curriculum-guided hard negative mining; layer-dependent adaptive thresholds; and a warm-up cosine annealing learning-rate schedule for improved optimization stability. Together, these modifications strengthen the FF paradigm while preserving its biologically plausible and memory-efficient properties. Experiments on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST demonstrate consistent performance improvements over the baseline FF algorithm, achieving up to +1.45% improvement on MNIST and +1.50% improvement on Fashion-MNIST without significant computational overhead. Our results suggest that local learning methods can become substantially more competitive when goodness estimation and training dynamics are carefully designed.

</details>


### [19] [Block-Based Double Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18807)
*Asher Labovich,Benjamin Bradley,Vanessa Alexander,Chaitanya Harsha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于块的雙解碼器（Block-based Double Decoders）架构，通过双重因果块级注意力掩码，结合了解码器训练效率与编码器-解码器推理效率，在扩展定律实验中优于编码器-解码器，并接近解码器性能，同时推理时KV缓存和逐token计算量降低至少2/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码器-解码器模型在推理时比仅解码器模型更高效，但其预训练目标存在监督稀疏和序列长度动态变化的问题，导致在大规模实践中难以使用。

Method: 提出块级雙解碼器（Block-based Double Decoders）架构，采用双重因果块级注意力掩码（doubly-causal block-based attention masks），实现全损失监督和静态序列打包，结合了解码器训练效率与编码器-解码器推理效率。

Result: 在扩展定律实验中，块级雙解碼器显著优于编码器-解码器，并紧密追踪仅解码器模型的性能。推理时KV缓存和逐token计算量降低至少2/3，且无需牺牲预填充缓存或其他现有推理优化。

Conclusion: 块级雙解碼器架构有效弥合了编码器-解码器推理效率与解码器训练效率之间的差距，为大规模实践提供了一种可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Encoder-decoder models offer substantial inference-time savings over decoder-only models, but their pretraining objectives suffer from sparse supervision and dynamic sequence lengths, keeping them out of practice at scale. We propose block-based double decoders, a novel transformer architecture that utilizes doubly-causal block-based attention masks to train with full loss supervision and static sequence packing, combining decoder-only training efficiency with encoder-decoder inference efficiency. In scaling law experiments, block-based double decoders strongly outperform encoder-decoders and closely track decoder-only models across scales. At inference time, they cut KV-cache memory and per-token compute by at least 2/3 without sacrificing prefill caching or other existing inference optimizations available to decoder-only models.

</details>


### [20] [Compositional Literary Primitives in Instruction-Tuned LLMs: Cross-Architectural SAE Features for Self, Style, and Affect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18808)
*Joao Paulo Cavalcante Presa,Savio Salvarino Teles de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器分析两个指令微调大语言模型（Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct 和 Gemma 2 9B-IT）中文学基元的组合架构，发现了四类特征（命名门控、第一人称自我特征集群、文体风格调制器、组合情感），并利用5-LLM法官面板在27类情感分类上验证了覆盖能力，同时揭示了跨架构不对称性和方法论验证流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型内部是否具有可组合的文学基元表征结构，并验证这些基元能否通过稀疏自编码器被识别和操控，进而实现细粒度情感输出控制。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器在中等深度残差流上提取特征；采用三阶段验证流程（logit-lens、LLM-rate、5-LLM法官面板）；利用Cowen-Keltner 27类情感分类法进行覆盖评估；通过单特征调控和多特征组合实验分析特征功能。

Result: Llama模型通过命名门控+多特征配方+单自我特征调控达到27/27全覆盖；Gemma达到23/27（崇拜为严格失败项）；单细胞假阳性概率约10^{-3}，非随机主导；存在跨架构不对称性：Llama直接命名情感而Gemma通过场景意象唤起情感；两类模型均存在同时作为语域标记和情感发射器的自我特征。

Conclusion: 大语言模型内部存在可组合的文学基元架构，通过稀疏自编码器可系统化发现和调控；情感覆盖能力远超随机水平，但存在架构间差异；提出的三阶段验证方法论具有可复现性，单GPU约15分钟即可完成一个情感特征的发现周期。

Abstract: We characterize a compositional architecture of literary primitives in two instruction-tuned large language models (Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct and Gemma 2 9B-IT) via sparse autoencoders on mid-depth residual streams. Four feature classes emerge: naming-gates that promote lexical tokens of a target affect, an eleven-self cluster of first-person register features, stylistic register modulators (show-don't-tell and defamiliarization), and compositional emotions that arise only from multi-feature steering. Under a forced-choice 5-LLM judge panel applied to a 27-category emotion taxonomy (Cowen-Keltner), Llama reaches full 27/27 coverage by combining naming-gates, multi-feature recipes, and single self-feature steering; Gemma reaches 23/27 with adoration as the single residual strict-fail. Under random judging, the per-cell pass probability is on the order of $10^{-3}$ and the expected number of two-seed false-positive cells across the catalog is negligible, so the observed coverage is not consistent with chance. A cross-architectural asymmetry sits in the strict-versus-soft judge contrast: on the same generations, judges agree more often on Llama outputs than on Gemma outputs because Llama outputs name the target affect more directly while Gemma outputs evoke it through scene and imagery. Both architectures contain self-features that serve simultaneously as register markers and as emotion emitters, including a single most-RLHF-loaded self-feature per architecture that intensifies the institutional Helper-AI persona at one operating regime and produces affect-categorizable output at the same calibrated coefficient. Methodologically, the paper presents a three-stage validation pipeline (logit-lens, LLM-rate, 5-LLM judge) with documented anti-patterns; the total compute is single-GPU and about 15 minutes per emotion-feature discovery cycle.

</details>


### [21] [Metric-Gradient Projection for Stable Multi-Agent Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18809)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Sizhe Tang,Mahdi Imani,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HPML通过Hodge投影将多智能体系统的联合更新场分解为度量梯度势流和非势部分，并沿势流方向更新，从而增强多智能体学习的稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体学习中各智能体的策略更新相互耦合，产生非可积的循环交互动力学，导致学习缓慢或不稳定。现有方法通过局部或算法级修改来稳定MARL，但缺乏从全局更新场几何结构入手的方案。

Method: 将联合更新场视为L²空间中的向量场元素，计算Hodge型投影得到最接近的度量梯度势流；投影通过变分定义、Poisson方程刻画，并利用图基和摊销神经实现从样本中恢复投影方向。

Result: 投影动力学具有Lyapunov势，并给出带有显式非势项的均衡间隙界；控制实验验证了几何机制，CTDE基准测试表明HPML作为即插即用投影层可提升稳定性和归一化回报。

Conclusion: HPML通过纯几何投影方法解耦联合更新场中的可积改进分量，为多智能体学习提供了一种与现有正则化、信用分配等方法互补的稳定化框架。

Abstract: General-sum multi-agent learning is often governed by a stacked update field in which each agent's policy update changes the optimization landscape faced by the others. This coupling can entangle an integrable component of collective improvement with cyclic interaction dynamics, leading to slow or unstable multi-agent learning. Existing approaches, such as regularization, credit assignment, and consensus methods, stabilize MARL through local or algorithmic modifications; HPML complements them by projecting the joint update field onto a metric-gradient component. We introduce \textbf{HPML} (\textbf{H}odge-\textbf{P}rojected \textbf{M}ulti-agent \textbf{L}earning), which views the joint update field of a multi-agent system as an element of an $L^2$ space of vector fields and computes a Hodge-type projection onto the closest metric-gradient potential flow. HPML follows the projected component as the update direction, yielding the closest metric-gradient field under the chosen metric and sampling measure. The projection is defined variationally, characterized by a Poisson-type equation, and implemented through graph-based and amortized neural realizations that recover projected directions from samples. We show that the projected dynamics admit a Lyapunov potential and yield equilibrium-gap bounds with an explicit additive non-potentiality term. Controlled experiments validate the geometric mechanism, and CTDE benchmarks show improved stability and normalized return when HPML is used as a plug-in projection layer in MARL pipelines.

</details>


### [22] [GRASP: Deterministic argument ranking in interaction graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19141)
*Diganta Misra,Antonio Orvieto,Rediet Abebe,Volkan Cevher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出GRASP框架，通过确定性的局部交互判断聚合替代整体式LLM评判，解决评判不一致问题，并区分结构性充分性与修辞说服力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自动裁判时，整体式评判（holistic judging）存在严重的跨模型不一致性，这是因为将辩论的复杂交互结构坍缩为单一不透明分数所致。

Method: 提出GRASP（Gradual Ranking with Attacks and Support Propagation），一个确定性框架，通过收敛的攻击-防御传播算子聚合稳定的局部交互判断，生成全局排名。

Result: 局部交互判断比整体排名更具可复现性；GRASP生成的全局排名更一致；GRASP得分与人类"说服力"标签不相关，表明其衡量的是结构性充分性而非修辞效果。

Conclusion: GRASP提供了一种透明、可审计的替代方案，用于替代整体式LLM评判，并强调了论据评估中结构稳健性与修辞说服力之间重要的社会技术区分。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as automated judges to evaluate the strength of arguments. As this role expands, their legitimacy depends on consistency, transparency, and the ability to separate argumentative structure from rhetorical appeal. However, we show that holistic judging - a common LLM-as-a-Judge practice where a model provides a global verdict on a debate - suffers from substantial inter-model disagreement. We argue that this instability arises from collapsing a debate's complex interaction structure into a single opaque score. To address this, we propose GRASP (Gradual Ranking with Attacks and Support Propagation), a deterministic framework that aggregates stable local interaction judgments into a global ranking via a convergent attack--defense propagation operator. We show that local interaction judgments are more reproducible than holistic rankings in LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations, allowing GRASP to produce more consistent global rankings. We further show that GRASP scores do not correlate with human "convincingness" labels, highlighting a vital sociotechnical distinction: GRASP does not measure persuasion, factuality, or rhetorical appeal, but structural sufficiency - a defense-aware notion of argument robustness over the explicit interaction graph. Overall, GRASP offers a transparent and auditable alternative to holistic LLM judging.

</details>


### [23] [D-PACE: Dynamic Position-Aware Cross-Entropy for Parallel Speculative Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18810)
*Tianyu Wu,Yu Yao,Zhenting Qi,Han Zheng,Zhuohan Wang,Haoran Ma,Lawrence Liao,Himabindu Lakkaraju,Ju Li,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出D-PACE损失函数，通过可微的预期接受长度替代指标动态调整各位置的训练权重，使信号集中于当前限制接受率的位置，在不改变架构和推理过程的情况下，在多个基准、模型和温度设置下持续提升推测解码的加速比和平均生成长度，训练开销仅增加2.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式并行推测解码器中，多token训练目标使用固定的位置依赖权重（如头部依赖权重或块位置衰减），这些权重在训练过程中不会随限制接受的位置变化而自适应调整，导致训练信号分配不优，限制了推断加速效果。

Method: 从预期接受长度的可微替代量出发，推导出逐位置的训练权重，使得每个位置的权重与其对数概率的梯度贡献相匹配，从而动态地将训练信号集中于当前限制接受率的位置。具体实现为D-PACE（Dynamic Position-Aware Cross-Entropy）损失函数。

Result: 在六个基准、两个Qwen3-4B草案深度、两种解码温度和两个额外目标模型上，D-PACE一致提升了实际加速比和平均接受长度，训练开销仅为2.3%，且无需改变草案模型架构或推理过程。

Conclusion: 动态位置感知的交叉熵损失能有效自适应调整训练信号，在不增加推理开销的前提下显著提升推测解码性能，为并行草案模型的训练提供了更优的目标函数方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by having a small drafter propose tokens that a larger target model verifies in parallel. Recent diffusion-based parallel drafters such as DFlash predict the full B-token block in one forward pass, enabling deeper drafters and longer accepted blocks. However, existing multi-token drafter objectives often use fixed position-dependent weighting schedules, such as head-dependent weights or block-position decays, which do not adapt as the positions limiting acceptance change during training. To address this, we derive per-position training weights from a differentiable surrogate of expected accepted draft length, matching the weight of each position to its log-probability gradient contribution. The resulting loss, D-PACE (Dynamic Position-Aware Cross-Entropy), shifts training signal toward positions that currently limit acceptance as the drafter improves. Across six benchmarks, two Qwen3-4B draft depths, two decoding temperatures, and two additional target models, D-PACE consistently improves both wall-clock speedup and average emitted length, with 2.3\% measured training-time overhead and no changes to the drafter architecture or inference procedure.

</details>


### [24] [PASC: Pipeline-Aware Conformal Prediction with Joint Coverage Guarantees for Multi-Stage NLP and LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18812)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PASC（Pipeline-Aware Split Conformal）方法，将多阶段流水线联合覆盖问题归约为单一标量共形预测问题，提供有限样本无分布保证，在NER→NED→实体类型流水线上实现96.4%端到端覆盖率（Bonferroni为93.4%，独立CP为86.5%），且更快、更紧、对分布偏移更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流水线系统（如NER→NED→实体类型、检索增强生成、智能体链）中错误会跨阶段累积，但现有不确定性量化方法要么独立校准各阶段（无联合覆盖保证），要么使用Bonferroni联合界（保守且松弛），缺乏既能提供有限样本联合覆盖保证又不保守的方法。

Method: PASC将多阶段流水线的联合覆盖问题转化为单一标量共形预测问题：定义联合最大非一致性分数（joint maximum nonconformity score），在该分数上执行单次分位数计算，从而将K个阶段的同时覆盖概率控制在至少1-α，紧度高至1/(n+1)因子。

Result: 在三阶段NER→NED→实体类型流水线（CoNLL-2003）上，PASC端到端覆盖率达96.4%（Bonferroni 93.4%，独立CP 86.5%），平均预测集大小相同（1.083）。在WNUT-17 Twitter和WikiNEuRal分布偏移下，PASC经验性地维持目标覆盖，独立CP降至59%。PASC仅需单次分位数计算，速度比Bonferroni快1.7倍，可扩展至K=6阶段（独立CP端到端覆盖率跌至0.53）。

Conclusion: PASC提供了一种简单、高效、有理论保证的多阶段流水线联合不确定性量化方法，直接适用于复合LLM系统和智能体流水线。

Abstract: Modern NLP and LLM systems are pipelines: named entity recognition (NER) -> entity disambiguation (NED) -> entity typing, retrieval-augmented generation (retriever -> reader), and agentic chains of planner -> tool -> critic. Errors compound across stages, but existing uncertainty quantification methods either calibrate each stage independently (no joint coverage) or apply a Bonferroni union bound (joint coverage, but conservative). We present PASC (Pipeline-Aware Split Conformal), which reduces multi-stage joint coverage to a single scalar conformal prediction problem on the joint maximum nonconformity score. PASC provides a finite-sample distribution-free guarantee that all K stages are simultaneously covered with probability at least 1 - alpha, and is nearly tight up to a 1/(n+1) factor. On a three-stage NER -> NED -> entity-typing pipeline over CoNLL-2003, PASC achieves 96.4% end-to-end coverage versus 93.4% for Bonferroni and 86.5% for independent CP, at identical average prediction set size (1.083). Under distribution shift to WNUT-17 Twitter and WikiNEuRal Wikipedia data, PASC empirically maintains the target coverage in the tested shift settings while independent CP collapses to 59%. PASC requires a single quantile computation, runs 1.7x faster than Bonferroni, and scales to K = 6 stages where independent CP drops to 0.53 end-to-end coverage. The same joint-maximum-score reduction applies directly to compound LLM systems and agent pipelines.

</details>


### [25] [Composition of Memory Experts for Diffusion World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18813)
*Sebastian Stapf,Pablo Acuaviva Huertos,Aram Davtyan,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型的组合式专家框架，通过对比乘积-of-experts机制融合短期记忆、长期记忆和空间长期记忆三种专家，在不产生二次复杂度的前提下实现长上下文的高保真世界模型预测，在模拟和真实基准上提升了时间一致性、历史召回和导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型架构在记忆保真度和计算效率之间存在根本性权衡：Transformer虽保留局部细节但受限于二次注意力复杂度，循环和状态空间模型虽可扩展但压缩历史导致保真度下降。需要一种新范式来打破这种权衡。

Method: 提出扩散基框架，通过对比乘积-of-experts（contrastive product-of-experts）公式集成异质记忆模型。实例化三种专家：短期记忆专家（捕获细粒度局部动态）、长期记忆专家（通过轻量测试时微调将情节历史存储在外部扩散权重中）、空间长期记忆专家（强制几何和空间一致性）。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界基准上，该方法在时间一致性、历史观察召回和导航性能方面均优于现有方法，避免了模式坍塌，并能在不产生二次成本的情况下扩展到长上下文。

Conclusion: 该组合式设计为构建和运行记忆增强扩散世界模型建立了新范式，有效解决了传统架构中记忆保真度与计算效率之间的根本矛盾。

Abstract: World models aim to predict plausible futures consistent with past observations, a capability central to planning and decision-making in reinforcement learning. Yet, existing architectures face a fundamental memory trade-off: transformers preserve local detail but are bottlenecked by quadratic attention, while recurrent and state-space models scale more efficiently but compress history at the cost of fidelity. To overcome this trade-off, we suggest decoupling future-past consistency from any single architecture and instead leveraging a set of specialized experts. We introduce a diffusion-based framework that integrates heterogeneous memory models through a contrastive product-of-experts formulation. Our approach instantiates three complementary roles: a short-term memory expert that captures fine local dynamics, a long-term memory expert that stores episodic history in external diffusion weights via lightweight test-time finetuning, and a spatial long-term memory expert that enforces geometric and spatial coherence. This compositional design avoids mode collapse and scales to long contexts without incurring a quadratic cost. Across simulated and real-world benchmarks, our method improves temporal consistency, recall of past observations, and navigation performance, establishing a novel paradigm for building and operating memory-augmented diffusion world models.

</details>


### [26] [DynaTrain: Fast Online Parallelism Switching for Elastic LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18815)
*Yuanqing Wang,Yuchen Zhang,Hao Lin,Junhao Hu,Chunyang Zhu,Quanlu Zhang,Boxun Li,Guohao Dai,Zhi Yang,Daning Cheng,Yunquan Zhang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DynaTrain 是一个支持亚秒级在线重配置的分布式训练系统，通过虚拟参数空间（VPS）抽象统一多维度并行状态，在70B稠密模型上重配置仅需2秒以内，235B MoE模型仅需4.36秒，比现有方法快三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM训练具有动态性（资源波动、RLHF阶段切换、集群弹性），而现有训练框架基于静态执行模型，无法高效应对频繁变化的并行布局需求。

Method: 提出虚拟参数空间（VPS）抽象，将分布式训练状态统一到单一逻辑坐标空间；在此基础上构建状态路由与传输层实现内存感知、无死锁的rank局部传输；弹性设备管理器将新世界构建与正在进行的训练重叠，掩盖拓扑变化开销。

Result: 在70B稠密模型上重配置时间低于2秒，235B MoE模型上为4.36秒，性能比最先进的基于检查点和弹性系统提升三个数量级，同时保持正确性。

Conclusion: DynaTrain通过VPS抽象和在线重配置机制，有效解决了动态训练环境中的并行布局优化难题，实现了极高的重配置效率。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) training is inherently dynamic: resource fluctuations, RLHF phase shifts, and cluster elasticity continually reshape the optimal parallelism layout, posing a significant challenge to existing training frameworks built around a static execution model. We present DynaTrain, a distributed training system for sub-second, online reconfiguration across arbitrary multi-dimensional parallelism. At its core, we propose a Virtual Parameter Space (VPS) abstraction that unifies all distributed training states under one logical coordinate space, turning any parallelism configuration into a deterministic mapping and collapsing complex transition into manageable geometric intersections. On top of VPS, a state routing-and-transition layer executes rank-local transfers under a memory-aware, deadlock-free schedule, and an Elastic Device Manager overlaps new-world construction with ongoing training to mask topology-change cost. On dense and MoE models up to 235B parameters, DynaTrain reconfigures a 70B dense model in under 2s and a 235B MoE model in 4.36s, outperforming state-of-the-art checkpoint-based and elastic systems by up to three orders of magnitude while preserving correctness.

</details>


### [27] [Emergence of Frontier Superposition: Möbius attractor and Cascade Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18820)
*Hongyu Gu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在Erdős-Rényi图的可达性推理任务中，通过梯度下降可以学习到超位置（superposition）推理机制。关键发现是：Möbius吸引子（架构贡献）结合级联监督（Cascade Supervision，监督贡献）能够使Transformer在单次前向传播中并行执行广度优先搜索式的深度推理，而端到端监督由于内部梯度随深度指数衰减而失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhu等人（2025）手工构造了等权重广度优先超位置状态用于图可达性推理，但梯度下降能否在置换对称鞍点的阻碍下找到这一目标状态尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，揭示超位置推理在梯度下降下的可学习性。

Method: 架构层面：识别出Möbius吸引子——在树状区域中，由于S_n对称性，逐层动力学约化为1D Möbius映射，其零集是包含等权重超位置状态的全局最优的余维一流形。监督层面：提出级联监督损失类，其反向传播同时提供选择性自举、跨深度梯度持久性和每步判别能力；证明端到端监督因内部梯度在深度D下衰减为(np)^{-(D-c-2)/2}而失败。

Result: 理论预测：在深度D=3时，最终步余弦相似度端到端为0.35，级联为0.71；实验验证结果为0.37 vs 0.69，每步误差在0.02以内。

Conclusion: Möbius吸引子 + 级联监督 = 超位置推理的涌现。该工作从架构和监督两个维度系统揭示了Transformer通过梯度下降学习深度并行推理的机制。

Abstract: Superposition allows Transformers to reason in depth, carrying an entire reasoning frontier in parallel through a bounded-depth forward pass instead of unrolling serial chain-of-thought tokens. While Zhu et al. (2025) hand-crafted an equal-weight breadth-first frontier in a single residual stream for graph reachability, it remained open whether gradient descent could ever find this target amidst permutation-symmetric saddles.
  We close this gap on Reachability-by-Superposition over Erdős-Rényi graphs by isolating architectural and supervisional contributions. Architecturally, we identify a Möbius attractor: under $S_n$-symmetry in the tree regime, layerwise dynamics reduce to a 1D Möbius map whose zero set is a codimension-one manifold of global optima containing the equal-weight superposition state.
  On the supervision side, we identify Cascade Supervision: a loss class whose backward pass simultaneously delivers (A) selectivity bootstrap, (B) gradient persistence across depth, and (C) per-step discrimination (e.g., \mathcal{L}_{sup} and \mathcal{L}_{node}). End-to-end supervision fails condition (B) and is provably insufficient: internal gradients at layer c decay as (np)^{-(D-c-2)/2} in the graph fan-out and stall before the manifold is reached.
  Our thesis: Möbius attractor + Cascade Supervision = emergence of superposition reasoning. The parameter-free decay law predicts a final-step cosine of 0.35 vs. 0.71 (end-to-end vs. cascade) at depth D=3; experiments confirm 0.37 vs. 0.69, matching within 0.02 at every step.

</details>


### [28] [Quantum Adversarial Machine Learning: From Classical Adaptations to Quantum-Native Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18821)
*Roozbeh Razavi-Far,Mohammad Meymani,Erfan Mahmoudinia,Dorsa Vazirzade,Peyman Paknezhad,Fateme Ghasemi,Saeed Saravani,Somayeh Nikkhoo,Kimia Haghjooei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了量子对抗机器学习领域，探讨了量子机器学习模型面临的对抗性攻击漏洞及防御策略，回顾了理论基础、新兴趋势和关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习虽在复杂任务上展现出超越经典机器学习的潜力，但仍易受对抗性攻击。鉴于量子计算和机器学习的快速发展，亟需系统研究量子机器学习的脆弱性、可能的攻击方式及量子增强的防御策略。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了量子对抗机器学习领域的现有攻击手段和防御对策，并对理论基础、新兴趋势和关键挑战进行了回顾与分析。

Result: 本文提供了量子对抗机器学习的详细概述，整理了现有的攻击与防御方法，并识别了该领域的理论基础、新兴趋势和关键挑战。

Conclusion: 量子对抗机器学习作为新兴交叉领域，对保障量子机器学习的安全性至关重要；未来需进一步研究量子特有的攻击向量和高效的防御机制。

Abstract: Machine learning has revolutionized numerous industrial domains. Despite recent advances, machine learning models remain vulnerable to adversarial threats. Adversarial machine learning is a field that studies these vulnerabilities to build robust machine learning models. Quantum machine learning is an interdisciplinary field that bridges quantum computing and classical machine learning. While quantum machine learning shows potentials to outperform classical machine learning in complex tasks such as regression, classification, and generative modeling, it remains vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Given the recent advancements in quantum computing and machine learning, the quantum adversarial machine learning field has emerged to study the vulnerabilities of quantum machine learning, possible attacks, and novel quantum-enhanced defense strategies. In this survey, we provide a detailed overview on quantum adversarial machine learning and explore the existing attacks and countermeasures. We also review the theoretical underpinnings of this area, emerging trends, and critical challenges.

</details>


### [29] [Hybrid-LoRA: Bridging Full Fine-Tuning and Low-Rank Adaptation for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18822)
*Chengqian Zhang,Wei Zhu,Kyumin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hybrid-LoRA，一种高效混合后训练框架，在固定参数预算下选择性地对部分模块进行全微调，其余模块使用LoRA适配，在10%全微调模块预算下性能接近全微调，平均比最优基线提升4.36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR后训练中全微调GPU内存消耗大、训练成本高；PEFT方法虽降低计算成本但在复杂推理任务的后训练中与全微调存在明显性能差距。

Method: 提出Hybrid-LoRA混合后训练框架，对不适合低秩适配的少量模块进行全微调，其余组件使用LoRA适配；引入Hybrid-LoRA Score，根据模块对低秩适配的敏感度在固定参数预算下对候选模块进行排序。

Result: 在10%全微调模块预算下，Hybrid-LoRA性能接近全微调；一致优于四种最先进PEFT后训练基线，最高提升5.65%，平均提升4.36%。

Conclusion: Hybrid-LoRA通过选择性混合全微调与LoRA，有效弥合了PEFT与全微调在复杂推理后训练中的性能差距，同时保持计算效率。

Abstract: Post-training has become essential for adapting large language models (LLMs) to complex downstream behaviors, including instruction following, preference alignment, and multi-step reasoning. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has recently emerged as a particularly effective post-training paradigm for improving reasoning capabilities, with critic-free algorithms such as GRPO and GSPO enabling scalable optimization. However, RLVR post-training with full fine-tuning (FFT) requires substantial GPU memory and incurs high training costs. Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), effectively reduce computational costs, they often suffer from a noticeable performance gap compared to full fine-tuning in post-training for complex reasoning tasks. In this paper, we propose Hybrid-LoRA, an efficient hybrid post-training framework that selectively applies full fine-tuning to a small subset of modules less suited to low-rank adaptation, while adapting the remaining components with LoRA. We introduce a novel Hybrid-LoRA Score to rank candidate modules according to their sensitivity to low-rank adaptation under a fixed parameter budget. Experiments show that Hybrid-LoRA closely matches full fine-tuning performance under a 10% full fine-tuning module budget, with the remaining candidate modules adapted by LoRA, consistently outperforming four state-of-the-art PEFT post-training baselines, achieving improvements of up to 5.65% and on average 4.36% over the best baseline.

</details>


### [30] [Not All Tokens Are Worth Caching: Learning Semantic-Aware Eviction for LLM Prefix Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18825)
*Shaoke Fang,Ziang Li,Wenfei Wu,Jiatong Ji,Qingsong Liu,Ruizhi Pu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAECache是一种语义自适应的前缀缓存淘汰策略，通过多队列架构、语义感知令牌权重机制和全自适应在线学习，针对LLM提示中不同令牌类型的复用率差异（最高756倍）进行优化，相较于生产级基线实现1.4x-2.7x的TTFT提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前缀缓存淘汰策略（如LRU）将所有缓存块统一对待，忽略了LLM提示中不同令牌类型（系统提示、用户查询、工具输出、模型响应、思维链推理等）在复用率上存在高达756倍的差异，导致缓存效率低下。

Method: (1) 多队列架构：将KV块路由到任务特定队列，使用定制化优先级指标，兼顾多轮会话复用和模板化单次请求的结构复用；(2) 语义感知令牌权重机制：通过淘汰反馈在线学习不同令牌类型的复用价值；(3) 全自适应在线学习：对所有参数（对数正态时序参数、位置衰减幂次、队列权重和元参数）进行自适应更新，无需手动调参。

Result: 在异构工作负载下，SAECache相比生产级基线实现1.4x-2.7x的TTFT提升，而固定参数方案在工作负载不匹配时性能可下降2.7倍。

Conclusion: 语义感知的缓存淘汰策略能显著提升前缀缓存效率，全自适应学习机制有效避免了手动调参带来的性能退化风险。

Abstract: Prefix caching is a key optimization in Large Language Model (LLM) serving, reusing attention Key-Value (KV) states across requests with shared prompt prefixes to reduce expensive prefill computation. However, its benefit depends critically on the eviction policy as GPU memory is scarce, and existing policies such as LRU largely treat cached blocks uniformly. This view ignores a fundamental property of LLM prompts: not all tokens are equally worth caching. We show that different token types within a prompt, including system prompts, user queries, tool outputs, model responses, and chain-of-thought reasoning, exhibit up to 756x variation in reuse rates, yet no existing eviction policy exploits this signal. In this paper, we present SAECache (Semantic-Adaptive Eviction for prefix caches), a semantic-adaptive prefix cache eviction policy that addresses this gap through three innovations: (1) a multi-queue architecture that routes KV blocks to task-specific queues with tailored priority metrics, capturing both session reuse in multi-turn requests and structural reuse in templated single-turn requests; (2) a semantic-aware token weighting mechanism that learns the reuse value of different token types online through eviction feedback; and (3) a fully adaptive online learning schema for all parameter updates, including log-normal timing parameters, position decay power, queue weights, and meta-parameters, which eliminates manual tuning and enables automatic adaptation to deployment-specific workload characteristics. Through extensive evaluation across heterogeneous workloads, we demonstrate that SAECache achieves 1.4x-2.7x TTFT improvement over production-style baselines, while fixed-parameter alternatives can degrade by up to 2.7x under workload mismatch -- a failure mode our adaptive approach avoids entirely.

</details>


### [31] [The Routing and Filtering Structure of Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18826)
*Shafayeth Jamil,Rehan Kapadia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现注意力机制中的QK^T矩阵可分解为路由（斜对称）和过滤（对称）两种成分，路由在低秩下运行。通过提出的S-D注意力解耦二者，发现路由呈现谱级联现象，据此可预测哪些层可简化为线性注意力，实现参数-困惑度的高效权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制中QK^T的计算同时包含了路由（信息位置间重分配）和过滤（相互相关性缩放）两种纠缠的运算，作者希望解耦这两种机制，理解注意力内部的工作方式，并据此优化注意力架构设计。

Method: （1）对1776个注意力头进行分解分析，识别QK^T中的斜对称（路由）和对称（过滤）成分；（2）提出S-D注意力参数化方法，显式解耦路由与过滤，保证稳定性（Re(λ)≤0）且无需层归一化即可稳定训练；（3）在多尺度模型（7M-355M参数）上分析解耦后路由的谱结构。

Result: （1）路由在低秩（远低于权重核分配的路由容量）下运行；（2）解耦后路由自组织为谱级联：首层有效秩为2，随深度扩展；（3）可预测线性化区域：125M模型中前7层线性化代价<5%困惑度，而标准注意力崩溃；（4）用ELU+1替换前4层线性注意力，在全头维度下仅差1.4%；（5）级联分配架构以47%-65%更少注意力参数换取+3.9%到+8.4%困惑度。

Conclusion: 路由-过滤分解使注意力的谱预算变得可理解，谱级联使注意力简化和参数优化变得可行，揭示了不同层对全秩注意力的需求差异，为设计更高效的注意力架构提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The attention interaction matrix $QK^{\top}$ contains two entangled computations: a skew-symmetric component that redistributes information between positions (routing) and a symmetric component that scales mutual relevance (filtering). We decompose 1776 heads across five pretrained transformers and find routing operating at low rank, well below the routing capacity allocated by the weight kernel. We introduce $S$-$D$ attention as a diagnostic parameterization that disentangles routing from filtering by construction with guaranteed stability ($\mathrm{Re}(λ) \le 0$) and trains stably without layer normalization. When disentangled and unnormalized, routing self-organizes into a spectral cascade, effective rank $2$ at the first layer, expanding with depth across six scales from 7M to 355M parameters. The cascade predicts where attention can be simplified: linearizing the first seven layers of 125M $S$-$D$ attention costs ${<}5\%$ perplexity, whereas standard attention collapses under the same intervention. The linearizable region widens with depth. Replacing the first four layers with ELU+1 linear attention reaches within $1.4\%$ of baseline at full head dimension. Cascade-allocated architectures trade attention parameters for perplexity ($47\%-65\%$ fewer attention parameters at $+3.9\%$ to $+8.4\%$ PPL). The routing-filtering decomposition makes the spectral budget legible; the cascade makes it actionable.

</details>


### [32] [Lossless Anti-Distillation Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18829)
*Zibo Diao,Jingchu Gai,Xinyue Ai,Zhang Zhang,Zhenyu He,Di He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LADS是一种无损抗蒸馏采样方案，通过基于查询语义和用户查询次数的私有种子控制生成随机性，使良性用户获得独立采样响应，而蒸馏者不同账户因共享潜在随机性导致收集数据相关，从而在理论上降低蒸馏模型的泛化收敛速度，实验验证了其在图像生成、数学推理和代码生成任务上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿商业生成模型面临蒸馏攻击威胁，现有防御方法要么牺牲良性用户的响应质量，要么依赖行为检测（易被多账户查询绕过），因此需要一种既能抵御多账户蒸馏又不对良性用户造成质量损失的方法。

Method: 提出无损抗蒸馏采样（LADS），设计基于查询语义内容和用户查询次数的私有种子来决定每次生成的随机性，使得良性用户每次获得独立采样响应，而蒸馏者多账户在相同语义桶内共享潜在随机性，导致收集数据相关。

Result: 利用均匀收敛理论证明LADS在无条件/条件生成设置下均能降低蒸馏模型泛化差距的收敛速率；在图像生成、数学推理和代码生成实验中，LADS显著降低了蒸馏学生模型的性能，同时保持了对单个用户精确的统计保真度。

Conclusion: LADS实现了无损的多账户蒸馏防御，在理论上和实验上均证明能有效破坏蒸馏数据的统计独立性，降低蒸馏模型性能，同时不影响良性用户体验。

Abstract: Frontier commercial generative models face a growing threat from distillation, whereby a distiller harvests generated responses and trains a competing model of its own at drastically lower cost. Existing defenses either rely on modifying the models outputs, thereby sacrificing response quality for benign users, or on behavioral detection methods, which can be readily circumvented by distributing queries across multiple accounts. In this work, we propose Lossless Anti-Distillation Sampling (LADS), a novel sampling scheme specifically designed to counter multi-account distillation while maintaining a lossless experience for benign users. Concretely, LADS derives the randomness underlying each generation from a private seed determined by the semantic content of the query and the number of times the user has queried the model. By construction, every benign user receives a response independently sampled from the original model at each visit, and thus experiences no distortion. In contrast, for a distiller, different accounts share latent randomness whenever their queries fall in the same semantic bucket. As a result, the harvested data becomes correlated, potentially reducing sample diversity and degrading generalization. Using uniform convergence theory, we show that LADS provably degrades the convergence rate of the distillers generalization gap relative to standard i.i.d. sampling in both unconditional and conditional generation settings. Experiments on image generation, mathematical reasoning, and code generation confirm that LADS substantially degrades the performance of distilled students while preserving exact statistical fidelity for individual users.

</details>


### [33] [Precision Tracked Transformer via Kalman Filtering, Kriging and Process Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18832)
*Bo Long,Deepak Agarwal,Jelena Markovic-Voronov,Yi Wang,Liuqing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯滤波Transformer (BFT)，通过引入精度（precision）机制替代传统Transformer中所有token同等对待的设定，将注意力机制变为精度加权克里金插值、残差连接变为卡尔曼更新、FFN变为动态模型。BFT可无缝替换任意Transformer层，在序列推荐和LLM微调中均取得显著提升，尤其对冷启动和高噪声场景效果明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer对所有token赋予统一置信度，无法处理现实应用中普遍存在的不确定性（如冷启动token、异质信号质量、注意力塌缩），缺乏对不确定性的原则性处理机制。

Method: 提出贝叶斯滤波Transformer (BFT)，将注意力机制重新表述为精度加权克里金插值，残差连接变为带自适应增益的卡尔曼更新，FFN变为通过Jacobian加过程噪声规则传播精度的动态模型。观测精度通过无参数约束最大似然估计（REML）结合共轭贝叶斯先验获得。

Result: 在三个主要序列推荐架构上应用于六个基准取得显著提升，冷启动用户和稀有物品提升最大；在LLM有监督微调中，对噪声监督（QA中token标签污染）和噪声上下文（RAG含干扰项）两种场景均提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 恢复精度这一单一原则性修改，在经典序列建模和现代LLM场景中均可释放显著的性能空间，验证了不确定性建模在Transformer架构中的重要性。

Abstract: The Transformer is the foundational building block of modern AI, yet offers no principled handling of \emph{uncertainty}, which is prevalent in real applications: cold-start tokens with sparse histories in sequential recommendation, heterogeneous signal quality in language models, and attention sinks induced by unconstrained softmax. Every token is treated with uniform confidence. We show this uniformity is a degenerate case of our \emph{Bayesian Filtering Transformer} (BFT): attention becomes precision-weighted kriging, the residual connection becomes a Kalman update with adaptive gain, and the FFN becomes a dynamics model propagating precision via a Jacobian--plus--process-noise rule. Observation precision comes from a parameter-free Restricted Maximum Likelihood (REML) estimator with a conjugate Bayesian prior. BFT replaces any Transformer layer with negligible overhead. On sequential recommendation, BFT applied to three major architectures yields significant gains on six benchmarks, with the largest improvements on cold-start users and rare items where uncertainty is highest. On supervised fine-tuning of large language models with noisy data, BFT improves robustness in two regimes: noisy supervision (token-label corruption in question answering) and noisy context (retrieval-augmented QA with real RAG distractors). A single principled modification -- restoring precision -- unlocks substantial headroom across both classical sequence-modeling and modern LLM regimes.

</details>


### [34] [Automated Big Data Quality Assessment using Knowledge Graph Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18833)
*Hadi Fadlallah,Rima Kilany,Mitri Haber,Ali Jaber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于知识图谱嵌入的自动化数据质量评估方法，通过预测输入数据集上下文与质量规则/维度之间的缺失边，构建上下文感知的综合数据质量评估计划，并在真实辐射传感器数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化数据质量评估方法难以实现准确的上下文感知评估，传统方法依赖严格匹配而忽略了上下文特征。

Method: 利用知识图谱嵌入预测输入数据集上下文表示与相关质量规则/维度之间的缺失边；通过文献调研整合多样化表示；注入数值边属性为每个预测的质量测量分配权重，生成上下文特定的数据质量评估计划。

Result: 在黎巴嫩原子能委员会提供的真实辐射传感器数据集上评估，展示了生成综合数据质量评估计划的能力。

Conclusion: 该方法通过知识图谱增强了上下文理解，克服了传统严格匹配方法的局限性，能够为不同上下文生成全面的数据质量评估方案。

Abstract: Automated data quality assessment is crucial for managing big data, but existing solutions face challenges in achieving accurate context-aware assessment. This paper presents a novel knowledge-based approach to enhance automated data quality assessment. Our approach utilizes knowledge graph embeddings to predict missing edges between the input dataset's context representation and the relevant quality rules and dimensions within a knowledge graph representing contextual data characteristics and the required quality assessment operations. We surpass conventional practices by integrating diverse representations within the knowledge graph, drawing insights from contextual information from a thorough literature investigation. This integration allows us to develop a comprehensive and context-specific data quality assessment plan tailored to each context. Leveraging the knowledge graph improves our understanding of the input dataset's context, overcoming the limitations of traditional methods that rely solely on strict matching and overlook contextual characteristics. By injecting numerical edge attributes, we assign corresponding weights to each predicted quality measurement, providing a comprehensive data quality assessment plan for the input dataset.
  To evaluate our approach, we leverage AmpliGraph, a framework developed and benchmarked by AccentureLabs. The evaluation involves employing a real-world radiation sensors dataset provided by the Lebanese Atomic Energy Commission (LAEC-CNRS). The results obtained from this evaluation demonstrate the capability of our solution to generate a comprehensive data quality assessment plan for the given input dataset.

</details>


### [35] [Spectral Gradient Surgery for Domain-Generalizable Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18836)
*Minyoung Oh,Najeong Chae,Jae-Young Sim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出领域可泛化数据集蒸馏（DGDD）问题，针对分布匹配（DM）方法在分布外（OOD）泛化上的脆弱性，提出频谱梯度手术（SGS）方法，通过解耦类别判别信息与领域特定信息，在蒸馏过程中增强跨域共享梯度成分并促进数据集多样性，显著提升OOD泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准数据集蒸馏假设训练与测试数据同分布，但实际中常违背；现有领域泛化方法依赖真实数据集的自然多样性，而紧凑合成数据集缺乏这种多样性，且数据增强开销与蒸馏的效率目标冲突。

Method: 提出频谱梯度手术（SGS），利用跨域梯度在频谱域的一致性来区分类别判别组件（跨域共享）与领域特定组件，在标准DM更新中补充两个梯度：一个强化跨域共享成分，另一个显式促进蒸馏数据集的多样性。

Result: 在多种规模基准上，SGS显著提升了OOD泛化性能，且与现有DM方法即插即用兼容。

Conclusion: 通过解耦类别判别与领域特定信息，SGS有效解决了数据集蒸馏在分布偏移场景下的泛化问题，为蒸馏数据的实际部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Dataset Distillation (DD) synthesizes a compact synthetic dataset that preserves the training utility of a full dataset. However, its standard formulation assumes that test data follow the same distribution as training data, an assumption that rarely holds in practice. A straightforward extension-applying post-hoc Domain Generalization (DG) techniques to distilled data-is ill-suited because existing DG methods rely on the natural diversity of real datasets, which compact synthetic sets inherently lack, while also incurring substantial augmentation overhead that conflicts with the efficiency objective of dataset distillation. To address this limitation, we introduce Domain Generalizable Dataset Distillation (DGDD), a new problem setting that explicitly targets out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization of distilled datasets. We study this problem through a widely adopted DD baseline of Distribution Matching (DM). We attribute the OOD vulnerability of DM to the entanglement of class-discriminative and domain-specific information within the compressed synthetic set, and propose Spectral Gradient Surgery (SGS) to disentangle the two. The key insight of SGS is that cross-domain agreement among domain-wise gradients in the spectral domain reveals which gradient components are shared across source domains-and are therefore class-discriminative-and which are domain-specific.
