Extensive offline evaluations demonstrate that D$^3$-Subsidy improves \texttt{Rides} and \texttt{GMV} while enhancing cap compliance, and a real-world A/B test confirms significant uplift while keeping budget-related violation metrics within operational thresholds.

</details>


### [143] [Active Context Selection Improves Simple Regret in Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20040)
*Mohammad Shahverdikondori,Jalal Etesami,Negar Kiyavash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限上下文空间的上下文多臂老虎机问题，允许学习者主动选择采样上下文。对于已知上下文分布p，被动采样与主动采样的遗憾率分别为√(n/T)∥p∥_{1/2}和√(n/T)∥p∥_{2/3}，主动采样可获得高达Θ(k^{1/4})的改进。对于未知p，提出了EETC算法，在大时间范围内匹配已知p的主动采样率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何通过主动选择采样上下文（类似于实验设计中的受控子群体采样）来降低上下文多臂老虎机中基于上下文加权的简单遗憾，特别是针对有限上下文空间（子群体）的情形。

Method: 理论分析被动采样与主动采样的遗憾率下界与上界；提出已知p时的最优主动分配q_j ∝ p_j^{2/3}；针对未知p提出Explore-Explore-Then-Commit (EETC)算法，平衡上下文分布估计与切换至主动分配的时间。

Result: 被动采样遗憾率为√(n/T)∥p∥_{1/2}，主动采样达到紧致率√(n/T)∥p∥_{2/3}，改进幅度可达Θ(k^{1/4})。EETC算法在未知p时匹配已知p的主动率（至多常数因子）。

Conclusion: 主动采样策略能显著提升有限上下文空间下多臂老虎机的性能，且当上下文分布未知时，EETC算法可有效逼近最优主动采样率。

Abstract: We study the contextual multi-armed bandit problem with a finite context space (a.k.a. subpopulations), where the learner recommends a best action for each context and is evaluated by context-weighted simple regret. Our guarantees are worst-case over the reward distributions, while remaining instance-dependent with respect to the context distribution vector $p$. Akin to experimental design problems where the population of interest is fixed but the sampled subpopulation can be controlled, we allow the learner to actively choose which context to sample from. For a known $p$, we characterize tight regret rates: passive sampling where contexts are randomly revealed achieves regret of order $\sqrt{n/T \, \lVert p \rVert_{1/2}}$, whereas active sampling with allocation $q_j \propto p_j^{2/3}$ achieves the tight rate $\sqrt{n/T} \, \lVert p \rVert_{2/3}$. The resulting improvement can be as large as $Θ(k^{1/4})$, where $k$ is the number of contexts. We further extend the analysis to budgeted active sampling, characterize the corresponding tight rate, and identify when a limited active budget suffices to recover the fully active rate. When $p$ is unknown, we propose the Explore-Explore-Then-Commit (EETC) algorithm, which optimally balances estimating the context distribution and the time to switch to active allocation, such that for large horizons, it matches the known-$p$ active rate up to constants. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data support our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [144] [Smooth Partial Lotteries for Stable Randomized Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20069)
*Alexander Goldberg,Giulia Fanti,Nihar B. Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"平滑性"作为部分抽签机制的设计原则，并引入裁剪线性抽签，该机制在选择概率与评估分数之间建立Lipschitz连续性，理论证明其遗憾值达到下界，实验验证了现有抽签设计的不稳定性及所提方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部分抽签机制存在固有不稳定性：单个候选人分数的微小变化可能导致其选中概率大幅波动，这削弱了抽签减少决策边界处细微分数区分影响的核心目标。

Method: 将平滑性形式化为从候选人评分到选择概率映射的Lipschitz条件；提出裁剪线性抽签，在上下阈值之间选择概率与估计质量呈线性关系，上下阈值之外分别全接受或全拒绝；从理论上证明其最坏情况遗憾值与任何平滑选择规则的下界匹配（至多相差因子(1 - k/n)）。

Result: 理论证明裁剪线性抽签的平滑性-遗憾权衡优于个体公平性和差分隐私等替代方案；在ICLR 2025、NeurIPS 2024和瑞士国家科学基金的同行评审数据上实验表明现有抽签高度不稳定，而所提方法实现了更好的平滑性-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 平滑性应作为部分抽签机制的核心设计原则，裁剪线性抽签在理论上最优、在实践中有效，能够稳定地降低评选过程中分数细微波动的负面影响。

Abstract: Competitive selection processes, from scientific funding to admissions and hiring, use evaluations to score candidates, and eventually choose a subset of them based on those scores. Recently, many organizations have adopted partial lotteries, which randomize selection based on evaluation scores. However, existing lottery designs are inherently unstable, as a small change to a single candidate's score can cause large shifts in their selection probabilities. This instability undermines a key goal of lotteries: reducing the influence of fine-grained score distinctions near the decision boundary. We propose smoothness as a design principle for partial lotteries, formalizing it as a Lipschitz condition on the mapping from review scores over candidates to selection probabilities. We introduce the Clipped Linear Lottery, a simple mechanism in which selection probabilities scale linearly with estimated quality between an upper threshold, above which we always accept, and a lower threshold, below which we always reject. We prove that the Clipped Linear Lottery's worst-case regret matches a lower bound for any smooth selection rule up to a factor of $(1 - k/n)$, where $k/n$ is the acceptance rate. We compare smooth selection to other stability notions like Individual Fairness and Differential Privacy, showing that the Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-regret tradeoff than alternatives. Experiments on real peer review data from ICLR 2025, NeurIPS 2024, and the Swiss National Science Foundation demonstrate that existing lottery designs are highly unstable in practice even under perturbations to a single score. Our experiments also confirm the tightness of our theoretical analysis and show that our proposed Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-utility tradeoff than alternatives in practice.

</details>


### [145] [INSHAPE: Instance-Level Shapelets for Interpretable Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20088)
*Seongjun Lee,Seokhyun Lee,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INSHAPE提出了一种实例级可解释时间序列分类框架，通过发现每个时间序列特有的可变长度判别性时间模式（shapelets），并建模其时间依赖关系，同时支持局部与全局可解释性，在128个UCR和30个UEA基准数据集上优于现有shapelet方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有shapelet方法主要关注全局（数据集级）模式，存在两个根本局限：(i) 全局模式与实例特定特征不匹配，导致次优性能和误导性解释；(ii) 大多将shapelet视为独立实体，忽略了多个模式间的时序依赖关系。

Method: INSHAPE框架：(1) 为每个时间序列发现非重叠的可变长度判别性子序列（实例级shapelet）；(2) 建模这些shapelet之间的时序依赖关系；(3) 通过自底向上的方法，将实例级shapelet聚合为原型（全局级）shapelet，桥接局部与全局可解释性。

Result: 在128个UCR和30个UEA基准数据集上的大量实验表明，INSHAPE一致优于最先进的基于shapelet的方法，同时提供更直观和可解释的洞察。

Conclusion: INSHAPE通过实例级shapelet发现与时序依赖建模，有效解决了全局模式与实例特征不匹配的问题，兼顾了分类性能与可解释性，并能自然地将局部解释聚合为全局理解。

Abstract: Discovering shapelets -- i.e., discriminative temporal patterns within time series -- has been widely studied to address the inherent complexity of time-series classification (TSC) and to make model decision-making processes more transparent. However, existing methods primarily focus on population-level shapelets optimized across the entire dataset, which leads to two fundamental limitations: (i) population-level patterns often misalign with instance-specific features, resulting in suboptimal performance and potentially misleading interpretations, and (ii) most methods treat shapelets as independent entities, overlooking important temporal dependencies and interactions among multiple patterns. To address these limitations, we propose INSHAPE, an interpretable TSC framework that discovers variable-length, discriminative temporal patterns specific to each time series. INSHAPE identifies these patterns as non-overlapping segments and models their temporal dependencies, thereby providing clear instance-level interpretations while achieving strong predictive performance. Furthermore, INSHAPE bridges local and global interpretability through a bottom-up approach, aggregating instance-level shapelets into prototypical (population-level) shapelets. Extensive experiments on 128 UCR and 30 UEA benchmark datasets show that INSHAPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art shapelet-based methods while providing more intuitive and interpretable insights.

</details>


### [146] [Draft Less, Retrieve More: Hybrid Tree Construction for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20104)
*Yuhao Shen,Tianyu Liu,Xinyi Hu,Quan Kong,Baolin Zhang,Jun Dai,Jun Zhang,Shuang Ge,Lei Chen,Yue Li,Mingcheng Wan,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAFT是一种无需训练、无损的推测解码加速框架，通过“剪枝-嫁接”机制，将剪枝释放的计算预算用于检索高预测性token，填补剪枝造成的覆盖损失，从而打破密集草案树与剪枝策略之间的帕累托权衡，在短上下文、长上下文及大规模模型场景下均取得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法中，密集草案树虽能最大化接受率，但带来严重的VRAM带宽和计算开销，成为端到端加速的瓶颈。动态深度剪枝虽能降低延迟，但会丢弃潜在有效候选token，使接受率无法达到密集树上界。如何在剪枝释放计算预算的同时弥补覆盖损失，成为打破这一帕累托权衡的关键。

Method: 提出GRAFT框架，核心是“先剪枝后嫁接”（prune-then-graft）的顺序机制。首先对密集草案树进行剪枝以释放计算预算，然后在剪枝打开的拓扑位置中插入通过检索得到的高预测性token，以近零开销弥补剪枝导致的覆盖损失。该框架无需训练、无损，可适配自回归草案树和块级草案范式。

Result: 在短上下文基准上实现最高5.41倍加速，在Qwen3-235B大模型上相比EAGLE-3平均加速提升21.8%；在长上下文和大规模模型场景下均建立新的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: GRAFT通过剪枝与检索的互补协同，有效解决了推测解码中密集树与剪枝策略之间的效率-覆盖率权衡问题，为大规模语言模型推理加速提供了新的高效范式，并初步展示了向块级草案范式扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model inference by leveraging a draft-then-verify paradigm. To maximize the acceptance rate, recent methods construct expansive draft trees, which unfortunately incur severe VRAM bandwidth and computational overheads that bottleneck end-to-end speedups. While dynamic-depth pruning can reduce this latency by removing marginal branches, it also discards potentially valid candidates, preventing the acceptance rate from reaching the upper bound of dense trees. In this paper, we identify a critical opportunity in resource allocation: the transition from dense to pruned drafting frees up significant computational budget. To break this Pareto tradeoff, we introduce Graft, a compensation framework that couples pruning and retrieval as mutually reinforcing operations. Pruning supplies sufficient budget for retrieval, while retrieval compensates for pruning-induced coverage loss and recovers accepted length. By employing a sequential `prune-then-graft' mechanism, Graft attaches highly predictive retrieved tokens into positions opened by pruning, filling the topological gaps with near-zero overhead. Graft is entirely training-free and lossless. Comprehensive evaluations show that Graft establishes a new Pareto frontier across practical deployment settings, including short-context generation, long-context generation, and large-scale models. On short-context benchmarks, it achieves up to 5.41$\times$ speedup and improves average speedup over EAGLE-3 by up to 21.8% on the large-scale Qwen3-235B. We also provide a preliminary exploration of applying Graft to the DFlash-style block drafting paradigm, offering initial evidence and insights for extending grafting beyond autoregressive draft trees.

</details>


### [147] [Optimal Representation Size: High-Dimensional Analysis of Pretraining and Linear Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20105)
*Valentina Njaradi,Clémentine Dominé,Rachel Swanson,Marco Mondelli,Andrew Saxe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过主成分分析（无监督）和线性回归（有监督）的理论模型，解析了预训练-微调范式中表示维度、无标签/有标签样本量与任务对齐度对泛化性能的影响，并推导了最优表示维度的条件及预训练与监督之间的精确权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解从有限数据中泛化的基本挑战，揭示预训练结构提取与下游任务适应之间的相互作用机制，为表示维度选择提供理论依据。

Method: 将结构提取形式化为无标签数据的主成分分析，将下游学习形式化为有标签数据的线性回归；在高维极限下推导训练误差和泛化误差的精确表达式。

Result: 预训练表示显著影响下游泛化；最优表示维度取决于任务参数：预训练数据充足但下游数据稀缺时，最大化压缩表示最优；预训练数据有限时，高维表示泛化更好。建立了预训练与监督之间的精确权衡，量化了无标签样本替代单个有标签样本所需的数量。

Conclusion: 优化表示维度至关重要，给出了压缩在预训练中何时能改善泛化的条件；该现象在自编码器和预训练大语言模型中同样存在。

Abstract: Learning to generalise from limited data is a fundamental challenge for both artificial and biological systems. A common strategy is to extract reusable structure from abundant unlabelled data, enabling efficient adaptation to new tasks from limited labelled data. This two-stage paradigm is now standard in modern training pipelines, where pretraining is followed by fine-tuning or linear probing. We provide an analytical model of this process: structure extraction is formalized as principal component analysis on unlabelled data, and downstream learning as linear regression on a separate labelled dataset. In the high-dimensional regime, we derive exact expressions for training and generalisation error showcasing their dependence on representation dimensionality, unlabelled and labelled sample sizes, and task alignment. Our results show that pretrained representations strongly influence downstream generalisation, and we characterize the optimal representation size as a function of task parameters: with abundant pretraining data but scarce downstream data, maximally compressed representations are optimal, whereas with limited pretraining data, higher-dimensional representations generalise better. Furthermore, we establish an exact trade-off between pretraining and supervision, quantifying how much unlabelled data is required to replace a single labelled sample. Beyond our idealised model, we observe similar phenomenology in autoencoders and pretrained LLMs. Altogether, we highlight that optimising representation size is critical, giving conditions for when compression during pretraining improves generalisation.

</details>


### [148] [Beyond Isotropy in JEPAs: Hamiltonian Geometry and Symplectic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20107)
*Robert Jenkinson Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示JEPA中常用的各向同性高斯正则化并非无害默认设置：当下游几何结构已知时，最优协方差为(c/d)H^{-1}，各向同性会导致可计算的代价；当下游几何未知时，任何固定协方差形状都可能被最坏情况对齐。进一步证明即使oracle单视角边缘分布也无法确定视角间预测耦合。提出HamJEPA，将每个视角编码为相空间状态(q,p)，用哈密顿跳跃映射预测视角间转换，非各向同性尺度与谱底防止坍塌。在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上显著超越SIGReg，且消融实验证明辛耦合是邻域几何增益的关键驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战JEPA中普遍采用的各向同性高斯正则化假设，证明该假设在下游几何结构已知或未知时均存在根本性问题，并指出结构偏差应置于交叉视角耦合而非固定编码器边缘分布。

Method: 理论分析证明各向同性正则化的代价和局限性；提出HamJEPA方法，采用相空间编码(q,p)和哈密顿 leapfrog 映射进行视角间预测，结合非各向同性尺度与谱底防止坍塌。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上30 epoch时kNN@20提升+4.89、线性探针提升+3.52；80 epoch时分别提升+6.45和+10.64。在ImageNet-100上45 epoch时kNN@20提升+4.82、线性探针提升+7.52。消融实验证明辛耦合是邻域几何增益的关键。

Conclusion: JEPA的结构偏差应通过交叉视角耦合引入而非固定编码器边缘分布；HamJEPA通过哈密顿动力学建模视角间转换，在保持表示质量的同时显著提升下游性能。

Abstract: JEPAs often regularize one-view embeddings toward an isotropic Gaussian, implicitly baking Euclidean symmetry into the representation. We show that this is not merely a benign default. For a known structured downstream geometry $H\succ0$, the minimax and maximum-entropy covariance under a Hamiltonian energy budget is $(c/d)H^{-1}$, and Euclidean isotropy incurs a closed-form price of isotropy. More importantly, when the downstream geometry is unknown, no geometry-independent fixed marginal target is canonical: every fixed covariance shape can be maximally misaligned for some structured geometry. We further show that even oracle one-view marginals do not identify the JEPA view-to-view predictive coupling. These results suggest that the structural bias in JEPAs should enter the cross-view coupling rather than a fixed encoder marginal. We instantiate this principle with \textbf{HamJEPA}, which encodes each view as a phase-space state $(q,p)$ and predicts view-to-view transitions with a learned Hamiltonian leapfrog map, while non-isotropic scale and spectral floors prevent collapse. In a deliberately headless token protocol, HamJEPA improves over SIGReg on CIFAR-100 by $+4.89$ kNN@20 and $+3.52$ linear-probe points at 30 epochs, and by $+6.45$ kNN@20 and $+10.64$ linear-probe points at 80 epochs, while a matched MLP predictor ablation shows that the symplectic coupling is the ingredient driving the neighborhood-geometry gain. On ImageNet-100, HamJEPA-$q$ improves by $+4.82$ kNN@20 and $+7.52$ linear-probe points at 45 epochs.

</details>


### [149] [Toto 2.0: Time Series Forecasting Enters the Scaling Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20119)
*Emaad Khwaja,Chris Lettieri,Gerald Woo,Eden Belouadah,Marc Cenac,Guillaume Jarry,Enguerrand Paquin,Xunyi Zhao,Viktoriya Zhukov,Othmane Abou-Amal,Chenghao Liu,Ameet Talwalkar,David Asker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文表明时间序列基础模型具有扩展性：从4M到25亿参数，同一训练方案可稳定提升预测质量。发布了Toto 2.0系列（5个开源模型），在BOOM、GIFT-Eval和TIME三个基准上取得SOTA。报告详述了架构、训练方案、数据及u-muP超参数迁移流程，所有模型以Apache 2.0开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证时间序列基础模型是否具备与语言模型类似的规模扩展能力（Scaling Law），并建立一套统一的、可复现的高质量训练方案。

Method: 提出Toto 2.0训练方案：采用统一训练配方从4M到2.5B参数进行扩展；引入u-muP超参数迁移流程以在不同规模间高效迁移超参数；使用公开时间序列数据构建训练集；设计适合时间序列的架构。

Result: Toto 2.0系列（5个模型）在三个权威基准（BOOM、GIFT-Eval、TIME）上均取得当前最佳结果（SOTA），验证了时间序列基础模型的scaling能力。

Conclusion: 时间序列基础模型可像语言模型一样通过规模扩展持续提升性能，且可通过标准化训练方案实现。开源模型有望推动该领域研究与应用。

Abstract: We show that time series foundation models scale: a single training recipe produces reliable forecast-quality improvements from 4M to 2.5B parameters. We release Toto 2.0, a family of five open-weights forecasting models trained under this recipe. The Toto 2.0 family sets a new state of the art on three forecasting benchmarks: BOOM, our observability benchmark; GIFT-Eval, the standard general-purpose benchmark; and the recent contamination-resistant TIME benchmark. This report describes our experimental results and details the design decisions behind Toto 2.0: its architecture and training recipe, training data, and the u-muP hyperparameter transfer pipeline. All five base checkpoints are released under Apache 2.0.

</details>


### [150] [TrajTok: Adaptive Spatial Tokenization for Trajectory Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20134)
*Zhen Xiong,Shang-Ling Hsu,Cyrus Shahabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TrajTok提出了一种基于多分辨率六边形网格划分和掩码令牌预训练的轨迹编码器，能够在无需任务特定微调的情况下，从原始GPS轨迹中学习可迁移的轨迹表征，在轨迹相似性搜索、分类、ETA预测和旅行时间回归等多项任务上超越多种专用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始GPS轨迹数据具有连续性、噪声和采样不规律等特点，且现有空间分词化方法存在两难：细粒度网格导致稀疏单元和弱嵌入，粗粒度网格则将异质运动模式合并到同一令牌中，难以学到通用的轨迹表征。

Method: 1) 利用GPS点的空间分布学习多分辨率六边形单元划分，将噪声GPS序列转化为离散单元令牌；2) 采用分解式Transformer编码器，包含早期逐模态自注意力模块、交叉注意力融合层和时空旋转位置嵌入(ST-RoPE)；3) 通过掩码令牌建模进行预训练，从部分轨迹观测中同时恢复几何结构和运动模式。

Result: 在Porto数据集上，冻结的TrajTok编码器配合轻量级任务适配器，在轨迹相似性搜索、分类、ETA估计和全旅行时间回归任务中均取得优异性能，超越多种任务专用方法，且同一编码器同时支持几何主导和运动学主导的任务。

Conclusion: 学习到的多分辨率空间分词化结合掩码令牌预训练是构建通用轨迹基础模型的有效方向，TrajTok学到的是可迁移的轨迹结构而非任务特定的捷径。

Abstract: Learning generalizable trajectory representations from raw GPS traces remains difficult because the data is continuous, noisy, and irregularly sampled. Spatial tokenization is also challenging: fine grids yield sparse cells with weak embeddings, while coarse grids merge heterogeneous movement patterns into the same token. We present TrajTok, a trajectory encoder with a simple pretraining recipe for transferable trajectory embeddings. TrajTok first learns a multi-resolution hexagonal cell partition from the spatial distribution of GPS points, converting noisy GPS sequences into discrete cell tokens. To capture both geometry and kinematics, it uses a factorized transformer encoder with early per-modality self-attention blocks, cross-attention fusion layers, and spatiotemporal rotary position embeddings, ST-RoPE, to encode where and when each token occurs. TrajTok is pretrained with masked-token modeling that recovers both geometric structure and kinematic patterns from partial trajectory observations. On the Porto dataset, a frozen TrajTok encoder with lightweight task adapters achieves strong performance across trajectory similarity search, classification, estimated time of arrival, and full travel-time regression, outperforming multiple task-specific methods. The same frozen encoder supports both geometry-dominated and kinematics-dominated tasks, suggesting that TrajTok learns transferable trajectory structure rather than task-specific shortcuts. These results indicate that learned multi-resolution spatial tokenization combined with masked-token pretraining is a promising direction for general-purpose trajectory foundation models.

</details>


### [151] [When Does Model Collapse Occur in Structured Interactive Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20151)
*Yuchen Wu,Kangjie Zhou,Weijie Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成式AI在交互式学习环境中的模型坍缩问题，利用有向图建模模型间的交互模式，推导出模型坍缩发生的充要条件，并在线性回归和一般M估计量上建立了理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI训练中，模型参数不仅使用自然数据更新，还使用其他模型生成的合成数据，这带来了两个挑战：(1)训练数据不再完全来自目标分布，违背经典统计学习假设；(2)模型训练过程因反复接触彼此的合成输出而变得相关。模型坍缩现象引发关注，但已有研究仅关注单模型自训练场景，无法捕捉多模型交互环境下的性能表现。

Method: 使用有向图对模型间的交互模式进行形式化建模，推导模型坍缩发生的充要条件；针对线性回归建立有限样本结果，对一般M估计量建立渐近保证。

Result: 模型坍缩的发生关键取决于交互图的拓扑结构，给出了描述坍缩条件的显式充要条件，并通过大量数值实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 在多模型交互学习环境中，交互图的拓扑结构是决定模型是否发生坍缩的核心因素，这为设计稳健的交互式学习系统提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The proliferation of generative artificial intelligence has given rise to an interactive learning environment, where model parameters are continuously updated using not only data generated by natural processes, but also synthetic outputs produced by other models. This paradigm introduces two major challenges: (1) training data are no longer drawn exclusively from the target population, undermining a core assumption of classical statistical learning, and (2) model training processes become inherently correlated, as models interact with one another through repeated exposure to each other's synthetic outputs in a potentially complex manner. Establishing reliable statistical inference in such structured interactive learning environments therefore remains an important open problem. In particular, there is growing concern about model collapse, a phenomenon in which the performance of generative models progressively degrades as they are trained on synthetic data produced by earlier model generations.
  Prior work on model collapse primarily focuses on a single model trained on its own output, failing to capture model performance in multi-model interactive settings. In this work, we fill this gap by investigating the performance of generative models in an interactive learning environment with general interaction patterns. In particular, we formalize model interactions using directed graphs and show that the occurrence of model collapse depends critically on the topology of the interaction graph. We further derive an explicit necessary and sufficient condition characterizing when model collapse occurs, and establish finite-sample results for linear regression and asymptotic guarantees for general M-estimators. We support our theoretical findings through extensive numerical experiments.

</details>


### [152] [Atoms of Thought: Universal EEG Representation Learning with Microstates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20182)
*Xinyang Tian,Ruitao Liu,Ziyi Ye,Siyang Xue,Xin Wang,Xuesong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于微状态（microstates）的通用EEG表征方法，通过将连续EEG信号聚类为离散微状态序列构建通用分词器，在睡眠分期、情感识别、运动想象等多个下游任务中优于传统时域和频域特征，且具有更好的可解释性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEG表征学习通常将EEG视为多变量时序信号并提取时域或频域特征，缺乏通用且可解释的表征方式。本文旨在探索一种简单有效且通用的EEG微状态表征方法，以提升不同脑电下游任务的表现。

Method: 从大规模医学EEG数据集中对连续EEG信号进行聚类，将其离散化为微状态序列，构建通用微状态分词器（universal microstate tokenizer），并应用于多个下游任务的表征学习。

Result: 在不同的模型和任务（睡眠分期、情感识别、运动想象分类）下，基于微状态的EEG表征学习均优于传统时域和频域特征。微状态具有更强的可解释性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 微状态作为一种简单有效的EEG通用表征，在性能上超越传统特征，并在可解释性和可扩展性方面展现优势，有望推动认知神经科学和临床研究的应用。

Abstract: Learning universal representations from electroencephalogram (EEG) signals is a cutting-edge approach in the field of neuroinformatics and brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). Conventionally, EEG is treated as a multivariate temporal signal, where time- or frequency-domain features are extracted for representation learning. This paper investigates a simple yet effective EEG representation, i.e., microstates. Microstates represent the building blocks of brain activity patterns at a microscopic time scale. We build a universal microstate tokenizer from a large medical EEG dataset by clustering continuous EEG signals into sequences of discrete microstates. The microstate tokenizer is then adopted universally across a series of downstream tasks, including sleep staging, emotion recognition, and motor imagery classification. Experimental results show that EEG representation learning with microstates outperforms traditional time-domain and frequency-domain features under different models and across different tasks. Further analysis shows that microstates offer greater interpretability and scalability, thereby opening up applications in both cognitive neuroscience and clinical research.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [What Do Evolutionary Coding Agents Evolve?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20086)
*Nico Pelleriti,Sree Harsha Nelaturu,Zhanke Zhou,Zongze Li,Max Zimmer,Bo Han,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoTrace数据集和EvoReplay方法，用于分析LLM结合进化搜索时的代码演化过程。发现大多数得分提升仅来自少数编辑类型，且约30%新增代码行是之前删除行的字节级重复引入，表明基准测试收益可能来自多种不同机制而非真正的算法创新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将LLM与进化搜索结合以迭代生成、修改和选择代码，虽在数学发现和算法设计上取得强结果，但根本问题未解：它们究竟在演化什么？最佳分数可能反映多种机制（新算法结构、策略调参、重组已有知识、过拟合），需要审视搜索过程本身而非仅看最终结果。

Method: 构建EvoTrace数据集（涵盖4种进化框架、推理与非推理模型、16项数学与算法任务），开发EvoReplay回放方法：重构高分解背后的局部搜索状态，进行受控干预（调整常数、移除组件、替换模型/提示上下文）。采用LLM-as-judge流水线对每次代码编辑标注9种编辑类型之一，并经盲人重标注验证。

Result: 大多数得分提升仅来自少数编辑类型；发现确定性循环模式：约30%搜索过程中新增的代码行是之前删除行的字节级重复引入，几乎每个运行中都存在。

Conclusion: 进化编码智能体的基准测试收益可能来自性质不同的机制，仅部分对应新的算法结构。EvoTrace支持对进化编码智能体进行超越最终基准分数的诊断性评估。

Abstract: Recent work pairs LLMs with evolutionary search to iteratively generate, modify, and select code using task-specific feedback. These systems have produced strong results in mathematical discovery and algorithm design, yet a fundamental question remains: what do they actually evolve? Progress is typically summarized by the best score a run reaches under a task-specific evaluator, but that score can reflect several different mechanisms: new algorithmic structure, re-tuning an existing strategy, recombining ideas already in the model's internal knowledge, or overfitting to the evaluator. Distinguishing these mechanisms requires inspecting the search process itself, not only its final outcome. We introduce EvoTrace, a dataset of evolutionary coding traces spanning four evolutionary frameworks, reasoning and non-reasoning models, and 16 tasks across mathematics and algorithm design. To analyze these traces, we develop EvoReplay, a replay-based methodology that reconstructs the local search states behind high-scoring solutions and tests controlled interventions, including adjusting constants, removing program components and substituting models or prompting contexts. We annotate every code edit in EvoTrace with one of nine recurring edit types using an LLM-as-judge pipeline validated against blind human re-annotation. Across EvoTrace, most score gains come from a small subset of these edit types. We further find a deterministic cycling pattern: about 30% of code lines added during search are byte-identical re-introductions of previously-deleted lines, present throughout nearly every run. These results show that benchmark gains in evolutionary coding agents can arise from qualitatively different mechanisms, only some of which correspond to new algorithmic structure. EvoTrace enables more diagnostic evaluation of evolutionary coding agents beyond final benchmark scores.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Feasible Plan Generation with Ambiguity-Boundedness in Cross-Model Query Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19197)
*Subhasis Dasgupta,Amarnath Gupta*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可行性约束的Packed Plan Forest (PPF)结构，用于在自然语言到数据库的查询规划中紧凑编码所有可行的中间逻辑计划，并早期剪除不可行计划，具有多项式复杂度和最小开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言数据库接口常因运算符作用域和谓词语义不确定而产生大量歧义的中间逻辑计划（ILP），其中许多因类型不匹配、绑定缺失或引擎特定约束而不可行，亟需一种高效方法来检测和剪除这些不可行计划。

Method: 提出可行性约束用于检测局部不一致性，并引入Packed Plan Forest (PPF)结构——将packed parse forest思想扩展到多模型设置，通过带注释的运算符紧凑编码所有可行ILP，在早期阶段剪除不可行候选。

Result: 形式化证明表明PPF在有界元数和注释词汇下具有多项式大小；实验证实PPF能以最小开销捕获指数数量的ILP。

Conclusion: PPF为跨异构系统的NL-to-DB查询规划建立了可扩展的基础，有效平衡了表达能力和计算效率。

Abstract: Natural language (NL) interfaces to databases broaden access to heterogeneous data but often yield many ambiguous intermediate logical plans (ILPs) due to uncertain operator scope and predicate semantics. Many candidates are infeasible because of type mismatches, missing bindings, or engine-specific constraints. We address this challenge with \emph{feasibility constraints} for detecting local inconsistencies and introduce the Packed Plan Forest (PPF) a polynomially bounded structure that compactly encodes all feasible ILPs while pruning infeasible ones early. Extending packed parse forest ideas to multi-model settings, PPF supports efficient feasibility analysis through annotated operators. Formal results show polynomial size under bounded arity and annotation vocabularies, and experiments confirm that PPFs capture exponentially many ILPs with minimal overhead, establishing a scalable foundation for NL-to-DB query planning across heterogeneous systems

</details>


### [155] [Example-Driven Intent Synthesis for Constrained Data Bundle Retrieval: Focused Text Snippet Extraction and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19246)
*Whanhee Cho,Kuangfei Long,Mahmood Jasim,Matteo Brucato,Alexandra Meliou,Peter J. Haas,Anna Fariha*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Ex2Bundle是一个基于示例驱动的束检索框架，用户通过提供示例束来表达意图，系统自动合成带有聚合约束的包查询，并在约束不可行时进行数据感知的约束松弛，最终返回与用户意图一致的束结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统束检索（如包查询）需要用户通过良好格式的查询表达多维意图，且当查询结果为空时难以处理。现有方法缺乏用户友好的意图表达方式，也无法保证查询的可行性。

Method: 提出Ex2Bundle框架，用户通过示例束指定意图，系统自动从示例中推断聚合约束并合成包查询；当推断的约束在目标数据上不可行时，采用数据感知的约束松弛方法，最小化调整约束边界同时保留用户意图对齐。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验和用户研究表明，Ex2Bundle提高了可用性，即使在目标数据库分布发生变化的情况下也能一致地返回与用户意图对齐的束。

Conclusion: Ex2Bundle通过示例驱动的方式有效解决了束检索中用户意图表达和查询可行性两个关键挑战，为数据库、推荐系统和文本摘要等领域的束检索任务提供了更友好的解决方案。

Abstract: Selecting a bundle of items that collectively satisfies constraints is a fundamental task across databases, recommender systems, and text summarization. Unlike traditional retrieval that returns individual or top-k items, bundle retrieval is inherently combinatorial and, in general, NP-hard. Although package queries can efficiently retrieve bundles given a well-formed query, two key user-centric challenges remain: (1) expressing and tuning multi-dimensional bundle intent through a user-friendly interface, and (2) ensuring feasibility when the query yields empty results. We introduce Ex2Bundle, an Example-driven Bundle retrieval framework that enables users to specify their intent through example bundles and automatically synthesizes package queries that capture the intent implicit in those example bundles via aggregate constraints. Ex2Bundle also addresses a challenge unique to bundle retrieval: when inferred aggregate constraints are infeasible over the target data, our data-aware constraint relaxation minimally adjusts the constraint bounds while preserving alignment with user intent. We instantiate a specific application of focused text snippet extraction by example to demonstrate the efficacy of the Ex2Bundle framework. Extensive experiments over real-world datasets and a user study demonstrate that Ex2Bundle improves usability and consistently returns intent-aligned bundles even under distributional shifts of the target database.

</details>


### [156] [Leveraging I/O Stalls for Efficient Scheduling in ANNS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19335)
*Juncheng Zhang,Yuanming Ren,Yongkun Li,Patrick P. C. Lee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LIOS是一个利用ANNS系统搜索线程的磁盘I/O等待时间（超过40%的CPU时间）来执行索引更新的框架。它将更新任务拆分为可恢复子任务、限制子任务超界期望、并动态调整空闲时间分配，在FreshDiskANN和OdinANN上实现了最高2.68倍的插入加速和2.18倍删除加速，同时搜索延迟接近用户指定目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于磁盘的图索引ANNS系统中，搜索线程超过40%的CPU时间因磁盘I/O而停滞，这些空闲周期对线程级调度不可见但实际可用。现有系统没有利用这些I/O停滞窗口来执行其他有用工作，导致计算资源浪费。

Method: (i) 将每个更新操作拆分为可恢复的子任务，使其大小适合单个I/O停滞窗口；(ii) 限定更新子任务超期的期望值在给定阈值内；(iii) 动态调整用于更新的空闲时间比例，使端到端搜索延迟退化趋近用户指定目标。

Result: 在FreshDiskANN和OdinANN上，插入速度提升最高2.68倍，删除速度提升最高2.18倍，搜索延迟退化保持在用户指定目标附近。

Conclusion: LIOS有效利用搜索线程的I/O停滞窗口执行更新操作，在不显著牺牲搜索延迟的前提下大幅提升ANNS系统的更新吞吐量。

Abstract: Disk-based graph indexes for approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) must serve latency-sensitive queries and throughput-demanding updates concurrently. We observe that over 40% of search-thread CPU time is spent stalling on disk I/O; such idle cycles are invisible to thread-level scheduling yet available for other work. We present LIOS(Leverage I/O Stall), a framework that executes index updates inside search-side I/O stall windows. LIOS introduces three techniques: (i) splitting each update into resumable subtasks small enough to fit within a single stall window; (ii) bounding the expected overrun of update subtasks to a given threshold; and (iii) dynamically adjusting the fraction of idle time devoted to updates to drive end-to-end search latency degradation toward a user-specified target. We integrate LIOS into two update-optimized ANNS systems, FreshDiskANN and OdinANN. LIOS achieves speedups of up to 2.68$\times$ in insertion and 2.18$\times$ in deletion, with search latency degradation maintained near the user-specified target.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Ordered Adjoint Logic (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19112)
*Sophia Roshal,Frank Pfenning*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文通过伴随模态（adjoint modalities）将具有不同细粒度结构性质（如弱化、左收缩、右收缩、左移动、右移动）的逻辑组合起来，推广了Kanovich等人基于子指数（subexponentials）的线性有序逻辑工作，证明了相应的相继式演算可消去切割，并给出了一个结构规则隐式的自然演绎系统，其证明检查可判定，为表达性伴随编程语言或逻辑框架提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Kanovich等人）通过子指数在有序线性逻辑中放松了"有序类型必须线性使用"的约束，但缺乏对更细粒度结构性质（如左右收缩、左右移动）的系统性组合研究，本文旨在通过伴随模态统一处理这些性质。

Method: 利用伴随模态（adjoint modalities）将多个具有不同结构性质（弱化、左收缩、右收缩、左移动、右移动）的逻辑进行组合，构建相继式演算并证明切割消去定理；进一步给出自然演绎系统，其中结构规则隐式编码，并证明其证明检查可判定。

Result: 成功构建了基于伴随模态的广义有序逻辑系统，证明了相继式演算的切割消去性质，以及自然演绎系统中证明检查的可判定性。

Conclusion: 该工作为设计表达性伴随编程语言或逻辑框架提供了坚实的形式化基础，能够灵活控制资源使用的各种结构约束。

Abstract: Ordered logics and type systems have been used in a variety of applications
  including computational linguistics, memory allocation, stream processing,
  logical frameworks, parametricity, and enforcing security protocols. In most
  formulations, ordered types are also linear, requiring each resource to be
  used exactly once. Prior work by Kanovich et al. has investigated calculi
  that relax this constraint through subexponentials within a linear ordered
  logic. We generalize their work by using adjoint modalities to combine logics
  with varying fine-grained structural properties, including weakening, left
  contraction, right contraction, left mobility, and right mobility. We show
  that the resulting sequent calculus admits cut elimination.
  We further provide a natural deduction formulation in which structural rules
  are implicit, and show that proof checking for this system is decidable. This
  makes it a suitable foundation for an expressive adjoint programming language
  or logical framework.

</details>


### [158] [Executable Boundary Contracts for Sound Event Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19632)
*Faruk Alpay,Hamdi Alakkad*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出用于有限声音事件轨迹的可执行边界契约，将时间边界行为定义为框架片段和事件层约束，并通过多个基准测试验证了标准评分与契约坐标之间存在可解释的分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声音事件报告通常将时间边界行为压缩为帧、片段或事件评分，缺乏对边界行为的精确可执行规范，导致测量不透明。

Method: 定义了可执行边界契约：框架片段为可嵌入STL的有界布尔片段；事件层包含声明间隔匹配、持续时间子句、碎片化子句和受限向量评分。

Result: 在Mini LibriSpeech、MAESTRO Real、冻结预训练时序探针和DCASE 2024 Task 4基线上的实验表明，标准评分与契约坐标之间存在可解释的分歧。最强发现是联合活动可能隐藏类型化边界失败。

Conclusion: 边界契约提供了一种新的测量视角，而非新的通用时序逻辑或排行榜挑战；外部DCASE输出提供了类别索引的挑战级别参考。

Abstract: Sound event reports often compress timed boundary behavior into frame, segment, or event scores. This paper defines executable boundary contracts for finite sound event traces. The frame fragment is a bounded Boolean fragment embeddable in STL after grid projection. The event layer adds declared interval matching, duration clauses, fragmentation clauses, and obligation restricted vector scoring. The aim is measurement, not a new general temporal logic and not a challenge leaderboard. The artifact evaluates controlled Mini LibriSpeech seeded scenes, MAESTRO Real soundscapes, frozen pretrained timing probes, and an official DCASE 2024 Task 4 baseline track. Across these tracks, standard scores and contract coordinates disagree in interpretable ways. The strongest real corpus finding is that union activity can hide typed boundary failure, while external DCASE outputs provide a class indexed challenge level reference. Code, generated tables, manifests, and Lean checks for the finite frame core are supplied as ancillary material.

</details>


### [159] [Completeness of Synthesis under Realizability Assumptions using Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19683)
*Márton Hajdu,Petra Hozzová,Laura Kovács,Eva Maria Wagner*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于超演算的改进演算，用于合成无递归程序，支持不可计算符号，并证明其可靠性和完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自动定理证明与程序合成结合，可以自动构建被证明正确的程序，从而提高软件可靠性。现有超演算在合成无递归程序时存在失败情况，需要改进。

Method: 扩展基于超演算的演算以支持无递归程序合成，允许使用不可计算符号进行推理；针对演算失败的情况进行改进和精炼。

Result: 提出了一个改进的演算，并证明了其可靠性；同时证明了如果存在至少一个满足规约的可计算程序，改进的演算能够找到它（完备性）。

Conclusion: 改进后的超演算能够可靠且完备地合成无递归程序，为程序合成领域提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Program synthesis is the task of automatically deriving a program that has been specified by a user in advance. Combining automated theorem proving with program synthesis enables the automated construction of proven-to-be-correct programs, thereby ensuring software reliability. In this paper, we consider the superposition-based calculus extended to support synthesis of recursion-free programs allowing reasoning with uncomputable symbols. We present cases where the calculus fails and refine it to solve them. We prove that the refined calculus is sound. Finally, we also prove completeness in the following sense: if at least one computable program satisfying the given specification exists, we show that the modified calculus finds one.

</details>


### [160] [Guarded Negation Transitive Closure Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.15303)
*Diego Figueira,Santiago Figueira,Yoshiki Nakamura*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了带保护否定的传递闭包逻辑（GNTC）的可满足性与模型检测问题，证明了GNTC的可满足性问题是2ExpTime完全的，模型检测问题的组合复杂度是P^{NP[O(log² n)]}-完全的。同时，该结果也解决了UNTC和UNFO^{reg}的复杂度开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究GNTC（带保护否定的传递闭包逻辑）的可满足性与模型检测问题的计算复杂度，填补UNTC和UNFO^{reg}复杂度分析中的空白。

Method: (i) 将GNTC的可满足性问题多项式时间归约到UNTC的可满足性问题；(ii) 将UNTC的可满足性问题指数时间直接归约到2-向交替奇偶树自动机的非空性问题。

Result: GNTC可满足性问题是2ExpTime完全的；GNTC模型检测问题的组合复杂度是P^{NP[O(log² n)]}-完全的。

Conclusion: 建立了GNTC的精确复杂度界限，同时推论得出UNTC和UNFO^{reg}的复杂度也是P^{NP[O(log² n)]}-完全的，解决了之前工作中遗留的开放问题。

Abstract: We study the guarded negation fragment of transitive closure logic (GNTC). We show that the satisfiability problem for GNTC is 2ExpTime-complete, by establishing the following reductions: (i) a polynomial-time reduction from the satisfiability problem for GNTC to the satisfiability problem for the unary negation fragment UNTC of GNTC, and (ii) a direct exponential-time reduction from the satisfiability problem for UNTC to the non-emptiness problem for 2-way alternating parity tree automata. Furthermore, we show that the model checking problem for GNTC is $\mathsf{P}^{\mathsf{NP}[\mathcal{O}(\log^2 n)]}$-complete in combined complexity. Our result implies $\mathsf{P}^{\mathsf{NP}[\mathcal{O}(\log^2 n)]}$-completeness for both UNTC and $\mathrm{UNFO}^{\mathrm{reg}}$, which were left open in previous works.

</details>


### [161] [Satisfiability for Knowing How over Linear Plans is NP-complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19819)
*Carlos Areces,Pablo Barceló,Valentin Cassano,Pablo F. Castro,Stéphane Demri,Raul Fervari*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究一种基于线性规划的知道如何（knowing-how）模态逻辑的可满足性问题，证明了其NP完全性，并通过归约到S5模态逻辑来完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于知道如何模态逻辑的可满足性问题的复杂度上界不够精确，本文旨在改进此前已知的复杂度界。

Method: 通过将知道如何公式归约到模态逻辑S5的可满足性问题来证明其NP完全性。

Result: 证明了知道如何模态逻辑的可满足性问题是NP完全的，相比此前已知的复杂度界有了改进。

Conclusion: 知道如何模态逻辑的可满足性问题具有NP完全性，且归约到S5模态逻辑的方法可作为处理知识表示中一系列问题的有效工具。

Abstract: We study the satisfiability problem for a modal logic expressing knowing-how assertions, which captures an agent's ability to achieve a given goal under the standard semantics based on linear plans. Our main result shows that satisfiability of knowing-how formulas is NP-complete, improving previously known complexity bounds. The proof proceeds via a translation into modal logic S5, an instrumental tool for addressing a variety of problems in knowledge representation.

</details>


### [162] [Automating proof search when equality is a logical connective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20054)
*Kaustuv Chaudhuri,Arunava Gantait,Dale Miller*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将语法等式作为逻辑联结词进行证明搜索的程序，通过扩展合一处理量词交替和正负出现等式，在轻量级逻辑框架中实现了对Abella证明助手所使用等式理论的支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语法等式作为逻辑联结词虽然提供了优雅的项同一性处理方法，并能推导皮亚诺公理等核心数学原理，但将其集成到自动证明搜索中仍然具有挑战性。现有逻辑框架如λProlog和LF缺乏对此类等式的直接支持。

Method: 提出一种扩展合一的证明搜索程序，处理量词交替以及同时出现在正负出现位置的等式，构建一个轻量级逻辑框架以支持在多种一阶相继式演算中直接使用该类等式进行证明搜索。

Result: 该程序能够在一阶相继式演算范围内实现合一感知的证明搜索，填补了现有逻辑框架对语法等式支持的空缺。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法使轻量级逻辑框架能够直接利用语法等式作为逻辑联结词进行自动证明搜索，弥合了Abella等工具中理论优势与实践自动化之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Treating syntactic equality as a logical connective -- governed by left- and right-introduction rules within the sequent calculus -- offers an elegant and powerful approach to term identity. This treatment of equality allows for the derivation of core mathematical principles, such as Peano's axioms (excluding induction), and serves as a foundation for the Abella interactive proof assistant. However, integrating this equality into automated proof search remains challenging. We present a proof search procedure that extends unification to handle the complexities of quantifier alternation and equations that occur in both positive and negative occurrences. While established logical frameworks such as $λ$Prolog and LF lack direct support for this kind of equality, our procedure enables a lightweight logical framework that addresses this gap. Our system enables unification-aware proof search across a diverse range of first-order sequent calculi that can directly use this form of equality.

</details>


### [163] [Long-term Power Grid Planning via Answer Set Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20172)
*Antonio Ielo,Francesco Doria,Sandra Castellanos-Paez,Marco Maratea,Francesco Percassi,Mauro Vallati*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于回答集编程(ASP)的长期电网规划自动化与优化方法，通过简洁编码拓扑和组合不变量，在合成和真实数据上验证了其表达能力和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期电网规划需要满足可持续性目标、需求模式和城市化趋势，同时保持供电连续性和服务质量，但现有规划语言难以表达所需的拓扑和组合不变量约束。

Method: 利用回答集编程(ASP)对电网规划的拓扑和组合不变量进行优雅简洁的编码，实现长期电网规划的自动化与优化。

Result: 在合成数据和真实电网数据上的实验验证了ASP方法的表达能力和有效性。

Conclusion: ASP是一种适合长期电网规划任务的形式化方法，能够有效表达复杂约束并实现自动化优化。

Abstract: The Power grid is a critical infrastructure underpinning all aspects of modern society and its services. Maintaining its effectiveness requires continuous adaptations. In particular, addressing sustainability targets, demand patterns, and urbanisation trends requires implementing changes to the network. Actual developments can potentially span over a decade, with supply continuity and service quality that must be preserved throughout by ensuring conformance to several topological and combinatorial invariants. Long-term power grid planning deals with the above process, and although planning languages could be a natural choice, the kind of properties and invariants needed are cumbersome to express in such languages; on the contrary, they can be elegantly and succinctly encoded in Answer Set Programming (ASP). In this paper, we propose the first approach to automate and optimise the long-term power grid planning process using ASP. Experimental evaluations conducted on synthetic and real-world grid data confirm the expressive power of the proposed ASP-based approach and demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Prompt Optimization for LLM Code Generation via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19102)
*Ali Mohammadi Esfahani,Nafiseh Kahani,Samuel A. Ajila*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个基于强化学习的提示词优化框架，通过PPO智能体结合混合动作空间和测试驱动奖励，显著提升了LLM在代码生成任务中的功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM从自然语言生成代码的性能对提示词形式高度敏感，现有方法缺乏系统性的提示词优化机制。

Method: 将提示词优化建模为序列决策问题，使用PPO智能体迭代优化，采用混合动作空间（直接生成、遗传词法变异、语义重写），并基于单元测试反馈设计形状奖励。

Result: 在MBPP+上对CodeT5+/CodeLLaMA/DeepSeek-Coder实现Strict Pass@1分别为57.58%/64.80%/85.50%，Soft-Pass@1达67.90%/73.10%/88.20%，超越EPiC、Reflexion和Random-Hybrid基线。

Conclusion: 强化学习结合测试驱动的形状奖励能有效提升LLM代码生成的功能正确性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate code from natural language, but their performance is highly sensitive to prompt formulation. We propose a reinforcement-learning-based framework that models prompt refinement as a sequential decision-making problem. A Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) agent iteratively improves prompts using a hybrid action space that combines direct generation, genetic lexical mutation and semantic rewriting, guided by shaped rewards derived from unit-test feedback. We evaluate the framework on MBPP+, HumanEval+, and APPS using CodeT5+, CodeLLaMA, and DeepSeek-Coder as frozen code generators. On the 500-task MBPP+ test set, the PPO agent achieves strict Pass@1 scores of 57.58%, 64.80%, and 85.50%, respectively, outperforming EPiC, Reflexion, and Random-Hybrid. Soft-Pass@1 reaches 67.90%, 73.10%, and 88.20%, respectively. Similar improvements are observed on HumanEval+ and APPS across all backbone models. The results demonstrate that reinforcement learning with shaped test-driven rewards improves functional correctness in LLM-based code generation.

</details>


### [165] [When Web Apps Heal Themselves: A MAPE-K Based Approach to Fault Tolerance and Adaptive Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19261)
*Sales Aribe,Rov Japheth Oracion*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于MAPE-K模型和AutoFix机制的模块化自愈框架，通过故障注入实验验证，在20种运行时故障场景下实现了90.7%的检测F1分数和93.2%的恢复成功率，平均恢复时间3.92秒，TTR降低56.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Web应用系统复杂性和动态运行环境日益增加，确保其可靠性和弹性仍然是一个关键挑战。

Method: 采用设计与发展研究（DDR）方法，基于MAPE-K模型构建模块化自愈框架，集成AutoFix自适应故障恢复机制，通过控制故障注入实验在20种运行时故障场景下进行评估。

Result: 故障检测F1分数90.7%，恢复成功率93.2%，AutoFix模块将平均恢复时间降至3.92秒（TTR降低56.2%），故障条件下吞吐量维持在88%-95%，响应时间仅增加3.1%，迭代反馈机制使恢复效率提升18.6%。

Conclusion: 该框架为增强Web应用容错能力提供了一种实用且可扩展的反馈驱动自适应方法，当前实现依赖预定义恢复策略，但学习导向的反馈为未来更自主的自愈系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Ensuring the reliability and resilience of modern web applications remains a critical challenge due to increasing system complexity and dynamic runtime environments. This study proposes a modular self-healing framework based on the monitor-analyze-plan-execute over a shared knowledge base (MAPE-K) model, integrated with an AutoFix-inspired mechanism for adaptive fault recovery. Using a design and development research (DDR) approach, the system was implemented and evaluated through controlled fault injection experiments across twenty runtime failure scenarios, including service crashes, memory leaks, and database disconnections. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieved a mean fault detection F1-score of 90.7% and a recovery success rate of 93.2%. The AutoFix module reduced the average time-to-recovery (TTR) by 56.2%, achieving an average recovery time of 3.92 seconds. System throughput was maintained between 88% and 95% during fault conditions, with only a 3.1% increase in response time. Additionally, iterative feedback mechanisms improved recovery efficiency by 18.6% over multiple cycles. These findings indicate that the proposed framework provides a practical and extensible approach to enhancing fault tolerance in web applications through feedback-driven adaptation. While the current implementation relies on predefined recovery strategies, the integration of learning-oriented feedback establishes a foundation for future development of more autonomous self-healing systems.

</details>


### [166] [Restructure This: Using AI to Restructure Onboarding Documents to Reduce Cognitive Overload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19174)
*Zixuan Feng,Prashant Tandan,Igor Steinmacher,Marco Aurelio Gerosa,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出基于认知理论的多媒体学习（CTML）策略，利用GenAI管道将开源软件（OSS）文档重构为任务导向、多模态形式，通过VisDoc原型验证了该方法在降低认知负荷、提升任务成功率和可用性方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OSS onboarding文档常呈现密集、结构不一致且碎片化的问题，导致新手上手困难、认知过载，进而引发挫败感、错误甚至放弃。现有文档缺乏基于认知理论的系统化重构方法。

Method: 基于CTML策略，设计GenAI驱动的文档重构管道，通过VisDoc原型实现：将文档分割为任务单元、推断工作流、去除冗余、生成多模态解释。采用专家评估（N=4）和组间实验（N=14）验证效果。

Result: 专家评估确认了VisDoc的完整性、准确性和可采纳性；组间实验表明，使用VisDoc的新手任务成功率更高，认知负荷显著降低，感知可用性更高。

Conclusion: 基于认知理论的文档重构能有效降低认知负荷，提升可用性和任务性能。贡献包括CTML指导的onboarding挑战分析、GenAI文档重构管道以及实证证据。

Abstract: Onboarding documentation is critical for attracting and retaining newcomers in open source software (OSS). However, it is often presented as dense, inconsistently structured, and fragmented presentations that are difficult to understand, which creates cognitive overload leading to frustration, errors, and abandonment. Here, we investigate how Cognitive Theory of Multimedia Learning (CTML) strategies can be used to restructure OSS documentation. We use a GenAI-based pipeline to operationalize these strategies to restructure OSS documentation through our prototype VisDoc. VisDoc segments documentation into task-based units, infers workflows, removes redundancy, and generates multimodal explanations. An expert evaluation (N=4) affirmed VisDoc's completeness, accuracy, and adoptability; A between-subjects evaluation (N=14) with newcomers found that VisDoc participants achieved higher task success, had significantly lower cognitive load, and perceived higher usability. The contributions of this work include a CTML-grounded analysis of onboarding challenges, a GenAI-based documentation restructuring pipeline, and empirical evidence that cognitively informed documentation restructuring reduces cognitive load and improves usability and task performance in OSS.

</details>


### [167] [DRReduce: Enhancing Syntax-Guided Program Reduction with Dependency Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19412)
*Qiong Feng,Xiaotian Ma,Yongqiang Tian,Wei Song,Peng Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DRReduce框架，通过在语法无关的程序缩减中引入轻量级语义层（依赖重建），修复因删除节点而破坏的程序依赖，显著提升缩减效果与效率。在C和Java上相比现有最优方法，缩减率提升高达51.9%，查询调用减少80.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序缩减技术存在两难：语言专用工具（如CReduce）效果好但局限于单一语言；语言无关工具（如Perses）依赖语法引导，但孤立删除节点或子树常破坏语义一致性，导致属性检查器拒绝删除，迫使缩减器回溯，限制了缩减效果和效率。

Method: DRReduce构建语义依赖图，执行语义一致的删除操作并通过依赖重建修复被破坏的程序依赖，再将精简后的程序委托给语法引导缩减器进一步最小化。

Result: 相比Perses、WDD、CDD，平均缩减率分别提升51.9%、14.9%、19.8%；相比CReduce和Latra，结果相当但效率分别提升3.3倍和1.2倍；消融实验显示依赖重建使查询调用减少80.2%，缩减时间减少58.7%，最终token数减少55.1%。

Conclusion: DRReduce成功弥合了语言无关与语言专用缩减器之间的差距，通过轻量级语义增强实现了跨语言的高效、高质量程序缩减。

Abstract: Program reduction is a technique for simplifying large, failure-inducing programs into minimal reproducible test cases. Language-specific tools such as CReduce achieve strong performance by leveraging deep semantic knowledge of C/C++, but are tightly coupled to a single language family. Language-agnostic reducers such as Perses address this by applying syntax-guided search across any grammar, yet share a fundamental limitation: deleting a node or subtree in isolation often breaks semantic coherence causing the property checker to reject the deletion and forcing the reducer to backtrack, limiting overall reduction effectiveness and efficiency. In this paper, we propose DRReduce, a framework that bridges this gap by augmenting language-agnostic syntactic reduction with a lightweight semantic layer: dependency reconstruction, which repairs program dependencies broken by a deletion in order to preserve the semantic validity of intermediate programs and increase the acceptance rate of the property checker. DRReduce constructs a semantic dependency graph from the input program, performs semantically coherent deletions with dependency reconstruction, and delegates further minimization to a syntax-guided reducer. We implement DRReduce for C and Java and evaluate it on real-world bug-triggering programs. Compared to SOTA syntax-guided reducers, DRReduce achieves average size reductions of 51.9%, 14.9%, and 19.8% over Perses, WDD, and CDD respectively, while completing reduction faster on the majority of programs. Compared to language-specific tools, DRReduce achieves results comparable to CReduce and Latra without any language-specific transformation rules, at 3.3x and 1.2x higher efficiency than CReduce and Latra on average, respectively. An ablation study confirms that dependency reconstruction reduces query invocations by 80.2%, reduction time by 58.7%, and final token count by over 55.1%.

</details>


### [168] [Supporting System Testing with a Multi-Agent LLM-based Framework for Knowledge Graph Extraction: A Case Study with Ethernet Switch Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19180)
*Rongqi Pan,Mahboubeh Dadkhah,Jean Baptiste Minani,Hussein Al Osman,Lionel Briand,Haiwei Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体LLM的框架，利用迭代的提取-评估-改进循环，从半结构化的以太网交换机配置手册中自动生成知识图谱，以支持交换机测试自动化。在50个真实手册上的实验表明，该方法在提取正确性（平均0.97-0.99）、人机一致性（Cohen's kappa ≥ 0.72）和下游测试用例生成方面均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术文档（如以太网交换机配置手册）包含丰富的领域知识，可用于自动化下游任务（如系统测试），但其半结构化格式、隐式步骤属性和复杂章节依赖关系使其难以直接用于测试自动化。

Method: 提出一个多智能体LLM框架，包含三个核心组件：1）细粒度的知识图谱模式（KG schema）；2）迭代式Extract-Evaluate-Improve循环；3）多智能体协作机制，用于从ESCM中提取、评估和改进知识图谱。

Result: 在50个真实ESCM上，平均正确性分数达0.97-0.99；EEI循环进一步提升了挑战性手册的提取质量；LLM判断与人工评估的Cohen's kappa ≥ 0.72；工业测试人员反馈表明生成的知识图谱能够支持生成有用且正确的测试用例规范。

Conclusion: 所提出的通用框架能够有效从半结构化工业技术文档中提取结构化知识，且生成的知识图谱可成功支持下游测试自动化任务，具有良好的适应性和实用性。

Abstract: Technical documents contain rich domain knowledge for automating downstream tasks such as system testing. While this paper focuses on Ethernet switch configuration manuals (ESCMs), we propose a general framework that can be adapted to different industrial contexts. ESCMs provide valuable domain knowledge for Ethernet switch testing, but their semi-structured format, implicit step attributes, and complex section dependencies make them difficult to directly leverage for test automation. To address this, we generate knowledge graphs (KGs) that capture configuration knowledge from ESCM in a structured form. We propose a multi-agent LLM-based framework that extracts, evaluates, and improves KGs from ESCMs using a fine-grained KG schema and an iterative Extract-Evaluate-Improve (EEI) loop. Our evaluation on 50 real-world ESCMs shows that our framework achieves high extraction correctness using the original prompts, with average correctness scores ranging from 0.97 to 0.99 across three extraction tasks. For challenging ESCMs, the EEI loop further improves correctness through manual-specific prompt refinement. Moreover, the LLM judgments and human evaluations show substantial agreement, with Cohen's kappa of at least 0.72 across all extraction tasks. Finally, feedback from industry testers indicates that the generated KGs can support the generation of useful and correct test case specifications (TCSs) for downstream testing.

</details>


### [169] [MuMuTestUp: Mutation-based Multi-Agent Test Case Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19265)
*Dawei Tian,Jiakun Liu,Yun Peng,Yichen Zhang,Jianlei Chi,Jun Sun,Xiaohong Su*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出MuMuTestUp，一个基于突变引导的多智能体框架，通过突变分析、覆盖分析和语义检索三个专门智能体来改进自动化测试更新，解决现有方法忽视断言充分性、依赖粗粒度覆盖和使用精确匹配检索的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动测试更新方法存在三个局限：(1)忽视测试断言充分性，削弱故障检测能力；(2)依赖粗粒度行覆盖而非具体的未覆盖行/分支；(3)使用精确匹配检索，无法处理LLM幻觉产生的查询。这些问题导致测试用例虽然可执行且覆盖率高，但缺陷检测能力不足。

Method: 提出MuMuTestUp，包含三个专门智能体：突变分析智能体（通过存活突变体加强断言）、覆盖分析智能体（为未覆盖行/分支生成针对性修复指令）、语义检索智能体（通过语义相似性搜索处理幻觉）。同时构建了PRBENCH数据集（571个样本，来自10个开源Java项目的PR级别数据集）。

Result: 在开源（DeepSeek-V3.2）和闭源（GPT-4.1）LLM上，与最先进基线进行对比评估。

Conclusion: MuMuTestUp通过突变引导的多智能体协同框架，有效解决了现有方法的三个关键局限，提升了测试用例的缺陷检测能力和覆盖率质量。

Abstract: Modern software systems evolve rapidly under CI/CD practices, where tests are critical for quality. However, substantial code changes often render existing test cases obsolete, causing pipeline disruptions, reduced productivity, and compromised quality. Recent automatic test update approaches leverage LLMs to refine test cases via execution feedback and exact-matching context retrieval, prioritizing executability and line coverage but suffering three limitations: (1) neglecting test assertion adequacy, weakening fault detection; (2) relying on coarse line coverage instead of specific uncovered lines/branches; (3) using exact-matching retrieval, which fails for LLM hallucinated queries. To address these, we propose MuMuTestUp, a mutation-guided multi-agent framework with three specialized agents: Mutation Analysis (strengthens assertions via surviving mutants), Coverage Analysis (generates targeted repair instructions for uncovered lines/branches), and Semantic Retrieval (handles hallucinations via semantic-similarity search). We also construct PRBENCH, a 571-sample pull-request-level dataset from 10 open-source Java projects (validated for cross-commit update scenarios). Evaluations against state-of-the-art baselines use both open-source (Deepseek-V3.2) and closed-source (GPT-4.1) LLMs.

</details>


### [170] [OpenHealth Lake: Designing and testing a data lakehouse platform for health applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19922)
*Danilo Silva,Monika Moir,Cheryl Baxter,Tulio de Oliveira,Joicymara Xavier,Marcel Dunaiski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenHealth Lake，一个基于数据湖仓架构、数据联邦和FAIR原则的数据管理原型平台，旨在解决生物信息学和健康科学领域中的复杂数据管理挑战，通过用户研究验证了其可用性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物信息学和健康科学领域持续产生大量异构数据集，但在全球健康合作项目中缺乏统一的数据管理平台，导致数据交换和治理效率低下。

Method: 基于数据湖仓架构、数据联邦和FAIR原则，采用开源工具构建原型平台，包含用户友好网站、开放API、Python和R包，并通过包含不同技术背景参与者的用户研究评估可用性。

Result: 用户研究表明该数据管理原型既可用又有用，展示了湖仓系统的适应性、可扩展性和可重复性。

Conclusion: OpenHealth Lake设计灵活且具有互补性，允许组织根据自身需求和资源定制数据管理系统，支持云端或自托管存储选择。

Abstract: Data management can be a complex challenge in fields such as bioinformatics and health sciences, which continuously generate extensive heterogeneous datasets. In the context of collaborative global health initiatives, secure storage and sharing of data are crucial to support impactful research. However, the absence of a unified data management platform complicates efficient data exchange and governance within these initiatives. In this paper, we introduce the design process of OpenHealth Lake, a data management prototype platform based on a data lakehouse architecture, data federation, and the FAIR principles. The platform is designed using open-source tools, guided by system requirements identified in previously published studies and complemented by insights from the existing literature. The current prototype platform comprises a user-friendly website, an open API, Python and R packages, allowing users to interact with the platform in multiple ways. Through a user study that included participants with varying technical backgrounds, we showed that our proposed data management prototype is both usable and useful. Our prototype design showcases the adaptability, scalability, and reproducibility of a lakehouse system that can be used by any organisation. It is designed as a flexible and complementary approach that allows organisations to customise data management systems to their specific requirements and resources, including cloud-based or self-hosted storage choices.

</details>


### [171] [On-the-Fly Input Adaptation for Reliable Code Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19365)
*Ravishka Rathnasuriya,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于即时输入自适应的策略，通过输入验证和输入转换两个阶段，在不修改模型参数或重新训练的情况下，减少代码语言模型在代码理解任务中的错误预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码语言模型即使使用最新数据训练仍存在明显错误预测，而现有解决方案（重训练、修改架构、重新设计提示）计算成本高、泛化能力差、调优不稳定。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：1) 输入验证——检测可能导致错误预测的输入；2) 输入适应——通过保留语法和语义的变换操作，使输入更好地与模型已学习的行为对齐。

Result: 该策略能减少多种代码理解任务中的错误预测，提升模型性能，且无需重训练。

Conclusion: 作为一种可扩展且资源高效的解决方案，该框架在可靠性要求高的软件工程应用中具有显著前景。

Abstract: Code language models (CLMs) play a central role in software engineering across both generation and classification tasks. However, these models still exhibit notable mispredictions in real-world applications, even when trained on up-to-date data. Existing solutions address this by retraining the model, modifying its architecture, or re-engineering prompts. These approaches incur high computational cost requiring substantial effort in data labeling, model updates, and redeployment, and often suffer from poor generalization across tasks and tuning instability across models. This work proposes an alternative strategy based on on-the-fly input adaptation, which improves model behavior without altering its parameters or requiring additional supervision. The method consists of two stages: input validation, which detects inputs likely to cause mispredictions, and input adaptation, which transforms them using syntax- and semantics-preserving operations to better align with the model's learned behavior. This dual strategy reduces mispredictions across diverse code understanding tasks, boosting model performance without necessitating retraining. As a scalable and resource-efficient solution, this framework holds significant promise for high-stakes applications in software engineering where reliability is critical.

</details>


### [172] [Operationalising Artificial Intelligence Bills of Materials (AIBOMs) for Verifiable AI Provenance and Lifecycle Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19755)
*Petar Radanliev,Omar Santos,Carsten Maple,Kay Atefi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展CycloneDX标准的人工智能物料清单（AIBOM）架构，通过结构化模式工程、密码学验证和智能体驱动自动化，实现了AI软件供应链的可验证来源追踪和可复现性保障。实验表明，该方法在容器化分析工作流中达到了98.7%的可复现性保真度和96.2%的漏洞匹配精度，并减少了63%的人工监督需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统日益依赖复杂的多层软件供应链，这给可复现性、透明性和安全保证带来了挑战。现有标准缺乏对AI特定来源、模型谱系和披露元数据的捕获能力，亟需一种形式化、可验证的来源保证框架。

Method: 基于CycloneDX标准扩展AIBOM模式，集成密码学验证和智能体驱动自动化；构建自主AI管线，实现持续环境检查、漏洞丰富化和基于机器可验证来源链的可复现性审计。

Result: 98.7%的可复现性保真度、96.2%的漏洞匹配精度、63%的人工监督减少。

Conclusion: 自动化来源保证和可复现AI生命周期验证是可行的；AIBOM框架为AI系统安全、来源完整性及国际信息安全标准合规提供了可泛化的方法论基础。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly dependent on complex, multi-layered software supply chains that introduce challenges for reproducibility, transparency, and security assurance. This study presents an Artificial Intelligence Bill of Materials (AIBOM) schema extending the CycloneDX standard to capture AI-specific provenance, model lineage, and disclosure metadata. The framework provides a formalised approach to verifiable software provenance through structured schema engineering, cryptographic validation, and agent-driven automation. An autonomous AI pipeline is developed to perform continuous environment inspection, vulnerability enrichment, and reproducibility auditing using machine-verifiable provenance chains. Empirical evaluation demonstrates 98.7% reproducibility fidelity, 96.2% vulnerability match precision, and a 63% reduction in manual oversight across containerised analytic workflows. These results confirm the feasibility of automated provenance assurance and reproducible AI lifecycle validation. The AIBOM framework advances the scientific foundations of software supply chain transparency and AI reproducibility engineering, offering a generalisable methodology for securing AI systems, strengthening provenance integrity, and supporting compliance with international information security standards.

</details>


### [173] [When to Answer and When to Defer: A Decision Framework for Reliable Code Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19369)
*Ravishka Rathnasuriya,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，将不确定性估计、模型校准和基于工具的弃权处理集成到代码语言模型中，使其能够可靠估计正确性概率、在不确定性下弃权，并通过轻量级程序分析处理弃权案例，从而支持风险感知和覆盖率可控的部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码语言模型虽然广泛应用，但常产生过度自信的错误预测和不足自信的正确预测，现有校准和不确定性估计方法主要针对自然语言任务，不适用于代码领域，且多数方法将不确定性视为被动指标而非可操作的信号。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，包含三个组件：不确定性估计、模型校准和基于工具的弃权处理。通过集成这些组件，模型能够分配可靠的正确性概率，在不确定性下弃权，并调用轻量级程序分析程序处理弃权案例。

Result: 该框架支持风险感知和覆盖率可控的代码模型部署，适用于分类和生成两种场景，解决了现有方法在预测排序和选择性预测方面的不足。

Conclusion: 通过将不确定性估计、模型校准和工具化弃权处理整合到统一的部署导向工作流中，该框架提升了代码语言模型在实际部署中的可靠性。

Abstract: Code language models are increasingly adopted for both understanding and generative tasks. Despite their success, these models frequently produce overconfident incorrect predictions and underconfident correct predictions, undermining their reliability in deployment. Practical deployment demands three capabilities: accurately estimating the likelihood of correctness, abstaining on uncertain predictions, and invoking external mechanisms to validate or repair abstained outputs. Existing calibration and uncertainty estimation methods, primarily developed for natural language tasks, do not readily transfer to code. Notably, post-hoc calibration techniques often reduce probability misalignment but fail to improve the ranking of predictions by correctness likelihood-a requirement for selective prediction under partial coverage. Furthermore, most approaches treat uncertainty as a passive indicator rather than an actionable signal. This work introduces a unified framework that integrates uncertainty estimation, model calibration, and tool-based abstention handling for code models. The proposed design enables models to assign reliable correctness probabilities, abstain under uncertainty, and invoke lightweight program analysis procedures to process abstained cases. By combining these components within a single deployment-oriented workflow, this framework supports risk-aware, coverage-controlled use of code models across both classification and generation settings.

</details>


### [174] [A Case for Agentic Tuning: From Documentation to Action in PostgreSQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19988)
*Hongyu Lin,Mingyu Li,Weichen Zhang,Yihang Lou,Mingjie Xing,Yanjun Wu,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出从静态文档转向动态执行的方法，通过PerfEvolve将专家调优方法论转化为LLM智能体可执行的技能，实现版本一致性验证、工作负载特定分析与多参数联合优化，在PostgreSQL上TPC-C和TPC-H基准测试中相比文档驱动调优基线提升最高35.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文档驱动调优存在三大缺陷：文档随软件演进而过时、无法适应异构工作负载、忽略参数间依赖关系。根本原因在于文档只保存专家结论，丢失了推理过程。

Method: 提出PerfEvolve框架，将专家调优方法论转化为LLM智能体可执行的技能，包括版本一致性验证、工作负载特定性能剖析和多参数联合优化三项核心能力。

Result: 在PostgreSQL数据库上，以TPC-C和TPC-H为基准，PerfEvolve相比最先进的文档驱动调优基线最高提升35.2%的性能。

Conclusion: 从静态文档转向动态执行是系统调优的有效范式转变，通过LLM智能体承载专家推理过程，可解决文档驱动方法的固有问题。

Abstract: Documentation has long guided computer system tuning by distilling expert knowledge into per-parameter recommendations. Yet such guides capture only what experts conclude, discarding how they reason. This fundamental gap manifests in three concrete deficiencies: documentation grows stale as software evolves, fails under heterogeneous workloads, and ignores inter-parameter dependencies.
  We propose shifting from static documentation to dynamic action for system tuning. We introduce PerfEvolve, which translates expert tuning methodologies into executable skills that equip LLM-based agents to perform version-consistency verification, workload-specific profiling, and multi-parameter joint optimization. Evaluated on PostgreSQL under TPC-C and TPC-H benchmarks, PerfEvolve outperforms state-of-the-art documentation-driven tuning baselines by up to 35.2%. The tool is available at https://github.com/ISCAS-OSLab/PerfEvolve.

</details>


### [175] [Characterizing Real-World Bugs in Tile Programs for Automated Bug Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19652)
*Ravishka Rathnasuriya,Zihe Song,Nidhi Majoju,Aaryaa Moharir,Tingxi Li,Wei Yang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了基于Tile的编程框架中代码生成bug，从GitHub收集401个bug报告并分析301个代码生成bug，分类了根本原因、症状、输入模式、触发测试预言及修复策略，为构建针对Tile编译器的调试、测试和修复工具提供基础洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tile编程框架因其多阶段编译流水线引入了与输入形状、数据类型和后端目标紧密耦合的代码生成bug，且表现为静默正确性或性能问题，现有编译器测试工具难以检测，而领域特定语言的编程惯例又使根因定位和修复变得困难。

Method: 从GitHub收集401个bug报告，筛选出301个Tile程序代码生成bug，从根本原因、症状、输入模式、触发测试预言及修复策略五个维度进行系统分类和分析。

Result: 识别并分类了301个Tile程序代码生成bug，揭示了其根本原因、症状表现、触发模式及修复策略的分布规律。

Conclusion: 该研究为构建专门针对Tile编译器基础设施的调试、测试和修复工具提供了基础性洞见。

Abstract: Tile-based programming frameworks are increasingly adopted to write high-performance GPU kernels in domains such as deep learning and scientific computing. While these frameworks enhance productivity and hardware utilization, their multi-stage compilation pipelines introduce distinct code generation bugs that are tightly coupled to input shapes, data types, and backend targets. These bugs often manifest as silent correctness or performance issues, making them difficult to detect using existing compiler testing tools. Additionally, the unique programming conventions of tile domain-specific languages complicate root cause identification, while fixing such bugs demands specialized knowledge of tile abstractions and compilation pipelines. Despite the growing adoption of tile-based systems, their code generation bugs remain largely unexplored. This paper presents the first systematic study of tile-program code generation bugs. We curate 401 bug reports from GitHub and identify 301 tile-program codegen bugs for analysis, categorizing the root causes, symptoms, input patterns, test oracles that trigger these bugs, and the strategies used to fix bugs. Our study provides foundational insights for building debugging, testing, and repair tools tailored to tile-based compiler infrastructures.

</details>


### [176] [CriterAlign: Criterion-Centric Rationale Alignment for Code Preference Judging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19665)
*Zhenyu Li,Aleksandar Cvejic,Zehui Chen,Peter Wonka*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CriterAlign框架，将基于量规的评判从逐点评分改造为面向成对偏好预测的逐准则直接评判，结合平局驱动的准则细化、交换一致性过滤和成对综合，并引入人类偏好对齐引导（HPAG）提升准确性，在BigCodeReward上将Qwen2.5-VL-32B从60.4%提升至66.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于量规的LLM评判器在代码评估中采用逐点评分后聚合比较的方式，与成对偏好预测任务不匹配，性能甚至不如单一的强评判器。

Method: 提出CriterAlign框架：直接进行逐准则成对评判、平局驱动的准则细化、交换一致性过滤和最终成对综合；并扩展出HPAG，从训练样例中离线合成人类偏好对齐引导信息，注入到准则生成器、准则评判器和最终评判器中。

Result: 在BigCodeReward上，CriterAlign将Qwen2.5-VL-32B的成对偏好预测准确率从60.4%提升至66.3%，消融实验证实了逐准则成对设计和HPAG的贡献。

Conclusion: 基于量规的评判器应针对成对偏好预测任务进行专门设计，直接进行逐准则成对判断比逐点评分后聚合更有效，且人类偏好对齐引导可进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Pairwise human preference prediction is central to evaluating code-generation systems, where quality often depends on task-specific trade-offs beyond functional correctness. While rubric-based LLM judges improve interpretability by decomposing evaluation into explicit criteria, most existing pipelines remain pointwise: they score each response independently and derive preferences by comparing aggregated scores. We show that this design is poorly matched to pairwise code preference prediction and can underperform a strong monolithic judge. We propose CriterAlign, a criterion-centric framework that adapts rubric-based judging to pairwise preference evaluation through direct criterion-level pairwise judgments, tie-driven criterion refinement, swap-consistency filtering, and final pairwise synthesis. We further introduce Human-Preference-Aligned Guidance (HPAG), synthesized offline from training examples by extracting recurring rationale gaps between human preferences and monolithic judge predictions, and injected into the criterion generator, criterion judge, and final judge. On BigCodeReward, CriterAlign improves a Qwen2.5-VL-32B monolithic judge from 60.4% to 66.3% accuracy, with ablations confirming the contributions of pairwise criterion design and HPAG.

</details>


### [177] [Can LLMs Produce Better Object-Oriented Designs than Human-Involved Development?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19901)
*Zushuai Zhang,Elliott Wen,Ewan Tempero*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究比较了PreAI（人类参与）、PostAI（人类+LLM辅助）和PureAI（纯LLM生成）三类Java项目的面向对象设计质量。PureAI项目代码异味密度更低、规模更小，但存在过度简化问题，如缺少抽象和职责分离薄弱。PostAI项目在多项指标上接近PureAI，也呈现过度简化趋势。结论认为，在使用LLM进行面向对象设计时，人类对分解和职责分配的正确指导仍然重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在代码生成中越来越普及，但它们在生成需要面向对象设计的多类项目方面的能力尚不明确，尤其是与有人类参与开发的项目相比。现有研究缺乏对LLM生成项目的OOD质量的系统评估。

Method: 采用比较案例研究方法，以研究生Java作业为实验材料。选取同一作业任务的两个版本分别作为PreAI和PostAI数据集，使用三种当代LLM生成PureAI项目。通过项目级OOD度量指标、代码异味密度和领域建模三个维度分析OOD质量。

Result: PureAI项目相比人类参与项目，代码异味密度更低，总体规模、复杂度和耦合度更简单。但这种简化与过度简化一致，表现为缺少抽象和职责分离薄弱。PostAI项目在多项OOD指标上更接近PureAI而非PreAI，也显示出过度简化倾向。

Conclusion: 在使用LLM进行面向对象设计时，人类对面向对象分解和职责分配的适当指导仍然至关重要。LLM生成的代码虽然表面质量指标良好，但存在设计层面的不足，需要人类专家的干预和修正。

Abstract: Background: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation. However, their ability to generate multi-class projects that require object-oriented design (OOD) remains unclear, especially relative to projects developed with human involvement. Aims: The primary objective of this study is to compare OOD quality in projects from three authorship conditions: PreAI (human-involved projects produced before widespread LLM use), PostAI (human-involved projects produced after widespread LLM use), and PureAI (projects generated end-to-end by contemporary LLMs). Method: We conducted a comparative case study on a postgraduate Java assignment. Two offerings of the same assignment were selected as the PreAI and PostAI datasets. PureAI projects were generated using three contemporary LLMs. We analyzed OOD quality using project-level OOD metrics, code smell density, and domain modeling. Results: Relative to human-involved projects, PureAI projects show lower code smell density and generally appear simpler in terms of total size, complexity, and coupling. However, this is consistent with oversimplification, as it is associated with missing abstractions and weaker responsibility separation. PostAI is closer to PureAI than PreAI on many OOD measures and also shows tendencies toward oversimplification. Conclusions: Our findings indicate that appropriate human guidance on object-oriented decomposition and responsibility assignment remains important when LLMs are used for object-oriented design.

</details>


### [178] [Towards LLM-Assisted Architecture Recovery for Real-World ROS~2 Systems: An Agent-Based Multi-Level Approach to Hierarchical Structural Architecture Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20055)
*Dominique Briechle,Raj Chanchad,Tobias Geger,Ruidi He,Dhruv Jajadiya,Dhruv Kapadiya,Andreas Rausch,Meng Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对ROS 2机器人系统中层级架构恢复困难的问题，提出了基于蓝图引导和LLM辅助的架构恢复流水线的两项改进：精细化提示工程和多级中间架构表示的分阶段恢复策略。在真实自动化产品拆解系统上的评估表明，该方法在结构一致性、可扩展性和鲁棒性方面优于先前工作，但仍面临大规模ROS 2系统中动态集成语义的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS 2机器人系统中，结构化的（解）组合与集成语义通常隐式编码在源代码和启动文件等分布式工件中，导致层级架构恢复尤为困难。现有方法主要关注节点级实体和通信连线，对跨多个抽象级别的层级结构（解）组合恢复支持有限。

Method: 在先前提出的蓝图引导的LLM辅助架构恢复流水线基础上，提出两项增强：(1) 精细化提示工程以提升架构合成的一致性和可控性；(2) 基于多级中间架构表示的分阶段恢复策略，融合原子ROS节点列表和启动文件依赖关系，实现跨多个抽象级别的结构约束重构。

Result: 在基于协作机械臂和异构ROS 2工件的真实自动化产品拆解系统上评估，相较于先前工作，该案例研究展现了更高的集成复杂性和更丰富的功能。结果证明了架构恢复在结构一致性、可扩展性和鲁棒性方面的改进，同时也揭示了大规糢ROS 2系统中动态集成语义相关的挑战。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能有效提升ROS 2系统层级架构恢复的质量，但在处理大规模系统中的动态集成语义方面仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Explicit software architecture models are essential artifacts for communicating, analyzing, and evolving complex software-intensive systems. In ROS~2-based robotic systems, however, structural (de-)composition and integration semantics are often only implicitly encoded across distributed artifacts such as source code and launch files, making recovery of hierarchical architecture particularly difficult. Existing approaches mainly focus on node-level entities and communication wiring, while providing limited support for recovering hierarchical structural (de-)composition across multiple abstraction levels.
  In this paper, we extend our previously proposed blueprint-guided LLM-assisted architecture recovery pipeline for ROS~2 systems through two major enhancements: (1) refined prompting to improve the consistency and controllability of architecture synthesis, and (2) a staged recovery strategy based on multi-level intermediate architectural representations that incorporate the atomic ROS node list and launch file dependencies, thereby enabling structurally constrained reconstruction across multiple abstraction levels.
  The approach is evaluated on a real-world automated product disassembly system based on cooperative robotic arms and heterogeneous ROS~2 artifacts. Compared to our previous work, the considered case study exhibits substantially higher integration complexity and richer functionality. The results demonstrate improved structural consistency, scalability, and robustness of architecture recovery, while also revealing remaining challenges related to dynamic integration semantics in large-scale ROS~2 systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Interoceptive Divergence in Aesthetic Evaluation and Implications for Human-AI Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18759)
*Yoshia Abe,Tatsuya Daikoku,Yasuo Kuniyoshi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过比较人类和大型语言模型在审美评价中的反应发现，两者在美与情绪的相关性及图像特征偏好上表现出相似模式，但在情绪反应分布和美感与身体感觉的关系上存在显著差异，揭示当前AI在审美体验中对内感受层面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类不仅具备智能（如LLM所展现的），还拥有感受美等感性能力。为了探究人类与AI在审美体验上的趋同与分歧，作者基于先前人类关于美、身体感觉与情绪关系的研究，采用相同问卷对LLM进行测试，旨在比较两者在审美评价上的异同。

Method: 采用与先前人类研究一致的问卷项目，将相同的问题呈现给大型语言模型（LLMs），收集AI对视觉场景的审美评价、情绪反应和身体感觉的响应数据，然后与人类的已有数据进行直接对比分析，包括相关性分析和分布比较。

Result: 人类与AI在美感评分与情绪的相关性以及图像特征优先级上表现出大致相似的模式；但在情绪反应的分布以及美感评分与身体感觉的关系上存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的LLM在审美评价中可以一定程度上接近人类的平均倾向，但在内感受（身体感觉）方面存在明显局限，这可能源于训练数据中对此类信息的表示不足或对齐过程的非预期后果。这为AI对齐和开发具有类人审美处理能力的AI系统提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI), exemplified by large language models (LLMs), is rapidly approaching and in some cases surpassing human performance across a wide range of cognitive tasks. However, human nature is not limited to intelligence alone; it also encompasses sensibility, including the capacity to perceive and experience beauty in visual scenes. This raises a fundamental question: how humans and AI systems converge or diverge in such aesthetic experiences. Aesthetic evaluation depends not only on objective properties of images but also on internal processes within the observer. As part of ongoing efforts in AI alignment, building upon prior human studies that have examined the relationship between beauty ratings, bodily sensations, and emotions, we adopt a comparable set of questionnaire items and present them to LLMs, enabling a direct comparison between human and AI responses. Our comparative analyses revealed that, while humans and AI exhibited broadly similar patterns in the correlations between beauty ratings and emotions, as well as in the image features they prioritized, notable divergences emerged in both the distribution of emotional responses and the relationship between beauty ratings and bodily sensations. These findings suggest that state-of-the-art LLMs, trained on large-scale textual data, can approximate average human tendencies in aesthetic evaluation to a certain extent. However, they also indicate limitations, particularly in relation to interoceptive aspects, which may reflect insufficient representation in training data or unintended consequences of alignment processes. These findings highlight key challenges for AI alignment and suggest important directions for developing AI systems with human-like aesthetic processing.

</details>


### [180] [Balancing Teacher and Student Agency: Co-Orchestration Tool Design Supporting Real-Time Dynamic Pairing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18761)
*Kexin Bella Yang,Menghan Liu,Liyi Xu,Nikol Rummel,Vincent Aleven*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在人机交互的教育场景中，如何平衡教师与学生在动态配对过程（个体学习与协作学习切换）中的控制权。通过参与式快速约会和混合方法分析，本文提出了“混合控制”设计空间，建议在活动初期提供结构化教师指导，随后逐步增加学生自主权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互中尊重用户主体性对于建立信任和有效使用技术至关重要。教育场景中动态整合个体与协作学习具有教学价值，但如何平衡教师与学生在此过程中的控制权尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用参与式快速约会（participatory speed dating）和混合方法分析，聚焦配对过程中“之前、期间、之后”三个阶段，探索教师与学生之间的控制权分配设计空间。

Result: 提出了一个理论化的设计空间，强调了多利益相关方主体性与控制权的平衡，并给出了实现分析型编排工具中“混合控制”的设计建议。

Conclusion: 建议在活动初期提供结构化的教师指导，随着活动展开逐步增加学生的自主权，从而实现混合控制。

Abstract: In human-AI interaction, respecting user agency is essential for fostering trust and sustaining effective use of technology. In educational settings, dynamically integrating individual and collaborative learning offers pedagogical value by supporting personalized, self-paced learning experiences. Prior research has demonstrated the feasibility of this approach through intelligent tutoring systems and human-AI co-orchestration tools. However, how to balance teacher and student control in this process remains largely unexplored. This work explores the design space of how control can be distributed between teachers and students across the orchestration process, using participatory speed dating and a mixed-method analysis.
