Compared to the state-of-the-art baseline, KIO-planner achieves lower inference latency (approximately 24 ms) and generates significantly smoother trajectories, reducing control cost by 28.4%. Most notably, our Dual Mapping substantially increases the worst-case safety margin, measured by minimum distance to obstacles, from 0.48 m to 0.76 m, ensuring fast, smooth, and safer navigation in highly constrained environments.

</details>


### [217] [Beyond Imitation: Learning Safe End-to-End Autonomous Driving from Hard Negatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19771)
*Junli Wang,Zhihua Hua,Xueyi Liu,Zebin Xing,Haochen Tian,Kun Ma,Hangjun Ye,Guang Chen,Long Chen,Qichao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BeyondDrive提出了一种故障感知的模仿学习框架，通过流匹配生成安全关键负轨迹、多样性感知采样和排斥距离损失，解决自动驾驶中轨迹相近但安全结果不同的问题，在NAVSIMv1基准上达到89.7 PDMS，并具备良好的泛化与零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶的模仿学习方法仅从成功演示中学习，通过最小化与专家轨迹的几何偏差来训练模型。这隐含了空间邻近性等同于行为安全性的假设，导致关键的目标错配：具有几乎相同模仿损失的轨迹可能产生截然不同的安全结果（一条可恢复，另一条导致碰撞）。

Method: 1) 基于流匹配的负轨迹生成器，合成安全关键且接近专家轨迹的负样本；2) 多样性感知采样策略，缓解模式坍塌，覆盖多种失败模式；3) 排斥距离损失，同时吸引预测向专家轨迹靠近、排斥远离硬负轨迹，建立轨迹空间中的判别性安全边界。

Result: 在NAVSIMv1闭环基准上达到89.7 PDMS，超越之前的最优方法；在不同自动驾驶架构（包括多模态规划器）上有效泛化；在HUGSIM基准上展现出强大的零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: 提出了一种联合学习成功与失败驾驶行为的故障感知框架，有效解决了轨迹空间中的安全不对称性问题，显著提升了自动驾驶的安全性。

Abstract: Existing imitation learning methods for end-to-end autonomous driving predominantly learn from successful demonstrations by minimizing geometric deviations from expert trajectories. This paradigm implicitly assumes that spatial proximity implies behavioral safety, leading to a critical objective mismatch: trajectories with nearly identical imitation losses may exhibit drastically different safety outcomes, where one remains recoverable while the other results in collision. To address this limitation, we propose BeyondDrive, a failure-aware imitation learning framework that jointly learns from successful and failed driving behaviors. First, we introduce a flow matching-based negative trajectory generator that synthesizes safety-critical yet expert-proximate trajectories, enabling explicit modeling of safety asymmetry. Second, we develop a diversity-aware sampling strategy that mitigates mode collapse and improves coverage of diverse failure modes during negative trajectory generation. Third, we propose a Repulsive Distance Loss that simultaneously attracts predictions toward expert demonstrations while repelling them from hard negative trajectories, thereby establishing discriminative safety boundaries in trajectory space. Applied to the uni-modal baseline Latent TransFuser, BeyondDrive achieves 89.7 PDMS on the NAVSIMv1 closed-loop benchmark, outperforming prior state-of-the-art methods. Moreover, BeyondDrive generalizes effectively across different autonomous driving architectures, including multi-modal planners, and further demonstrates strong zero-shot transferability on the HUGSIM benchmark.

</details>


### [218] [Justifying bio-inspired robotics research: A taxonomy of strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19840)
*Margaret J. Zhang,Justin Ting,Talia Y. Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生物启发式设计的动机分类法，旨在帮助机器人领域的研究者合理证明其生物启发方法的有效性，并协助资助方评估不同生物启发方法的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类历史上对如何以及为何将自然元素融入设计缺乏系统性思考，导致动机和方法不一致，难以预测或评估生物启发设计的成功。这种期望与结果的错配常使读者感到失望，尤其在机器人领域。

Method: 提出了一种生物启发式设计的动机分类法（taxonomy），系统梳理不同生物启发方法的动机及其可能产生的潜在重要贡献。

Result: 该分类法可帮助机器人研究人员合理化其生物启发方法选择，并帮助资助方辨别不同生物启发方法的价值。

Conclusion: 通过系统化的动机分类，可以减少生物启发设计与结果之间的期望落差，提升该领域的研究评估和预测能力。

Abstract: For most of human history, we have not thought systematically about how and why we incorporate aspects of the natural world into our designs. The lack of a systematic approach has resulted in inconsistencies in motivations and methods that make it difficult to predict or evaluate the success of bio-inspired design. This mismatch between expectations and results can lead to disappointment when a reader considers a bio-inspired design to be superficial, weak, or incomplete. This is especially true in the field of Robotics, in which similarity to a biological system might be the driving motivation for construction. In an effort to assist robotics researchers justify their specific bio-inspired approach and to assist funding program managers with discerning the value of different bio-inspired approaches, here we propose a taxonomy of motivations for bio-inspired design and describe the potential significant contributions that are likely to result from different approaches.

</details>


### [219] [Beyond Action Residuals: Real-World Robot Policy Steering via Bottleneck Latent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19919)
*Dongjie Yu,Kun Lei,Zhennan Jiang,Jia Pan,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Z-Perturbation强化学习（ZPRL），通过在预训练策略中引入紧凑的变分信息瓶颈（VIB）模块作为潜在接口，并在在线微调时仅对该潜在空间施加残差扰动，从而更高效、更平滑地适应机器人操作任务。在8个仿真和4个真实任务上，ZPRL相比基线方法提升了样本效率和最终性能，平均成功率提升33.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的模仿策略在机器人操作中表现良好，但常因执行误差、数据覆盖不足和部署环境不匹配需要在线改进。现有的轻量级方法通常在动作空间直接施加残差修正，导致噪声大、探索结构差。因此，需要一个更高效的在线RL适应接口。

Method: ZPRL在离线训练阶段为预训练策略附加一个即插即用的变分信息瓶颈（VIB）模块，从观测嵌入中提取与任务相关的紧凑潜在表征。在线微调时，冻结基策略，RL仅学习该潜在空间上的残差扰动，解码后的表示再条件化冻结的动作生成器。

Result: 在8个仿真任务和4个真实世界任务上评估。ZPRL在样本效率和最终性能上均优于多种后训练基线。真实世界测试中，ZPRL在四项任务上的平均成功率相比模仿基策略提升33.7%，且探索行为比动作残差方法更平滑。

Conclusion: 紧凑、任务对齐的瓶颈潜在空间为在线RL自适应提供了一个有效的接口，能够在不修改预训练策略权重或输出动作的前提下实现高效的策略改进。

Abstract: Pretrained imitation policies have become a strong foundation for robot manipulation, but they often require online improvement to overcome execution errors, limited dataset coverage, and deployment mismatch. A central question is therefore how reinforcement learning (RL) should adapt policies after offline pretraining. Existing lightweight methods commonly apply residual corrections directly in action space, but this often leads to noisy and poorly structured exploration. In this work, we propose Z-Perturbation Reinforcement Learning (ZPRL), an approach that steers pretrained policies through a compact bottleneck latent rather than through policy weights or output actions. During offline training, we augment the policy with a plug-and-play variational information bottleneck (VIB) module to extract a task-relevant latent interface from observation embeddings. During online finetuning, the base policy is frozen and RL learns only a residual perturbation on this latent, whose decoded representation conditions the frozen action generator. We instantiate ZPRL on flow-matching policies and evaluate it on eight simulation tasks and four real-world tasks. Across diverse manipulation settings, ZPRL improves both sample efficiency and final performance over strong post-training baselines. In the real world, ZPRL improves the average success rate on four tasks by 33.7% over imitation base policies while producing smoother exploration behaviors than an action residual counterpart. These results suggest that a compact, task-aligned bottleneck latent provides an effective interface for online RL adaptation. More videos can be found at https://manutdmoon.github.io/ZPRL/.

</details>


### [220] [RoHIL: Robust Human-in-the-Loop Robotic Reinforcement Learning Against Illumination Variations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19924)
*Shuoqin Zhang,Yixin Xiong,Xiru Gao,Kai Liu,Ke Wang,Xichuan Zhou,Zhe Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoHIL提出了一种无需额外真实机器人交互的离线微调框架，通过世界模型图像重打光、照明保持重放机制和锚定贝尔曼演员正则化，解决了跨工作站光照分布偏移导致的强化学习系统性能崩溃问题，在四个真实机器人操作任务上显著提升了光照迁移性能，同时保留了源工作站性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协同强化学习系统在同一工作站上表现近乎完美，但当机器人移动到几米外的另一个工作站时，由于新灯位置和窗户光引起的视觉输入分布偏移，性能完全崩溃。对每个工作站重新收集示教数据和重新运行HIL不切实际，而在光照偏移数据上简单微调会引发对源工作站的灾难性遗忘。

Method: RoHIL包含三个核心组件：(i) 基于世界模型的图像重打光器，在多种虚拟HDRI环境下重新合成源工作站轨迹的视觉流；(ii) 照明保持重放（IRR），一种数据级抗遗忘机制，交替使用重打光的适应转换和原始光照的保留转换；(iii) 锚定贝尔曼演员正则化器，约束表征和策略相对于原始工作站策略的漂移。

Result: 在四个真实机器人操作任务上，面对显著的跨工作站光照变化，RoHIL在标准HIL-RL失效的光照偏移条件下大幅提升了性能，同时保留了源工作站的性能，消除了为每个新工作站和环境重新收集数据和重新训练的需求。

Conclusion: RoHIL有效解决了跨工作站光照分布偏移导致的性能崩溃问题，实现了无需额外真实数据收集的离线微调，为机器人部署中的视觉域迁移提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning systems achieve near-perfect success on the workstation where they are trained, but collapse when the same robot is moved to a workstation a few meters away due to shifts in the visual input distribution caused by new lamp positions and window light. Re-collecting demonstrations and re-running HIL on every workstation is incompatible with deployment, and naively fine-tuning on shifted-light data triggers catastrophic forgetting of the source workstation. To close this cross-domain gap, we present RoHIL, an offline fine-tuning framework that uses no extra real-robot interaction. RoHIL combines (i) a world-model-based image relighter that re-synthesises the visual stream of source-workstation trajectories under multiple virtual HDRI environments, leaving actions and rewards real; (ii) Illumination-Retention Replay (IRR), a data-level anti-forgetting mechanism that interleaves relit adaptation transitions with original-light retention transitions to preserve source-workstation Bellman coverage; and (iii) an anchored Bellman-actor regulariser that constrains representation and policy drift from the original source-workstation policy. Across four real-robot manipulation tasks under significant cross-workstation illumination variations, RoHIL substantially improves shifted-light performance where standard HIL-RL collapses, while preserving source-workstation performance, eliminating the need to re-collect data and retrain for every new workstation and environment. Project page: https://anonymous4365.github.io/RoHIL/

</details>


### [221] [TravExplorer: Cross-Floor Embodied Exploration via Traversability-Aware 3-D Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19958)
*Han Zheng,Zhe Chen,Yudong Huang,Haoran Liu,Jinghao Wang,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TravExplorer是一个跨楼层零样本目标导航框架，通过统一三维体积地图、可通行前沿提取、FOV感知主动视觉、轻量级语义引导和分层规划器，实现了在未知环境中跨楼层开放词汇目标搜索，在HM3D/MP3D模拟和真实Go2机器人上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZSON系统局限于平面表示和单楼层假设，无法应对真实建筑中包含楼梯、平台和垂直重叠空间的多楼层环境，亟需一个能感知可通行性并支持跨楼层规划的三维导航框架。

Method: 1) 统一体积地图：区分占用结构与机器人可达支撑面；2) 可通行前沿提取：从连通支撑面（楼层、楼梯、平台）中提取前沿；3) FOV感知主动视觉：解决跨楼层遍历中的不完整观测；4) 轻量级语义引导：在线开放词汇分割的概率实例图与图像-文本匹配的空间价值图对齐；5) 分层规划器：在目标假设、可通行前沿和楼梯地标上进行目标感知前沿遍历，通过立足点引导的3D搜索和垂直约束局部轨迹优化生成跨楼层运动。

Result: 在HM3D和MP3D上4,195个模拟 episode 中一致优于代表性ObjectNav基线；在Unitree Go2上50次真实实验验证了单楼层和跨楼层开放词汇目标搜索的有效性，无需先验地图或人工干预。

Conclusion: TravExplorer通过几何-语义记忆与层次化规划的有效耦合，首次实现了零样本跨楼层目标导航，突破了现有系统对平面表示和单楼层假设的限制。

Abstract: Zero-shot Object Navigation (ZSON) has shown promise for open-vocabulary target search in unseen environments, yet most existing systems remain tied to planar representations and single-floor assumptions. These assumptions become inadequate in real buildings, where navigation involves floors, stairs, landings, and vertically overlapping spaces. This article presents TravExplorer, a cross-floor embodied exploration framework that couples zero-shot semantic guidance with traversability-aware 3-D planning. TravExplorer maintains a unified volumetric map that distinguishes occupied structures from robot-reachable support surfaces and extracts traversable frontiers from connected support surfaces, including floors, stairs, and landings. A FOV-aware active perception strategy further resolves incomplete observations during cross-floor traversal. To reduce semantic-reasoning latency, a lightweight guidance module aligns a probabilistic instance map from online open-vocabulary segmentation with a spatial value map from fast image-to-text matching. Based on these geometric and semantic memories, a hierarchical planner performs target-aware frontier touring over object hypotheses, traversable frontiers, and stair landmarks, and generates executable cross-floor motions through foothold-guided 3-D search and vertically constrained local trajectory optimization. Experiments over 4,195 simulated episodes on HM3D and MP3D demonstrate consistent advantages over representative ObjectNav baselines. Fifty real-world trials on a Unitree Go2 further validate open-vocabulary target search across single-floor and cross-floor indoor environments without prior maps or human intervention. The code will be released at https://github.com/wuyi2121/TravExplorer.

</details>


### [222] [CEER: Compliant End-Effector and Root Control as a Unified Interface for Hierarchical Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19981)
*Xinyuan Luo,Xingrui Chen,Xunjian Yin,Hongxuan Wu,Boxi Xia,Zhuoqun Chen,Jinzhou Li,Boyuan Chen,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CEER提出了一种基于末端执行器-根（EE-root）控制抽象的分层框架，实现了仿人机器人的柔顺全身控制与模块化操作规划，在仿真和硬件实验中展现出高精度跟踪、低抖动和良好的接触鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿人机器人在接触丰富、长时域操作任务中表现不佳，现有方法难以同时满足柔顺交互和模块化规划的需求。

Method: 提出CEER控制抽象，将根运动指令和末端执行器位姿目标作为可解释的任务空间；采用师生框架将通用运动跟踪控制器蒸馏为仅消费EE-root指令的低层策略；构建分层系统，通过EE-root接口集成异构规划器和任务模块。

Result: 末端执行器跟踪精度达3.3 cm，抖动显著低于基线；遥操作下接触鲁棒；室内规模模拟中单物体操作任务成功率最高达70%。

Conclusion: CEER提供的柔顺EE-root控制抽象为仿人机器人操作提供了实用且可扩展的模块化方案。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have achieved impressive locomotion performance, yet contact-rich and long-horizon manipulation remains a major bottleneck. Manipulation is inherently contact-rich and demands compliant whole-body control for stable interaction, while its diversity and long-horizon nature favor modular, planner-compatible interfaces over joint-space tracking.
  We propose CEER, a compliant end-effector-root (EE-root) control abstraction for modular humanoid loco-manipulation within a hierarchical planning framework. CEER enables compliance-aware whole-body control in an interpretable task space defined by root motion commands and end-effector pose targets, and supports plug-and-play integration with heterogeneous high-level planners. A teacher-student framework is adopted to distill a general motion-tracking controller into a low-level policy that consumes only EE-root commands.
  We further construct a hierarchical system that integrates heterogeneous planners and task modules through the EE-root interface, enabling diverse manipulation tasks without retraining the underlying whole-body policy. Experiments in simulation and on hardware demonstrate 3.3 cm end-effector tracking accuracy with substantially reduced jerk compared to baselines, stable contact-rich manipulation under teleoperation, and up to 70% success in simulated single-object loco-manipulation tasks within a room-scale environment. These results indicate that compliant EE-root control provides a practical abstraction for humanoid loco-manipulation, enabling modular and scalable integration of diverse skills.

</details>


### [223] [Beyond Binary Success: A Diagnostic Meta-Evaluation Framework for Fine-Grained Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19986)
*He-Yang Xu,Pengyuan Zhang,Zongyuan Ge,Xiaoshuai Hao,Serge Belongie,Xin Geng,Yuxin Peng,Xiu-Shen Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MetaFine 是一个用于细粒度操作任务的元评估框架，通过解耦理解、感知和受控行为三个维度，揭示现有基准将能力掩盖在二元成功率下的问题，并发现视觉编码器保持局部空间结构的能力是精细操作的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身AI基准将细粒度操作所需的多重能力（局部属性定位、高保真空间感知、约束执行）坍缩为二元成功率，系统性高估能力达70%，掩盖了阻碍真实部署的结构性瓶颈。

Method: 提出MetaFine诊断性元评估框架，沿理解、感知、受控行为三个轴解耦操作能力。基于组合式任务图吸收外部异构基准，重构为不同复杂度的诊断场景；通过因果干预定位瓶颈，并支持混合真实-仿真验证，用有限真实 rollout 校准可扩展的仿真估计。

Result: 对最新VLA模型的评估揭示了传统指标无法感知的维度特异性失败；因果干预发现视觉编码器保持局部空间结构的能力是关键瓶颈，改进它可直接解锁下游策略无法取得的能力；混合验证方案提高了物理基准测试的稳定性。

Conclusion: MetaFine将评估从排名转向诊断，使基准测试成为修复真实物理灵巧性背后分层能力的可操作指南，为细粒度操作研究提供了更有洞察力的评估范式。

Abstract: Fine-grained manipulation marks a regime where global scene context no longer suffices, and success hinges on the tight coupling of local attribute grounding, high-fidelity spatial perception, and constraint-respecting motor execution. However, current embodied AI benchmarks collapse these capacities into binary success rates, systematically inflating reported capabilities by up to 70% and masking the architectural bottlenecks that impede real-world deployment. We introduce MetaFine, a diagnostic meta-evaluation framework that disentangles manipulation competency along three axes: understanding, perception, and controlled behavior. Built on a compositional task graph, MetaFine absorbs heterogeneous external benchmarks and reconstructs them into diagnostic scenarios of varying complexity under a unified protocol. Evaluating state-of-the-art vision-language-action (VLA) models through this lens exposes severe dimension-specific failures invisible to conventional metrics. Through targeted causal intervention, we identify the visual encoder's ability to preserve local spatial structure as a key bottleneck for fine-grained precision: improving it directly unlocks previously inaccessible manipulation capabilities without modifying downstream policies. MetaFine further supports hybrid real-sim validation, using limited paired real-world rollouts to calibrate scalable simulation-based estimates for more stable physical benchmarking. By shifting evaluation from ranking to diagnosis, MetaFine turns benchmarking into an actionable compass for repairing the layered capacities underlying genuine physical dexterity. The MetaFine framework, benchmarks, and supporting resources will be publicly released at our project page: https://metafine.github.io/.

</details>


### [224] [Topology-Optimized Pneumatic Soft Actuator: Design and Experimental Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20101)
*Sumit Mehta,Konstantinos Poulios*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过非线性拓扑优化方法，将基于密度和多孔超弹性的拓扑优化框架从2D扩展到3D，设计了两种软弹性气动执行器。两种设计均以最大化弯曲响应为目标，在不同应变极限下进行优化，并通过立体光刻制造和实验验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体气动执行器设计多依赖经验或简单几何，缺乏系统性的优化方法，特别是3D拓扑优化框架尚未被有效应用于此类问题。

Method: 基于密度和多孔超弹性理论的拓扑优化框架从2D扩展到3D；以最大化弯曲响应为优化目标，设置两种不同允许应变极限；考虑加压过程中产生的大变形，在优化过程中一致地处理非线性；使用立体光刻3D打印制造优化后的设计。

Result: 成功生成了两种可制造的3D执行器设计；数值模拟与实验测试结果一致，验证了优化设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的3D非线性拓扑优化框架能够系统性地优化软弹性气动执行器的设计，并且优化结果具有可制造性和实验可验证性，为软体机器人执行器的设计提供了有效的计算工具。

Abstract: This paper demonstrates the computational design of soft elastomeric pneumatic actuators using nonlinear topology optimization. An existing density- and porohyperelasticity-based topology optimization framework was extended from 2D to 3D and used to generate two manufacturable actuator designs, which were then studied numerically and experimentally. For both designs, the objective was to maximize the bending response for a prescribed actuation pressure under two different allowable strain limits. A key advantage of the employed topology optimization framework is that it can consistently, during the optimization, account for the very large deformations induced upon pressurization. The two optimized 3D designs were fabricated using stereolithography and experimentally tested to validate their performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.SC'></div>

# cs.SC [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Computing Certificates in Archimedean Univariate Saturated Quadratic Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18980)
*Jose Abel Castellanos-Joo,Deepak Kapur*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的符号算法，用于计算单变量非负多项式在饱和二次模块中的平方和乘子（证书）。该算法基于Kuhlmann-Marshall的自然生成元，通过“基本引理”实现证书在不同表示形式间的转换，并在Maple中实现，与RealCertify相比在某些案例中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的平方和证书计算方法（如RealCertify）在某些情况下无法找到证书，需要一种更有效的符号算法来处理单变量非负多项式在饱和二次模块中的成员资格验证问题。

Method: 首先利用Kuhlmann和Marshall引入的自然生成元计算证书，该生成元可直接从半代数集中读出；然后通过Kuhlmann、Marshall和Schwartz的“基本引理”将非负因子拆分，实现自然生成元证书到原始生成元证书的转换；最后基于自然生成元的类型进行详细的案例分析，计算乘积证书。

Result: 在Maple中实现了所提算法，并与RealCertify进行了比较。实验结果表明，在某些RealCertify无法找到证书的案例中，本文方法能够成功计算证书。

Conclusion: 所提出的符号算法为单变量饱和二次模块中的证书计算提供了一种有效的替代方案，在特定场景下优于现有工具。

Abstract: A new symbolic algorithm to compute sums of squares multipliers (certificates) to witness the membership of non-negative univariate polynomials in a saturated univariate quadratic module is presented. Certificates are first computed in terms of natural generators introduced by Kuhlmann and Marshall for an Archimedean saturated quadratic module; natural generators can be easily read-off from a semialgebraic set. In the univariate case, an Archimedean quadratic module is also a preordering since it is closed under multiplication; certificates have different representations when a polynomial is viewed as a member in a quadratic module versus in a preordering An algorithm is given to compute certificates of natural generators in terms of the original generators; it uses a construction introduced by Kuhlmann, Marshall, and Schwartz known as the ``Basic Lemma'', which splits the non-negative factors of generators. To compute a quadratic module certificate, certificates of products of natural generators are computed using a detailed case analysis based on the types of natural generators.
  An implementation of the algorithms proposed in Maple is also discussed. The certificates obtained using this implementation are compared with those generated by RealCertify. We discuss examples where RealCertify is unable to find certificates while the proposed method is successful.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [226] [Rewrite System Showdown: Stochastic Search vs. EqSat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19005)
*Qiantan Hong,Rupanshu Soi,Yihong Zhang,Alex Aiken*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文通过五个基准测试，将等式饱和与随机搜索两种程序优化方法进行严格比较，回答“e-graphs是否真的有效”这一核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等式饱和已成为等式程序优化的主导范式，但从未与另一种方法（如随机搜索）进行过严格比较，因此需要实证评估e-graphs的实际效果。

Method: 在五个基准测试上，将等式饱和与随机搜索两种方法进行系统性对比实验。

Result: 通过五个基准测试的实验数据，展示了两种方法的相对表现。

Conclusion: 文章旨在通过实证比较来验证e-graphs（等式饱和）是否真的优于随机搜索等其他方法。

Abstract: Equality saturation has become a dominant paradigm for equational program optimization. However, it has never been rigorously compared to another approach to the same problem, even though several exist, the most notable being stochastic search. In this paper, we compare equality saturation to stochastic search over five benchmarks to answer the question: are e-graphs actually good?

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Decentralized autonomous organization and blockchain-based incentivization framework for community-based facilities management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18773)
*Reachsak Ly,Alireza Shojaei,Xinghua Gao,Philip Agee,Abiola Akanmu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于区块链和去中心化自治组织(DAO)的智能建筑社区设施管理框架，通过去中心化治理平台和代币激励机制促进利益相关者参与设施维护，并通过成本分析、可扩展性、安全性评估、可用性测试及半结构化访谈验证了该框架作为激励解决方案的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统设施管理依赖集中式决策结构，限制了利益相关者参与，导致与 occupant 需求脱节和满意度降低。

Method: 提出区块链和DAO驱动的社区设施管理框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于区块链投票的去中心化治理平台，实现透明集体决策；(2) 具有代币化奖励激励机制的维护管理平台。通过成本分析、可扩展性、数据安全评估、可用性测试以及对设施管理者和研究人员的半结构化访谈进行系统评估。

Result: 该框架在吸引利益相关者参与建筑基础设施的集体维护和改进方面显示出作为可行激励解决方案的潜力。

Conclusion: 基于区块链和DAO的去中心化设施管理框架通过代币激励和透明决策机制，能够有效提升利益相关者参与度，为解决传统集中式设施管理的痛点提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Traditional facility management often relies on centralized decision-making structures that limit stakeholder participation, leading to misalignment with occupant needs and reduced satisfaction. This paper proposes a novel blockchain- and Decentralized Autonomous Organization (DAO)-based framework for community-based facilities management in smart buildings. The framework comprises two key components: a decentralized governance platform that facilitates transparent collective decision-making through blockchain-based voting, and a maintenance management platform with an incentivization mechanism that encourages building occupants to actively contribute to facility upkeep through tokenized rewards. System evaluation includes cost analysis, scalability, data security considerations, usability testing, and semi-structured interviews with facility managers and researchers to assess the platform's usefulness, challenges, and adoption potential. The findings demonstrate the framework's potential as a viable incentivization solution for engaging stakeholders in the collective upkeep and improvement of building infrastructure.

</details>


### [228] [DarkLLM: Learning Language-Driven Adversarial Attacks with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18868)
*Ye Sun,Xin Wang,Jiaming Zhang,Yifeng Gao,Yixu Wang,Yifan Ding,Qixian Zhang,Henghui Ding,Xingjun Ma,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DarkLLM是一个利用大语言模型将自然语言攻击指令转换为视觉对抗扰动的新型攻击框架，仅需1B参数即可在4个任务、13个数据集和15个模型上实现高效攻击，揭示了基础模型的系统性漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对抗攻击局限于单一预定义目标，攻击与特定模型或任务紧密耦合，缺乏可扩展性和灵活性，无法适应现实场景中的多样化需求。

Method: 训练一个LLM将自然语言攻击指令翻译为潜在攻击向量，再解码为视觉对抗扰动。通过自然语言指令微调，统一了定向、非定向、分割和多模型攻击，实现灵活可控的对抗生成。

Result: 仅1B参数的DarkLLM能遵循攻击者指令，对CLIP、SAM和前沿LLMs生成高效攻击，在4个任务、13个数据集和15个模型上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: DarkLLM揭示了现代基础模型存在系统性对抗脆弱性，基于自然语言指令的统一攻击框架为评估多模型安全性提供了新范式。

Abstract: While vision and multimodal foundation models underpin critical tasks from perception to complex reasoning, they remain highly vulnerable to adversarial attacks. However, traditional adversarial attacks are typically limited to single, predefined objectives, tightly coupling each attack to a specific model or task, which restricts their scalability and flexibility in real-world scenarios. In this work, we present DarkLLM, a novel attack framework that trains an LLM to translate natural-language attack instructions into latent attack vectors, which are then decoded into visual adversarial perturbations. By leveraging natural-language instruction tuning, DarkLLM not only unifies targeted, untargeted, segmentation, and multi-model attacks within a single framework, but also achieves flexible and controllable adversarial generation, enabling each instruction to produce a perturbation that induces desired behaviors across heterogeneous models. Through extensive experiments across 4 tasks, 13 datasets, and 15 models, we demonstrate that DarkLLM with only 1B parameters can follow attacker instructions and generate highly effective attacks against CLIP, SAM, and frontier LLMs, revealing a systemic vulnerability in modern foundation models.

</details>


### [229] [Detecting and Mitigating Backdoor Attacks in OTA-FL Systems: A Two-Stage Robust Aggregation Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19253)
*Xiaoyan Ma,Seohyun Lee,Taejoon Kim,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对OTA-FL中后门攻击的防御问题，提出了一种两阶段鲁棒聚合框架，通过模态感知多指标信任评分和基于信任的多址接入机制，有效区分可信、可疑和恶意客户端，配合层-wise检查和纵向信誉机制，在多种数据集上抑制了多种隐蔽后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTA-FL利用无线信道叠加特性提升通信效率，但该特性导致参数服务器无法获取个体更新，难以识别和排除中毒梯度；尤其在Non-IID数据下，良性梯度漂移与恶意更新高度相似，进一步加剧了防御挑战。

Method: 提出两阶段鲁棒聚合框架：1) 为每个客户端分配模态感知多指标信任评分，根据数据模态和模型架构选择最能捕捉后门更新特征的指标；2) PS基于信任评分执行TBMA，将客户端分为可信、可疑和恶意三类；3) 对可疑客户端进行PS端逐层检查和纵向信誉机制进一步审查。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法能有效抑制多种隐蔽后门攻击（包括bounded-scaling、Euclidean-constrained、Cosine-constrained攻击及Neurotoxin），同时保持竞争性的主任务准确率。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段鲁棒聚合框架有效解决了OTA-FL在Non-IID场景下后门攻击的防御难题，兼顾了安全性与主任务性能。

Abstract: Over-the-air federated learning (OTA-FL) improves communication efficiency by exploiting the superposition property of wireless channels, but this same property also creates a critical security vulnerability: the parameter server (PS) cannot access individual local updates, making it difficult to identify and exclude poisoned gradients. The challenge is further exacerbated under non-independent and identically distributed (Non-IID) training data, where benign gradient drift can closely resemble malicious updates. In this paper, we propose a two-stage robust aggregation framework for defending against backdoor attacks in OTA-FL. Under our scheme, each client is first assigned a modality-aware multi-indicator trust score, where the specific indicators are selected according to the data modality (e.g., waveform, text, image) and model architecture to capture the most discriminative footprint of backdoor updates. Based on this score, the PS then performs trust-based multiple access (TBMA) to separate clients into trusted, suspicious, and malicious categories. Suspicious clients are further examined through PS-side layer-wise inspection and a longitudinal reputation mechanism. Experimental results on several datasets demonstrate that the proposed methodology effectively suppresses stealthy backdoor attacks, including bounded-scaling attacks, Euclidean-constrained attacks, Cosine-constrained attacks, and Neurotoxin, while maintaining competitive main-task accuracy.

</details>


### [230] [Fast and Lightweight Backdoor Detection via Head Random Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18908)
*Yinbo Yu,Xueyu Yin,Jing Fang,Chunwei Tian,Qi Zhu,Jiajia Liu,Daoqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出HTell，一种基于头部随机探测的无数据后门检测器，通过分析模型预测头对随机潜在探针的响应集中度来检测后门，无需数据、梯度或优化，检测速度比梯度方法快30000倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门检测器需要干净数据、替代数据、梯度或迭代触发器重建，计算成本高且在模型审计场景下鲁棒性有限，亟需一种快速、轻量级、无数据的后门检测方法。

Method: 生成架构感知的随机潜在探针，直接输入模型预测头，分析各类别响应统计量——被后门攻击的模型在目标类别上表现出异常的响应集中度，无需访问真实/替代数据、模型梯度或参数优化。

Result: 在超过6000个后门模型和700个干净模型的大规模基准上，包含4个数据集、14种架构、21种后门攻击；真阳性率99.03%，假阳性率2.11%，检测延迟仅12.69ms/模型，比梯度检测器快30000倍以上。

Conclusion: 头部随机探测为大范围无数据后门模型审计提供了准确、鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) remain critically vulnerable to backdoor attacks. Existing post-training detectors often require clean or surrogate data, gradients, or iterative trigger reconstruction, leading to high computational costs and limited robustness under practical model-auditing scenarios. In this paper, we propose HTell, a fast and lightweight data-free backdoor detector based on head random probing. Instead of reconstructing diverse trigger patterns, HTell inspects their unified manifestation in the prediction head: backdoored models tend to exhibit abnormal response concentration on the target class under random latent probes. HTell generates architecture-aware random latent probes, feeds them directly into the model head, and detects backdoors by analyzing class-wise response statistics, without accessing real or surrogate data, model gradients, or parameter optimization. We evaluate HTell on a large-scale benchmark containing more than 6,000 backdoored models and over 700 clean models, covering 4 datasets, 14 architectures, and 21 types of backdoor attacks. HTell achieves 99.03% true positive rate and 2.11% false positive rate with only 12.69 ms/model detection latency, reducing the time cost by over 30,000$\times$ compared with representative gradient-based detectors. These results demonstrate that head random probing provides an accurate, robust, and efficient solution for large-scale data-free backdoor model auditing.

</details>


### [231] [DMN: A Compositional Framework for Jailbreaking Multimodal LLMs with Multi-Image Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18915)
*Wenzhuo Xu,Zhipeng Wei,Zonghao Ying,Deyue Zhang,Dongdong Yang,Xiangzheng Zhang,Quanchen Zou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为DMN的组合式越狱框架，通过分布式指令、多模态证据和数字链任务，利用多图像输入的漏洞对多模态大模型进行攻击，在GPT-4o、Gemini-2.5-pro和Claude Sonnet 4上实现了超过90%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM越狱方法仅使用单张图像，限制了攻击空间：无法将有害请求分布到多张图像中、无法携带丰富信息、也无法利用额外的视觉推理任务来分散模型注意力。同时，多图像输入的安全性对齐工作不足，带来了新的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出DMN组合式越狱框架，包含三个核心组件：分布式指令（将有害请求分散到多张图像中）、多模态证据（利用多模态信息增强攻击）和数字链任务（通过视觉推理任务转移模型注意力），三者协同实现越狱。

Result: 在GPT-4o、Gemini-2.5-pro和Claude Sonnet 4上攻击成功率超过90%，大幅超越其他基线方法。

Conclusion: 这种组合式、多图像的越狱策略揭示了MLLMs安全机制的根本性弱点，表明当前多模态大模型在多图像场景下的安全对齐仍存在严重不足。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, which can elicit harmful responses from MLLMs. Many MLLMs support multi-image inputs, inadvertently introducing new vulnerabilities due to less efforts on multi-image safety alignment. Previous MLLM jailbreak methods only uses a single image, which restricts the attack space: they cannot distribute harmful requests across multiple images, carry abundant information, or exploit additional visual reasoning tasks to distract MLLMs. To address these limitations, in this paper, we propose a compositional jailbreak framework, \textbf{DMN}, which leverages \textbf{D}istributed instruction, \textbf{M}ultimodal evidence and a \textbf{N}umber chain task to fully enhance the jailbreak performance. Extensive experiments show that DMN is highly effective for MLLM jailbreaking, e.g. achieving attack success rates of over 90\% on GPT-4o, Gemini-2.5-pro and Claude Sonnet 4, surpassing other baselines by a large margin. This compositional, multi-image jailbreak strategy reveals fundamental weaknesses in their safety mechanisms.

</details>


### [232] [MoCo-EA: Exploiting Adversarial Mode Connectivity for Efficient Evolutionary Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18919)
*Hyo Seo Kim,Gang Luo,Can Chen,Binghui Wang,Yue Duan,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MoCo-EA利用贝塞尔曲线交叉算子替代传统遗传交叉操作，通过利用对抗扰动空间中的模式连通性来生成更高效的对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统进化算法中的交叉操作通过离散插值会破坏对抗扰动的有效性，需要设计能保持对抗性质的连续交叉机制。

Method: 提出MoCo-EA，用贝塞尔曲线交叉算子替代传统离散遗传交叉，在父代扰动之间沿连续贝塞尔曲线优化扰动路径。

Result: (1)成功对抗扰动表现出模式连通性；(2)优化路径上的中间点比端点具有更高的迁移性；(3)贝塞尔交叉大幅度优于离散遗传操作，同时减少收敛时间和查询需求。

Conclusion: 挑战了将对抗样本视为孤立点的传统观点，为攻击生成和防御研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Evolutionary algorithms for adversarial attacks leverage population-based search to discover perturbations without gradient information, but suffer from inefficient crossover operations that destroy adversarial properties through discrete interpolation. We introduce Mode Connectivity Evolutionary Attack (MoCo-EA), which replaces traditional crossover with a novel Bézier crossover operator that optimizes perturbations along a continuous Bézier curve between parent perturbations. Our key insight is that adversarial examples lie on connected manifolds where intermediate points maintain and often enhance attack effectiveness. We demonstrate three findings: (1) Successful adversarial perturbations exhibit mode connectivity; (2) Intermediate points along optimized paths achieve higher transferability than endpoints; (3) Bézier crossover dramatically outperforms discrete genetic operations while reducing convergence time and query requirements. By exploiting the geometric structure of adversarial space through path optimization, MoCo-EA provides an efficient and reliable method. Our work challenges the traditional view of adversarial examples as isolated points and opens new directions for both attack generation and defense research.

</details>


### [233] [Surviving the Unseen: Predictive Defense for Novel Multi-Turn Multimodal Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18988)
*Doohee You*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIAD框架，将多模态大模型的安全验证形式化为动态生存预测与轨迹动力学问题，通过结构异常检测、Ledoit-Wolf正则化马氏距离和拓扑轨迹加速度三大模块，结合时变Cox比例风险模型与贝叶斯隐马尔可夫模型反馈循环，实现对跨模态、多轮对话中渐进式对抗扰动的可解释、可预测防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在自主智能体工作流中的扩展引入了非平稳攻击面。对手利用渐进式、跨模态扰动，将恶意意图分布在整个对话轨迹中，绕过逐轮防护。静态防御机制受限于马尔可夫性质，孤立评估输入，无法检测累积的结构性投毒。

Method: 将安全验证形式化为动态生存预测与轨迹动力学问题；构建TRIAD框架，包含：(1) 结构异常检测监测协方差漂移；(2) Ledoit-Wolf正则化马氏距离监测高维空间协方差变化；(3) 拓扑轨迹加速度区分良性创意探索与持续恶意漂移。将这些运动学和几何特征通过贝叶斯HMM反馈循环输入时变Cox比例风险模型。

Result: 理论分析表明TRIAD框架在对抗扰动下提供了数学上有界的期望故障时间，确保恶意加速项正向发散。

Conclusion: 该框架为实时智能体AI系统提供了计算高效、可解释且可预测的安全保障，为无需经验重训练的持续安全对齐建立了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: The expansion of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and their integration into autonomous agentic workflows has introduced a non-stationary attack surface. Empirical observations indicate that adversaries employ progressive, cross-modal perturbations that evade turn-specific guardrails by distributing malicious intent across longitudinal conversational trajectories. Static defense mechanisms, constrained by the Markov property, evaluate inputs in isolation and fail to detect cumulative structural poisoning. To handle this limitation, this paper formulates safety verification as a dynamic survival prediction and trajectory dynamics problem. The Triple-tier Anomaly Defense (TRIAD) framework is proposed as a predictive model that maps multimodal and multi-turn conversational flow as a continuous trajectory. The framework integrates structural anomaly detection to monitor covariance shifts, a Ledoit-Wolf regularized Mahalanobis distance to monitor covariance shifts in high-dimensional spaces, and topological trajectory acceleration to differentiate benign creative exploration from continuous malicious drift. These kinematic and geometric features are integrated into a time-varying Cox Proportional Hazards model via a Bayesian Hidden Markov Model (HMM) feedback loop. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that the TRIAD framework provides a mathematically bounded expected time-to-failure under adversarial perturbations, ensuring that malicious acceleration diverges positively. This framework provides a computationally efficient, interpretable, and predictive safeguard for real-time agentic AI systems, establishing a rigorous foundation for continuous safety alignment without relying on empirical retraining.

</details>


### [234] [Structural Analysis of Cryptographic Sequences using Stringology-Based Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19123)
*Victor Kebande*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于字符串学（Stringology）的指纹识别框架（SBF），用于分析密码学序列的结构特征。该框架将密码学输出视为符号字符串，通过提取子串频率分布、重复模式和熵特征等结构统计量，生成表征序列生成器的指纹向量。实验表明，该方法能检测出不同序列源之间的可测量结构签名，为密码生成器的结构行为评估提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的统计随机性测试仅验证全局统计属性，无法揭示序列的结构特征是否泄露底层生成器的信息。因此，需要一种能够捕捉序列结构特征的分析方法，从新的维度评估密码学序列生成器的行为。

Method: 提出SBF框架，将密码学输出解释为符号字符串，应用基于模式的特征提取方法，捕获子串频率分布、重复模式和熵特征等结构统计数据，将这些结构特征聚合为表征序列生成器的指纹向量。

Result: 在密码生成序列（CGS）和均匀随机序列（URS）数据集上的实验表明，基于字符串学的模式分析能够揭示不同序列源之间可测量的结构签名差异。

Conclusion: 虽然检测到的结构信号并不暗示实际密码学弱点，但该方法为评估密码学生成器的结构行为提供了额外的分析视角，补充了传统统计测试的不足。

Abstract: Cryptographic primitives such as stream ciphers,Pseudorandom Number Generators (PRNGs), and block cipher modes produce sequences that are designed to be statistically indistinguishable from random data. As a result, the traditional evaluation techniques therefore rely primarily on statistical randomness tests to assess the quality of generated sequences. While these tests verify global statistical properties, they do not address whether structural characteristics of sequences can reveal information about the underlying generator. In this paper, we introduce a stringology-based fingerprinting, (SBF) framework for the structural analysis of cryptographic sequences. The proposed SBF framework interprets cryptographic outputs as symbolic strings and applies pattern-based feature extraction to capture structural statistics such as substring frequency distributions, recurrence patterns, and entropy characteristics. These structural features are aggregated into fingerprint vectors that characterize sequence generators. The experimental evaluation is conducted using datasets composed of Cipher-Generated Sequences (CGS) and Uniformly Random Sequences (URS). The results demonstrate that stringology-based pattern analysis can reveal measurable structural signatures across different sequence sources. Although these signals do not imply practical cryptographic weaknesses, they provide an additional analytical perspective for evaluating the structural behavior of cryptographic generators.

</details>


### [235] [Be Kind, Rewrite: Benign Projections via Rewriting Defend Against LLM Data Poisoning Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19147)
*John T. Halloran,Noopur S. Bhatt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出开放书良性改写（OBBR）方法，利用LLM基于良性样本对训练数据进行重写，作为对抗后门攻击的主动防御。理论证明OBBR比闭书重写更可能输出良性内容。实验表明，在5种已知后门攻击和4个LLM上，OBBR相较最先进防御平均提升51%安全性，比闭书重写提升25.7%，且计算高效、不降低自然语言任务性能，能防御非触发词数据投毒攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型极易受到后门攻击（BAs）的影响，现有防御方法在广泛测试中效果不佳，亟需更有效的防御手段。

Method: 提出开放书良性改写（OBBR）方法，利用LLM基于开放书（良性样本）对训练数据进行重写，将训练样本投影到良性提示空间，从而中和有害内容。

Result: 在5种已知后门攻击和4种广泛使用的LLM上，OBBR相比最先进BA防御平均提升51%安全性，相比闭书重写提升25.7%；计算效率高；微调后不降低自然语言任务性能；能防御非触发词数据投毒攻击。

Conclusion: OBBR是一种高效、有效的主动防御方法，能够广泛抵御多种后门攻击，且不影响模型在自然语言任务上的表现，优于现有防御方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are highly susceptible to backdoor attacks (BAs), wherein training samples are poisoned using trigger-based harmful content. Furthermore, existing defenses have proven ineffective when extensively tested across BA patterns. To better combat BAs, we explore the use of LLM rewriting as a proactive defense against data poisoning. First, we theoretically show that when LLM rewriting utilizes open-book benign samples--termed open-book benign rewriting (OBBR)--the probability of a rewritten output being benign is strictly greater than that of closed-book rewriting. Thus, OBBR neutralizes harmful content by projecting training samples to the space of benign prompts. We then show that, in contrast to previous defenses, OBBR effectively mitigates a large number of existing BAs: across five known BAs and four widely used LLMs, OBBR increases safety performance by an average 51% compared to state-of-the-art BA defenses and 25.7% compared to closed-book rewriting methods. Finally, we show that OBBR is computationally efficient relative to other BA defenses, does not degrade model performance on natural language tasks after fine-tuning, and is capable of defending against non-trigger based data poisoning attacks.

</details>


### [236] [On the Geometric Limits of Transformer Defenses against Obfuscation Attacks: Latent Embedding Collapse & Performance Robustness Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19159)
*Becky Mashaido,Tapadhir Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 现有提示注入防御在分类性能上表现优异，但存在潜在嵌入坍塌问题：多算子混淆提示的嵌入向量与干净提示近乎重叠（最小边界差δ=1.02），且混淆嵌入的类内方差显著偏高（3.33±6.23），揭示了性能-鲁棒性差距，表明标准评估指标无法捕捉潜在几何脆弱性，增加模型容量也无法消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前提示注入防御评估依赖于分类性能指标，忽略了潜在的表示鲁棒性问题，即高检测性能并不等同于嵌入空间的几何鲁棒性。

Method: 使用多算子混淆提示（结合同形字、零宽字符、标点符号和表情符号噪声）攻击BERT家族编码器，通过计算干净嵌入与混淆嵌入之间的最小边界差δ以及类内方差，量化潜在嵌入坍塌程度。

Result: 检测器达到近乎完美的分类性能，但干净-混淆嵌入的最小边界差δ=1.02，表明嵌入近乎重叠；混淆嵌入类内方差高达3.33±6.23，表明潜在空间严重不稳定。增加模型容量无法消除嵌入坍塌。

Conclusion: 标准性能指标无法反映潜在嵌入坍塌和几何脆弱性，亟需引入几何感知的鲁棒性分析作为性能评估的必要补充。

Abstract: Prompt injection attacks pose significant risks to language model safety, yet existing defenses are typically evaluated using classification performance. We show that high detection performance does not imply representational robustness. Specifically, multi-operator obfuscated prompts (combining homoglyphs, zero-width characters, and punctuation or emoji noise) can partially collapse onto the embedding manifold of clean prompts, a phenomenon we term latent embedding collapse. Results indicate that across multiple BERT family encoders with varying depth and capacity, detectors achieve near-perfect classification performance, yet the minimal clean-obfuscated margin delta = 1.02, indicating near-overlap of obfuscated and clean embeddings. Obfuscated embeddings further exhibit elevated intra-class variance (3.33 +/- 6.23), indicating severe latent-space instability despite high performance. These results reveal a substantial perf ormance-robustness gap, demonstrating that standard evaluation metrics fail to capture latent embedding collapse and underlying geometric fragility. Our findings show that increasing model capacity does not eliminate latent embedding collapse, motivating geometry-aware robustness analysis as a necessary complement to performance-based evaluation for prompt-injection defenses.

</details>


### [237] [Token by Token, Compromised: Backdoor Vulnerabilities in Unified Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19227)
*Tobias Braun,Jonas Henry Grebe,Hossein Shakibania,Anna Rohrbach,Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对统一自回归模型(UAMs)的多模态后门攻击方法ToBAC，通过数据投毒或模型操控，使普通词语触发有害的视觉与文本联合输出，攻击成功率高达63.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一自回归模型(UAMs)共享参数和多模态词汇表，这种统一架构可能引入新的安全漏洞，但目前尚无研究探索其后门攻击风险。

Method: 提出Token by Token后门攻击(ToBAC)，包括基于数据和基于模型的两种投毒策略，利用无害字符或常见词语作为触发器，在自回归图像生成中诱发恶意行为。

Result: 在可访问模型的情况下，对Liquid模型攻击中触发器"cool"可在55%的生成中诱导品牌推广或意识形态影响；在无模型访问的数据投毒场景下，对JanusPro的平均攻击成功率达63.1%。

Conclusion: UAMs的统一架构使多模态后门攻击成为可能，触发器能在多个输出模态间传播恶意效果，联合操控视觉与文本输出以增强伪造内容的可信度。

Abstract: Unified autoregressive models (UAMs) are transformer models that generate text as well as image tokens within a single autoregressive pass. Shared parameters and a multimodal vocabulary simplify the training pipeline and facilitate flexible multimodal generation, yet might introduce new vulnerabilities. In particular, we are the first to show that this unified architecture enables multimodal backdoor attacks, where a trigger can propagate malicious effects across multiple output modalities. Specifically, we present the Token by Token Backdoor Attack (ToBAC), the first backdoor attack targeting UAMs, exploring both data-based and model-based poisoning strategies. We demonstrate that innocuous characters or even common words can be transformed into triggers that elicit harmful behavior in autoregressive image generation. ToBAC can jointly manipulate visual outputs and accompanying text, increasing the perceived authenticity of fabricated content. With model access, ToBAC enables attacks on the unified Liquid model in which a subtle word (e.g., ``cool'') induces modality-aligned brand promotion or ideological influence in 55% of generations. Without model access, ToBAC can be induced through data poisoning, achieving an average success rate of 63.1% against JanusPro.

</details>


### [238] [Devilray: A Systematic Adversarial Model Revealing Blind Spots in Fake Base Station Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19232)
*Taekkyung Oh,Duckwoo Kim,Hansung Bae,Beomseok Oh,CheolJun Park,Tyler Tucker,Nathaniel Bennett,Sangwook Bae,Byeongdo Hong,Patrick Traynor,Yongdae Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Devilray是一个可重构的参考级对抗基线系统，通过对商业伪基站（C-FBS）的首次学术分析建立了经验基准，并基于3GPP规范扩展出2592个可行且真实的FBS实例，系统评估了7种现有检测器，揭示了所有检测器均存在由假设驱动的设计与评估造成的对抗盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去二十多年伪基站检测研究严重依赖自建原型设备，由于商业伪基站（C-FBS）的获取存在经济和监管壁垒，研究者无法直接观察真实世界的攻击操作，导致检测系统设计与评估脱离实际威胁模型。

Method: 首先对商业伪基站进行首次学术分析以建立经验基准，然后结合3GPP规范分析推导出规范允许的操作变体，最后构建了可重构的Devilray系统，系统生成了2592个可行且真实的FBS实例用于评估现有检测器。

Result: 对7种代表性伪基站检测器进行评估，发现所有7种检测器均存在覆盖缺口，其对抗盲区源于基于假设的设计与评估方法，未能覆盖真实世界中可行的对抗行为。

Conclusion: Devilray提供了首个基于真实行为与规范分析的鲁棒对抗模型，能够帮助社区以更严格的方式开发和评估未来的伪基站检测机制。

Abstract: Fake Base Station (FBS) detection has been a critical focus of cellular security research for over two decades. However, significant financial and regulatory barriers to accessing commercial FBS (C-FBS) devices have limited direct visibility into real-world operations, forcing detection systems to be designed and evaluated around self-built prototypes. In this paper, we present Devilray, a reconfigurable and reference-grade adversarial baseline designed to systematically explore the realistic adversarial space and identify adversarial blind spots in current detection -- regions of realistic adversarial behavior excluded by prevailing threat models. We establish an empirical ground truth through the first academic analysis of a C-FBS and extend these observations into specification-driven operational variants permitted by 3GPP standards. Devilray enables the systematic exploration of 2,592 feasible and realistic FBS instances, capturing a wide range of operational possibilities. Using Devilray, we evaluate seven representative accessible FBS detectors and uncover coverage gaps across all seven, revealing blind spots rooted in assumption-bound design and evaluation. Our work provides the first robust adversarial model grounded in real-world behavior and specification analysis, enabling the community to develop and evaluate future detection mechanisms in a rigorous manner.

</details>


### [239] [MultiBallot: Verifiable and privacy-preserving E-Collecting in the Swiss setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19312)
*Florian Moser,Léo Louistisserand*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于瑞士电子投票系统，提出了一个适用于电子签名收集的安全协议，在现实信任假设下同时实现了隐私保护和可验证性，并利用并行采集活动的特性在不依赖匿名信道的情况下保障参与隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公民通过签名收集参与政治进程，在瑞士这类行为甚至具有法律约束力。现有系统缺乏同时满足隐私与可验证性的安全协议，亟需设计适用于电子签名收集的实用方案。

Method: 首先从瑞士电子投票系统出发推导出电子签名收集的现实设定，然后在该设定下设计安全协议，利用多个采集活动并行的特性来实现参与隐私保护。

Result: 提出了一个在现实信任假设下同时实现隐私保护和可验证性的安全协议，且参与隐私无需依赖匿名信道。

Conclusion: 本文为瑞士电子签名收集提供了可行的安全协议方案，在保障隐私与可验证性的同时降低了假设强度，具有现实可用性。

Abstract: As part of the political process, citizens may participate in signature collections to influence policy changes. In Switzerland, this even results in legally binding acts, similar to an election system. In this work, we first derive a realistic setting for e-collecting in Switzerland, based on the setting established for e-voting. Then, we propose a secure protocol in this setting, achieving both privacy and verifiability under realistic trust assumptions. Notably, participation privacy is guaranteed without assuming an anonymous channel, by considering the fact that at any given point in time, many collections are active in parallel.

</details>


### [240] [Exploring and Developing a Pre-Model Safeguard with Draft Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19321)
*Hongyu Cai,Arjun Arunasalam,Yiming Liang,Antonio Bianchi,Z. Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用越狱攻击可迁移性的安全防护设计，在目标模型推理前通过SLM草稿响应检测提示安全性，降低预模型守卫的假阴性率，作为后模型守卫的低成本替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预模型守卫仅依赖提示词检测，对越狱攻击假阴性率高；后模型守卫虽更准确但需在目标模型推理后执行，计算开销大。需要一种兼顾检测准确性与计算效率的防护方法。

Method: 首先系统研究越狱攻击从LLM到SLM的可迁移性，识别关键影响因素。基于此观察，利用推测推理机制：先用小型草稿模型生成一组草稿响应，再将原始提示与这些草稿一同输入现有安全守卫进行安全预测。

Result: 该方法降低了预模型守卫的假阴性率，同时相比后模型守卫提供了低计算开销（更少的token使用量和处理时间）的替代方案。

Conclusion: 利用越狱攻击从大模型到小模型的可迁移性，通过SLM草稿响应在目标模型推理前实现高效准确的安全检测，在计算成本与检测效果之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) alignment remains vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that elicit unsafe responses, motivating pre-model and post-model guards. Pre-model guards audit the safety of prompts before invoking target models. However, relying solely on the prompt often leads to high false-negative rates (i.e., jailbreak attacks go undetected). Post-model guards address this issue by auditing both the user prompt and the target model's response. However, they incur a high computational cost, including increased token usage and processing time, because they operate after target model inference.
  In this paper, we introduce a safeguard design that leverages the transferability of jailbreak attacks to enforce prompt safety before target model inference. We first conduct a systematic study of jailbreak transferability, particularly from LLMs to small language models (SLMs). Through these experiments, we identify key factors influencing transferability. Building on these insights, we observe that responses from smaller draft models reflect the safety implications of those from large target models; \ie given a jailbreak prompt constructed for an LLM, an SLM is likely to be triggered to generate an unaligned response. Based on this observation, our safeguard design leverages speculative inference with SLMs to generate a set of draft responses. It then feeds the original prompt and these drafts into existing guards to predict their safety. We demonstrate that this design reduces the false-negative rate of pre-model guards and offers a low \Efficiency alternative to post-model guards. \textcolor{red}{\bf Notice: This paper contains examples of harmful language.}

</details>


### [241] [RoboJailBench: Benchmarking Adversarial Attacks and Defenses in Embodied Robotic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19328)
*Doguhuan Yeke,Yanming Zhou,Leo Y. Lin,Hongyu Cai,Antonio Bianchi,Z. Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboJailBench，首个标准化评估具身AI中越狱攻击的基准框架，包含基于ISO标准构建的18类安全违规分类法、意图对比数据集流水线（同时衡量安全性与实用性），以及统一的评估流程，填补了现有基准在对抗性风险、输入、后果和评估标准方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI越狱攻击评估依赖临时数据集、有限指标，仅关注攻击成功率而忽略安全性与遵循良性指令能力之间的权衡；现有基准要么针对传统聊天模型，要么聚焦于非对抗性安全评估，均无法捕捉具身AI系统越狱攻击所需的对抗性风险、输入、后果和评估标准。

Method: 1) 基于ISO标准、监管规则和记录事件构建安全分类法，衍生出18类具身AI安全违规后果；2) 提出意图对比数据集流水线，用配对的对抗性目标和良性目标增强现有数据集；3) 提供包含标准化指标和统一流程的进化式仓库，用于评估和集成新攻击与防御。

Result: 构建了新的分类平衡数据集，增强了5个现有数据集；集成了4种攻击和2种防御，在领先的具身VLM上评估了其性能；发布了首个标准化评估框架及代码、数据集和排行榜。

Conclusion: RoboJailBench为具身AI越狱攻击提供了首个标准化评估框架，支持未来研究，项目已开源并维护排行榜。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) facilitate a new class of embodied AI systems, where these models are integrated into physical platforms, e.g. robots and autonomous vehicles, to interpret visual scenes and execute natural language commands in diverse environments. Previous research has introduced jailbreak attacks and defenses for embodied AI. Their evaluations, however, rely on ad-hoc datasets, limited metrics, and emphasize attack success while neglecting the trade-off between security and the ability to follow benign commands. Existing benchmarks and evaluation frameworks either target traditional chat-based models or focus on non-adversarial safety evaluation for embodied AI; neither captures the adversarial risks, inputs, consequences, and evaluation criteria necessary for jailbreak attacks in embodied AI systems. In this paper, we address this gap with RoboJailBench, which consists of three core components. We establish a security taxonomy derived from ISO standards, regulatory rules, and documented incidents. This effort yields 18 categories of security violation consequences for embodied AI. We introduce an intent contrast dataset pipeline that augments existing datasets with paired adversarial and benign goals to measure both security and utility. Lastly, we provide an evolving repository with standardized metrics and a unified process for assessing and integrating new attacks and defenses. With this benchmark, we construct a new taxonomy-balanced dataset and augment five existing datasets. We integrate four attacks and two defenses to evaluate their performance on leading embodied VLMs. This benchmark provides the first standardized evaluation framework for jailbreak attacks in embodied AI and supports future research. We release our code, datasets, and artifacts, and maintain a leaderboard at https://purseclab.github.io/benchmark-for-robotics-security.

</details>


### [242] [High-Rate Public-Key Pseudorandom Codes for Edit Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19402)
*Shengtang Huang,Xin Li,Songtao Mao,Zhaienhe Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了对抗编辑错误的公钥伪随机码（PRC）。通过将汉明鲁棒的零比特PRC转化为编辑鲁棒的零比特PRC，在次线性编辑错误率下实现了二进制PRC。在高速率场景下，构建了接近速率1（大字母表）和接近速率1/2（二进制字母表）的公钥PRC，并在字母表大小为poly(λ)时达到插入-删除信道的Singleton界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编辑错误场景下的伪随机码研究尚不充分，特别是在高速率和小字母表情况下。为弥补这一空白，推动PRC在水印等应用中的实际部署。

Method: 提出从汉明鲁棒PRC到编辑鲁棒PRC的新归约方法；通过构造特定方案实现高速率公钥PRC，分别针对大字母表和二进制字母表设计，并利用poly(λ)大小字母表达到Singleton界。

Result: 1）在次线性编辑错误率下获得二进制零比特PRC；2）大字母表下速率任意接近1；3）二进制字母表下速率任意接近1/2；4）poly(λ)字母表下达到插入-删除信道的Singleton界。

Conclusion: 这些结果首次在相同假设下获得了对抗编辑错误的高速率公钥二进制PRC构造，填补了该领域的重要空白。

Abstract: Pseudorandom codes (PRCs), introduced by Christ and Gunn (CRYPTO '2024), are error-correcting codes whose codewords are computationally indistinguishable from uniformly random strings, while still being decodable by someone holding the key. They provide a natural primitive for robust and undetectable watermarking, particularly in applications to AI-generated content. Although recent works have obtained strong results for substitution errors, the edit-error setting remains much less understood, especially in the high-rate regime and over small alphabets.
  We study public-key pseudorandom codes against edit errors. First, we give a new reduction showing that binary zero-bit PRCs robust against a constant fraction of substitution errors can be transformed into binary zero-bit PRCs robust against edit errors. Consequently, under any assumption that yields zero-bit Hamming-robust PRCs, one also obtains zero-bit PRCs for edit channels, albeit only for the weaker class of sublinear polynomial edit channels, namely channels with edit error rate $1/n^γ$ for any constant $γ>0$.
  In the high-rate regime, we construct public-key PRCs with rate arbitrarily close to $1$ over sufficiently large constant alphabets, and with rate arbitrarily close to $1/2$ over the binary alphabet. Moreover, if we allow the alphabet size to be $\mathrm{poly}(λ)$, where $λ$ is the security parameter, then our public-key PRCs can attain the Singleton bound for insertion-deletion channels. Taken together, these results yield the first high-rate public-key binary PRC constructions for edit channels, under the same assumption that yields zero-bit Hamming-robust PRCs.

</details>


### [243] [Exposing Functional Fusion: A New Class of Strategic Backdoor in Dynamic Prompt Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19478)
*Zeyao Liu,Zhendong Zhao,Xiaojun Chen,Xin Zhao,Yuexin Xuan,Xiaoshuang Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了动态提示调整（VPT）架构中一种新型后门攻击威胁，提出VIPER攻击框架，利用视觉提示生成器（VPG）实现恶意逻辑与良性任务效用的功能融合，形成" hostage"困境——剪除攻击必然破坏良性性能。VIPER在保持SOTA干净数据性能的同时，即使VPG模块剪枝90%仍维持近100%攻击成功率，仅增加0.06ms推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ViT后门攻击基于全参数微调，计算成本高且导致性能下降；而基于适配器（如LoRA）的安全已有初步研究，但快速发展的提示调整生态系统的安全风险尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，揭示动态提示架构中存在的更危险的威胁。

Method: 提出VIPER攻击框架，核心是轻量级动态视觉提示生成器（VPG）。该动态架构实现了"功能融合"——将恶意逻辑与良性任务效用紧密融合到同一稀疏、高幅值参数核心中。

Result: VIPER在干净数据上达到SOTA性能；即使在VPG模块90%剪枝下仍保持近100%攻击成功率（而LoRA攻击在此条件下崩溃）；仅增加0.06ms（1.16%）的推理延迟。

Conclusion: 动态提示架构存在范式级的安全风险，功能融合机制使得攻击难以通过模型剪枝等常规防御手段消除，对提示调整生态系统的安全性提出严峻挑战。

Abstract: Existing ViT backdoor attacks based on backbone-overwriting full-tuning are computationally expensive and inflict performance degradation. This has forced adversaries towards the Visual Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) paradigm, dominated by adapter-based (e.g., LoRA) and prompt-based (e.g., VPT) approaches. While adapter security has seen initial study, the risks of the burgeoning prompt-based ecosystem remain critically unexplored. We fill this critical gap, exposing how the evolution of VPT towards dynamic and context-aware architectures can facilitate a far more dangerous and emergent threat. This vulnerability arises even though these dynamic modules unlock superior benign performance. We propose VIPER, an attack framework built on a lightweight, dynamic Visual Prompt Generator (VPG) that demonstrates this vulnerability. Critically, this dynamic architecture enables Functional Fusion: an emergent phenomenon where malicious logic and benign task utility are tightly fused into the same sparse, high-magnitude parameter core. This fusion creates a formidable ``hostage" dilemma, as pruning the attack necessarily destroys the benign performance. Comprehensive evaluations show VIPER effectively addresses the attacker's trilemma: VIPER not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on clean data, but also maintains near-100% ASR even under 90% VPG-module pruning (where LoRA attacks collapse), while adding only an imperceptible 0.06ms (1.16%) of inference latency. VIPER's results, driven by Functional Fusion, expose a new, paradigm-level risk in dynamic prompt architectures.

</details>


### [244] [Inferring Sensitive Attributes from Knowledge Graph Embeddings: Attack and Defense Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19644)
*Yasmine Hayder*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究知识图谱嵌入（KGE）推理过程中的隐私风险，特别是属性推断攻击，并提出了通过后处理消毒技术来缓解这些风险，初步结果展示了攻击有效性及推荐质量与隐私保护之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的知识图谱通常不完整，KGE技术常被用于推理缺失信息，但推理过程可能无意中泄露敏感用户属性，即使这些数据并未显式存储。现有研究对KGE推理中的隐私风险关注不足。

Method: 提出一个框架，通过对KGE输出应用后处理消毒技术（post processing sanitization）来缓解属性推断攻击；采用基于随机化的方法，在推荐质量与隐私保护之间进行权衡。

Result: 初步结果显示属性推断攻击对KGE模型输出有效；随机化方法在推荐质量与隐私保护之间存在权衡，表明需要更先进的技术。

Conclusion: KGE推理存在隐私泄露风险，后处理消毒技术可以缓解但尚不完善，未来需要探索更高级的隐私保护技术以更好地平衡推荐质量与隐私保护。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are a powerful representation of linked data, offering flexibility, semantic richness, and support for knowledge enrichment and reasoning. They help data owners organize and exploit heterogeneous data to provide insightful services (e.g., recommendations), yet real-world KGs are often incomplete, hiding true facts or missing valuable insights. Knowledge graph embedding techniques are commonly used to infer valuable missing information. However, reasoning over KGs can inadvertently expose sensitive user information, even when such data is not explicitly stored. In this work, we investigate the privacy risks associated with KGE-based reasoning, focusing on attribute inference attacks where adversaries attempt to deduce sensitive user attributes from seemingly non-sensitive outputs. We propose and evaluate a framework that mitigates these privacy risks by applying post processing sanitization techniques to KGE outputs. Preliminary results demonstrate the effectiveness of these attacks on the outputs of KGE models, and explore the trade-off between recommendation quality and privacy protection when applying randomization based approaches, highlighting the need to experiment with more advanced techniques in future work to address this issue.

</details>


### [245] [Auditing Privacy in Multi-Tenant RAG under Account Collusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19847)
*Florian A. D. Burnat,Brittany I. Davidson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多租户RAG系统的审计协议，揭示了同索引多账户共谋导致隐私边界失效的问题，并设计了首个无需修改部署即可进行定量隐私审计的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统宣称提供按账户级别的差分隐私保障，但忽略了同租户下多账户共谋攻击同一索引时，隐私边界会按Θ(√k·ε_acc)速率退化，存在隐私边界失效问题。

Method: 1. 识别同索引多账户共谋这一隐私边界失效模式，利用DP组合理论证明高斯噪声检索下隐私退化速率；2. 设计审计协议，组合通用密码学原语（Merkle账本、ZK函数应用证明、高斯噪声证明）与6种RAG专用原语；3. 支持闭式审计边界和Rényi-DP矩会计跟踪。

Result: 实现了符合理论速率的攻击，并推导了RAG特定的成员推断攻击预测，通过实验验证；审计协议可对检索得分通道输出定量(PASS, ε_audit) verdict。

Conclusion: 多租户RAG的按账户DP保障存在共谋导致的退化漏洞，所提审计协议在不暴露索引、不重构流水线、不暴露模型权重的前提下实现了可审计性，生成通道的隐私需与本文协议组合审计。

Abstract: Multi-tenant retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) services advertise per-account differential privacy as the operative leakage boundary: each account's queries are guaranteed to satisfy $(\varepsilon_{\text{acc}}, δ_{\text{acc}})$-DP with respect to the index. We identify same-index multi-account collusion as a privacy-boundary failure: for $k$ same-tenant accounts coordinating against the tenant's index -- the operative regime -- known DP composition theory implies joint leakage degrades unconditionally at rate $Θ(\sqrt{k} \cdot \varepsilon_{\text{acc}})$ for Gaussian-noised retrieval. Cross-tenant and external collusion match the rate only under explicit access-control failure (M4); without M4 these regimes have zero leakage by design and reduce to an architectural audit, not a DP audit. We exhibit an attack realizing the rate and derive a RAG-specific MIA prediction we test empirically. To make this per-account/joint gap auditable, we design the first audit protocol that operates against unmodified RAG deployments and issues a quantitative $(\textsf{PASS}, \varepsilon_{\text{audit}})$ verdict for the retrieval-score channel -- the noise-then-select step the per-account DP guarantee actually covers -- without index disclosure, pipeline redesign, or model-weight exposure. Generation-channel privacy (LLM output conditioned on selected documents) is a separate audit predicate that should compose with ours; we explicitly scope it out. The protocol composes generic cryptographic primitives (Merkle ledgers, ZK function-application proofs, Gaussian noise attestations) with six RAG-specific primitives (embedder commitment, index-content vector commitment, per-account query ledger, noise-then-select attestation, cross-tenant containment proof, coalition-size estimator) and supports both closed-form audit bounds and Rényi-DP moments-accountant tracking.

</details>


### [246] [Taking Cryptography Out of the Data Path via Near-Memory Processing in DRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20047)
*Nicola Barcarolo,Brahmaiah Gandham,Mohammad Sadrosadati,Roberto Passerone,Onur Mutlu,Flavio Vella*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了真实世界中的处理单元内存（PIM）架构在加速AES-128和SHA-256等加密算法方面的潜力与局限性，使用UPMEM PIM系统进行实验，发现单rank性能低于现代CPU，但多rank并行可显著提升性能，超过CPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CPU/GPU架构由于内存瓶颈，在处理大规模加密数据时存在高延迟和高能耗问题，而现有PIM研究缺乏在真实系统上的全面评估，因此需要探索真实PIM系统在加密算法加速中的实际潜力与限制。

Method: 采用UPMEM PIM架构作为实验平台，将AES-128和SHA-256加密算法部署在PIM系统上，通过单rank与多rank扩展的方式测试计算性能，并与现代CPU进行对比。

Result: 单rank运行时PIM性能低于现代CPU；当利用所有可用rank进行分布式计算时，真实PIM能够更有效地加速加密算法，性能超越CPU。

Conclusion: 真实世界PIM架构在充分扩展（多rank并行）时能够有效加速加密算法，克服传统处理器的内存瓶颈，具有在实际系统中应用的潜力。

Abstract: Cryptographic algorithms such as AES-128 and SHA-256 are fundamental to ensuring data security and integrity. Although these algorithms are computationally efficient, their performance is often constrained by the processor-centric architectures (e.g., CPUs, GPUs), primarily due to the memory bottleneck. This constraint leads to increased latency and higher energy consumption, particularly when handling large volumes of data. To overcome these challenges, Processing-in-Memory (PIM) has emerged as a promising architectural paradigm, allowing computation to occur directly within or near memory units. By minimizing data movement between the processor and memory units, PIM can significantly accelerate cryptographic algorithms while improving energy efficiency. Several pieces of prior work have demonstrated the effectiveness of PIM at fundamentally accelerating cryptographic algorithms. However, none of the prior works have extensively demonstrated the potential of a real-world PIM system. In this paper, we want to investigate the potential and limitations of real-world PIM in accelerating cryptographic algorithms. As part of our methodology, the UPMEM PIM architecture is used to assess the scalability of cryptographic algorithms. When these algorithms operate on a single rank, their performance remains below that of modern CPUs. However, distributing the computation across multiple ranks significantly enhances performance. When all available ranks are utilized, real-world PIM can accelerate cryptographic algorithms more effectively.

</details>


### [247] [Hunting Vulnerability Variants in AI Infra: Measurement and Reference-Driven Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20051)
*Tian Dong,Yanjun Chen,Shoufeng Zhang,Huaien Zhang,Yunlong Lyu,Keke Lian,Dong Zhang,Shaofeng Li,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对AI基础设施中的漏洞变体进行了测量研究，分析了688个GitHub仓库和251个公开漏洞，发现AI基础设施项目之间存在大量功能重叠和可复现的脆弱模式。基于此，提出了INFRASCOPE框架，该框架从已知漏洞中提取可迁移的语义，用于在新仓库中定位和验证漏洞变体。在20个真实AI仓库中发现了20多个漏洞，其中11个已被确认，4个已分配CVE编号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基础设施已成为模型训练、部署和智能体编排的共享执行层，但不同项目间存在大量相似的模型中心化工作流，导致一个仓库中披露的漏洞可能以变体形式出现在其他设计相似的仓库中。然而，这些变体的普遍性和可检测性尚不明确。

Method: 1) 对688个GitHub仓库和251个公开漏洞进行测量分析，研究AI基础设施中漏洞变体的普遍性；2) 提出INFRASCOPE，一个参考驱动的多智能体框架，从已知漏洞中提取可迁移的漏洞语义，并用其在新仓库中定位和验证变体。

Result: 在20个真实AI基础设施仓库中发现了20多个漏洞，其中11个已被确认，4个已分配CVE编号。

Conclusion: AI基础设施项目确实存在跨仓库的漏洞变体问题，且通过自动化的参考驱动框架可以有效识别这些变体，证明了该方法在提升AI基础设施安全方面的实际价值。

Abstract: AI infra has become a shared execution layer for model training, deployment, and agent orchestration. Because many projects reimplement similar model-centric workflows, a vulnerability disclosed in one repository can recur as a variant in another repository with a related design. Yet the prevalence and detectability of these variants remain poorly understood. This paper presents a measurement study of vulnerability variants in AI infra. Analyzing 688 GitHub repositories and 251 publicly disclosed vulnerabilities, we find that AI infra projects frequently share overlapping functionality and recurrent vulnerable patterns, creating a concrete basis for cross-repository variants. Building on this finding, we study how to automatically identify such variants from known disclosures. We propose INFRASCOPE, a reference-driven multi-agent framework that extracts transferable vulnerability semantics from known cases and uses them to locate and validate variants in new repositories. Evaluating INFRASCOPE on 20 real-world AI infra repositories, we uncover over 20 vulnerabilities, including 11 acknowledged cases and 4 cases that have been assigned CVEs so far.

</details>


### [248] [BiRD: A Bidirectional Ranking Defense Mechanism for Retrieval Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20123)
*Chengcai Gao,Zhihong Sun,Xiaochuan Shi,Qiufeng Wang,Chao Liang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BiRD，一种利用双向排序（前向排序评估语义相关性，后向排序量化排序上下文一致性）来防御RAG系统中毒攻击的轻量级方法，在3个数据集、3个检索器和3个LLM上验证了有效性，可将攻击成功率降低54%同时提升任务准确率56%，额外延迟低于1秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG防御方法（基于语义分析或投票）存在高计算成本与有限鲁棒性之间的权衡，其根本局限在于只关注语义内容相关性而忽略了由排序结构定义的检索上下文。

Method: BiRD建立在双信号框架上：前向排序评估语义内容相关性，后向排序量化排序上下文一致性。通过分析中毒文档和良性文档的双向排序行为，发现中毒文档的后向排序与查询的前向排序之间具有显著更强的对齐性，利用这一判别性模式进行防御。

Result: 在3个数据集、3个检索器和3个LLM上进行评估，在2种攻击场景下，BiRD将PoisonedRAG的攻击成功率降低最高54%，同时将任务准确率提升最高56%，平均额外延迟低于1秒。

Conclusion: BiRD通过引入双向排序机制，同时兼顾了语义内容和排序上下文，打破了现有方法在效率和鲁棒性之间的权衡，是一种高效且鲁棒的RAG防御方案。

Abstract: The growing adoption of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has led to a rise in adversarial attacks. Existing defenses, relying on semantic analysis or voting, face a trade-off between high computational cost and limited robustness under strong poisoning attacks. Their fundamental limitation is the exclusive focus on semantic content relevance, while neglecting the retrieval context that is critically defined by ranking structures. To this end, we investigate the bidirectional ranking behavior of poisoned and benign documents, and discover a key discriminative pattern: poisoned documents exhibit significantly stronger alignment between their backward rankings and the query's forward ranking. Capitalizing on this, we propose BiRD, a bidirectional ranking defense mechanism built upon a dual-signal framework that leverages forward ranking to assess semantic content relevance and backward ranking to quantify ranking context consistency. This design directly addresses the fundamental limitation of prior approaches, enabling simultaneous efficiency and robustness. Extensive evaluation across 3 datasets with 3 retrievers and 3 LLMs under 2 attack scenarios validates BiRD's effectiveness. Notably, BiRD reduces the attack success rate of PoisonedRAG by up to 54% while simultaneously improving task accuracy by up to 56%, with average additional latency under 1 second.

</details>
